With `BulgeSizeOn 3` (R1 onward) the remnant radius reaches `MergerBulgeRadius` on the major and spheroid-dominated minor branches, by one narrowing of the double into the float, and is discarded on the disk-dominated minor branch (`M:299-330`); the `merge_s3_*` fixtures record it, with the remnant's locals and branch flags, as narrowing-limited values ("The c07 Group"): checked through SAGE26's own `deal_with_galaxy_merger`, compared bit for bit by the package's test, never claimed as validated two-sided.

### Driver Transcriptions

`evolve_galaxies` and `join_galaxies_of_progenitors` are static in `core_build_model.c`, which the oracle's link excludes, so `merger_pass`, `join_progenitors` and `join_and_pass` are **source-reviewed transcriptions**, every statement cited to its line, and their fixtures are named `tr_*`. Where they call a SAGE26 routine (`disrupt_satellite_to_ICS`, `deal_with_galaxy_merger`, the halo helpers, `estimate_merging_time`, `get_disk_radius`, `get_bulge_radius`) the continuation is SAGE26's own; that shows nothing about the transcription itself. A mutation sweep covers them (this slice's validation record).

- `merger_pass` (`B:519-587`): for each galaxy in order, a Type 1 or 2 with `mergeType` 0 has its clock decremented by `deltaT / effective_steps` (`B:529-530`, float store); the interpolated `Mvir - deltaMvir * (1 - (step + 1) / N)` without a floor (`B:534`, a fused multiply-subtract in the reference objects) and the float `StellarMass + ColdGas` (`B:535`) gate it (`B:537`); the target is the FoF central for a Type 1 and the galaxy's own `CentralGal` for a Type 2, redirected once through the target's `CentralGal` if the target has merged (`B:539-543`); `mergeIntoID` is `call.numgals` plus the target (`B:545`); a finite clock (`B:547`) leads to `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS` while it is positive and otherwise to `deal_with_galaxy_merger` with the step bin and `dt` (`B:574-582`), at the event time `Age - (step + 0.5) * (deltaT / N)` (`B:549`, fused). Joining sets every galaxy's `CentralGal` to its subhalo's Type 0 or 1 galaxy, so a Type 1's `CentralGal` is itself and the one-hop redirect of an orphan whose Type 1 has just merged lands on that merged galaxy: SAGE26's quirk, kept (`tr_pass_redirect_consumed_target`). The diagnostic log (`B:550-572`) is not transcribed. The oracle refuses a galaxy whose clock fails `MergTime < 999.0` (`B:524-527`, where SAGE26 returns `EXIT_FAILURE`) and a layout whose `call.centralgal` is not `galaxies[0].CentralGal` or not Type 0 (`B:393-400`). `intermediate.pass.galaxy[<i>]` records, on every path, `considered` and `action` (0 none, 1 merger, 4 disruption), and `deltaT`, `currentMvir`, `galaxyBaryons`, `gate_zero_baryons`, `gate_passed`, `merger_centralgal`, `redirected`, `clock_finite`, `event_time`, `step_bin` and `dt`, each with an `_absent` marker (1, and no value, where the path does not evaluate it: everything for a galaxy the pass does not consider, everything after the gate when it fails, `event_time` for a non-finite clock, `step_bin` and `dt` for a disruption).
- `join_progenitors` (`B:210-345`, `B:358-372`): the progenitor galaxies of one descendant (sub)halo `call.halonr`, `call.ngal` of them (`galaxy[<k>]` in the input, as `halogal` holds them, in the order `B:213-214` visits them), each from progenitor halo `call.galaxy[<k>].prog`, with `call.first_occupied` (the choice of `B:181-208`, tree bookkeeping, an input) and `ngalstart` 0. As in SAGE26, the progenitor index and the `galaxies[]` slot are separate: each progenitor is copied into slot `ngal` (`B:234`) and `ngal` advances at `B:341`. A Type 0 or 1 with `mergeType` not 0 becomes Type 3 and the `continue` at `B:244` skips `B:341`, so its slot is overwritten by the next progenitor, or left past the returned count if it is the last, and it never enters the central search and assignment (`B:358-370`) or the returned count (`B:372`). `B:242` is unreachable in SAGE26's own build: joining copies only `halogal` records (`B:234`), `evolve_galaxies` stores a galaxy there only if its `mergeType` is 0 (`B:651`), and the one later write of a stored record's `mergeType` (`B:646`) goes to the record the galaxy's own descendant join has already copied (each progenitor halo is joined once, into its one descendant, before that FoF group is evolved, `B:139-152`); its fixtures are probes. On the main branch the galaxy takes the halo's `Len`, `Vmax` and virial mass, `deltaMvir` in float, a new `Rvir` and `Vvir` only if the halo grew (`B:267`), and its histories, `Cooling`, `Heating`, `QuasarModeBHaccretionMass` and `OutflowRate` reset (`B:273-282`); a FoF central gets `MergTime` 999.9, then, under `ConcentrationOn > 0`, the float store of SAGE26's own `get_halo_concentration(ngal, ZZ[SnapNum], galaxies, &params)` (`B:290-295`, at its source position: before `B:300`'s `Type = 0`, so the dispatch sees the Type before joining), then a new disk radius and zero bulge radii (`B:284-300`); a subhalo galaxy that was Type 0 records its infall (`B:306-313`, `TimeOfInfall` the descendant's snapshot as a float) and its clock is estimated if it was Type 0 or its clock is above 999 (`B:315-318`); off the main branch the galaxy becomes a Type 2 orphan with `Mvir` 0 and `deltaMvir` minus its old `Mvir`, keeping its histories, and if it was Type 0 or its clock was above 999 it gets clock 0 and the infall values but no `TimeOfInfall` (`B:322-337`). Not transcribed: `MostBoundID`, `Pos` and `Vel` (`B:254-259`), the array growth (`B:215-227`) and the creation of a new galaxy when no progenitor is retained (`B:347-351`), which the oracle refuses, as it refuses a join that leaves two Type 0 or 1 galaxies (`B:361-362`). The output records `return.ngal` and every member of each retained galaxy (`galaxy[0]` to `galaxy[ngal - 1]`); `intermediate.join.progenitor[<k>]` records, for every progenitor, `branch` (0 untouched, 1 already merged and dropped, 2 central, 3 subhalo satellite, 4 orphan), `slot`, `retained`, and `previousMvir`, `previousVvir`, `previousVmax` (every branch but 0 and 1), `new_Mvir` and `halo_grew` (main branch), `infall_recorded` (satellite and orphan) and `clock_estimated` (satellite), and, in a `ConcentrationOn 3` fixture only, `concentration_assigned` (central: `B:291`'s gate taken), each with an `_absent` marker (1, and no value, where the branch does not evaluate it). `tr_join_probe_already_merged_intervening` and `tr_join_probe_already_merged_trailing` drop a progenitor between two retained ones and after the last, and `tr_jp_probe_already_merged_trailing` drops one before the pass (owned by `sage26_initialise_merger_clock`, as the resolver module's fixture list was then at the reader's ceiling of 64, since raised to 256, "Reader Limits").
- `join_and_pass`: `join_progenitors` and then `merger_pass` for one substep over the `ngal` galaxies joining retained (`B:152` passes the returned count to `evolve_galaxies`), each recorded in full (the members of `BLOCK_ALL`, with `Concentration` at `ConcentrationOn 3`) after joining and after the pass. The ordinary physics of the substeps between them (`B:466-517`) is not composed. It shows a newly orphaned galaxy keeping its previous histories and, in a merger in the same snapshot, `add_galaxies_together` moving them into the target's bulge histories (`tr_jp_type0_orphan_history_merge`), or losing them to a disruption (`tr_jp_type1_orphan_history_disrupt`).

### Fused Multiply-Adds

Read with `objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces (whose `model_mergers.o` disassembles identically to the reference workspace's) and, for the driver, from the reference build's own `core_build_model.o`. Every fused instruction on these routines' paths:

| Object, function | Line | Instruction | Expression | On R0 | In `oracle_c06.c` |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_mergers.o`, `deal_with_galaxy_merger` (inlined remnant radius) | `M:158`, `M:171` | scalar `fmadd` | `(M_disk * R_disk_half + M_bulge * R_bulge_half) / M` for each progenitor | yes (output-inert on R0; on R1 it reaches `MergerBulgeRadius`, the c07 table) | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_merger_remnant_radius`) |
| same | `M:204` | scalar `fmadd` | the fallback `(M1 * R1 + M2 * R2) / M_tot` | yes (output-inert on R0; on R1, the c07 table) | transcribed, fused |
| `model_mergers.o`, `grow_black_hole` | `M:352` | scalar `fmadd` | `1.0 + SQR(BH_GROWTH_V_KMS / Vvir)` | yes | transcribed, fused |
| same | `M:362` | scalar `fmsub` | `MetalsColdGas -= metallicity * BHaccrete` | yes | transcribed, fused |
| `model_mergers.o`, `collisional_starburst_recipe` | `M:773` | scalar `fmadd` | `MetalsBulgeMass += metallicity * recycled_stars` | yes | transcribed, fused |
| same | `M:819` | scalar `fmadd` | `MetalsColdGas += Yield * (1.0 - FracZleaveDiskVal) * stars` | yes | transcribed, fused |
| same | `M:651`, `M:653` (two) | scalar float `fmadd` | the KMT09 molecular fraction | no (`SFprescription 5`) | not transcribed |
| same | `M:766` | scalar `fmadd` | `SFHMassBulge += (1.0 - RecycleFraction) * stars` | no (`SaveFullSFH`) | not transcribed |
| same | `M:802` | scalar `fnmsub` | `H1gas = ColdGas * HYDROGEN_MASS_FRAC - H2gas` | no (H2 tracking; yes on R2, "The c08 Group") | transcribed, fused (c08, `transcribe_collisional_starburst_recipe`) |
| `model_mergers.o`, `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS` | `M:871` | scalar `fmadd` | `ICS_sum_mt += StellarMass * time` | yes | transcribed, fused |
| `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `update_from_star_formation` | `S:1123` | vector `fmla.2d` | `ColdGas - (1 - RecycleFraction) * stars` and `StellarMass + (1 - RecycleFraction) * stars` | yes (every burst) | SAGE26's own |
| same | `S:1124` | vector `fmla.2d` | `MetalsColdGas` and `MetalsStellarMass` with `metallicity * (1 - RecycleFraction)` rounded first | yes (every burst) | SAGE26's own |
| `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `update_from_feedback` | `S:1168` | scalar `fmsub` | `MetalsColdGas -= metallicity * reheated_mass` | yes | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_update_from_feedback`) |
| same | line table `S:1176` | scalar `fmadd` | `MetalsHotGas += metallicity * reheated_mass` of `S:1229`: clang merged R0's hot-gas branch with the CGM hot branch and the line table attributes it to the regime test | yes | transcribed, fused |
| same | `S:1181` | scalar `fmadd` | `MetalsCGMgas += metallicity * reheated_mass` | no (`CGMrecipeOn 1`) | not transcribed |
| `model_halo_properties.o`, `get_virial_radius`, `get_virial_velocity` | `:67` | scalar `fmadd` | the `hubble_of_z_sq` polynomial | yes (the clock, joining) | SAGE26's own |
| `model_misc.o`, `get_disk_radius` | `:212-213` | scalar float `fmadd` | the spin magnitude | yes (joining) | SAGE26's own |
| `model_disk_instability.o`, `check_disk_instability` | `D:79` | scalar `fmadd` | `MetalsBulgeMass += metallicity * unstable_stars` | yes (the burst's recheck) | SAGE26's own |
| reference `core_build_model.o`, `evolve_galaxies` | `B:534` | scalar `fmsub` | `Mvir - deltaMvir * (1 - (step + 1) / N)` | yes | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_merger_pass`) |
| same | `B:549` | scalar `fmsub` | `Age - (step + 0.5) * (deltaT / N)` | yes | transcribed, fused |

`model_mergers.o` has no vector fused instruction, `estimate_merging_time`, `quasar_mode_wind`, `add_galaxies_together` and `make_bulge_from_burst` have none, and the joining block of `core_build_model.o` has none (c12 rechecked it with the concentration statement, `B:290-295`: "The c12 Group", Joining). The fused instructions of `oracle_c06.o` are exactly the twelve transcribed sites of the table (`M:158`, `M:171`, `M:204`, `M:352`, `M:362`, `M:773`, `M:819`, `M:871`, `S:1168`, `S:1229`, `B:534`, `B:549`) and no others, checked in its disassembly with line tables; c08 adds a thirteenth, the burst refresh's `M:802` ("The c08 Group"). A port may fuse a site explicitly with `fma()` (as c05 does for `S:1123-1126`) or keep the one C expression; the fixtures fail a port that rounds at other points where a store or recorded double is sensitive to it.

`burst_fused_update_sf` (owned by `sage26_starburst_feedback`) separates the fused `update_from_star_formation` from an unfused one: `StellarMass + (1 - RecycleFraction) * stars` rounds to `0x3fc3d70b` (1.53000009) fused and to `0x3fc3d70a` (1.52999997) unfused. The unfused result comes from an experiment build with `-ffp-contract=off` recorded in this slice's validation record, never written as a fixture.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

Each `.in` states in its comments what it exercises, and whether it is **R0-reachable** or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (inputs SAGE26's routine accepts but R0's dynamics cannot produce). Names carry the same marks: `_probe_` names a probe, `tr_` a source-reviewed transcription fixture, `r0_snap32`/`r0_snap63` a fixture whose galaxies take a real galaxy's values from the R0 reference output at snapshot 32 or 63 (end-of-snapshot output values used as plausible call inputs, not reconstructed call-time state; each `.in` names the rows, the conversions back to code units and what is constructed, such as the galaxies' call-time `SnapNum` (the rows store the output snapshot), `deltaMvir`, the history bins and a satellite's clock where the output has none, and which values are raw output values or SAGE26's sentinels). Probes are kept and asserted like every other fixture. The R0 fixtures of a routine that needs a clock or an accreted mass take it from the oracle's own output of an earlier R0 fixture of the same galaxy (`emt_r0_snap<N>_satellite`, `bh_r0_snap<N>_central`), and say so. Exact ties (a ratio of exactly `ThreshMajorMerger`, a wind energy equal to a binding energy, a clock of exactly 999, a disk fraction of exactly 0.5) and the `*_validate_*` and `*_rounding_*` constructions were found by scratch searches recorded in each `.in`'s comments. `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c06 outputs together with every earlier group's.

## The c07 Group: Bulge Sizes

`oracle_c07.c` covers SAGE26's two bulge-size routines, `get_bulge_radius` (`X:239-377`) and `update_instability_bulge_radius` (`X:385-424`), each called directly, and a **source-reviewed transcription** of the FoF central's disk-radius and bulge-radius synchronisation during progenitor joining (`B:297-298`). The three channels that call the incremental update are composed through SAGE26's own public routines in the existing mains with `BulgeSizeOn 3`: `check_disk_instability` in `oracle_c05` (`di_s3_*`), `add_galaxies_together` and `collisional_starburst_recipe` in `oracle_c06` (`add_s3_*`, `burst_s3_*`). The merger remnant radius and its assignment run through SAGE26's own `deal_with_galaxy_merger` in `oracle_c06` with `BulgeSizeOn 3` (`merge_s3_*`, "The Merger Remnant Radius" below); the remnant routine itself is static in SAGE26, so its locals come from `oracle_c06.c`'s extended **source-reviewed transcription**, `transcribe_merger_remnant_radius`, never from a direct call. `X` is `R/src/model_misc.c`, `B` is `R/src/core_build_model.c`, `M` is `R/src/model_mergers.c` and `D` is `R/src/model_disk_instability.c`. `build.sh` compiles `oracle_c07.c` with the same flags and links it against the same twelve objects, in the same way, into `output/oracle/oracle_c07`; `regenerate.sh` runs it on every fixture whose `routine` line names a c07 routine (the `C07_ROUTINES` list). Run one by hand as `output/oracle/oracle_c07 <input> <output>`. This group changes no earlier fixture's input or output (`regenerate.sh` reproduces all 429 earlier fixtures byte for byte, and after the remnant work all 491 before it); its changes to earlier mains are the mode-3 size block of `oracle_c05.c` below and, in `oracle_c06.c`, the remnant transcription's taps and branch flags and the mode-3 remnant record, which mode-0 fixtures never reach (their outputs are unchanged).

### Flags and Parameters

The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R1.par`: `BulgeSizeOn 3` (`R1.par:109`) and `Hubble_h 0.73` (`R1.par:40`); every other flag and parameter is `R0.par`'s: `R1.par` differs from it only in `BulgeSizeOn` (`:109`) and its output directory (`:7`). The c07 routines read `params.BulgeSizeOn` (0 or 3; any other value is refused, naming the parameter) and, in mode 3 only, `params.Hubble_h` (`X:249`, `:398`), as c06's reader does: a mode-0 fixture carries no `Hubble_h` and one that does is refused as an unread field. Mode-0 fixtures (`gbr_mode0`, `uibr_mode0_returns` and the `*_probe_mode0_stale_radii` probes) pin SAGE26's `BulgeSizeOn 0` behaviour, which the package keeps: `get_bulge_radius` zeroes the three radii and returns 0 (`X:242-247`) and the update returns at once (`X:395`).

### Routines

| Owning module | Routine (`routine` line) | What runs | Recorded |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sage26_set_disk_scale_radius` | `get_bulge_radius` | SAGE26's own routine on galaxy 0, and its checked transcription for the locals | `return.R_bulge` (double); the size block of `galaxy[0]`; the locals and branch flags |
| `sage26_disk_instability` | `update_instability_bulge_radius` | SAGE26's own routine on galaxy 0 with `call.delta_mass` and `call.old_disk_radius`, its input mass ALREADY holding the transfer (the callers store it first, `D:78-83`, `M:518-522`, `M:779-784`), and its checked transcription for the locals | the size block of `galaxy[0]`; the locals and branch flags |
| `sage26_set_disk_scale_radius` | `central_disk_and_bulge_radius` | a **source-reviewed transcription** of `B:297-298`, `DiskScaleRadius = get_disk_radius(...)` then `get_bulge_radius(...)`, calling SAGE26's own routines | `return.r_disk` (the double before the float store); the size block of `galaxy[0]` |
| `sage26_disk_instability` | `check_disk_instability` (c05) | with `BulgeSizeOn 3`: the stellar transfer's radius update (`D:68-83`) and, on gas-unstable disks, the continuation's mode-1 burst and its update (`D:106-107`, `M:779-784`) | as c05, plus the size members below |
| `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption` | `add_galaxies_together` (c06) | with `BulgeSizeOn 3`: satellite disk stars into the target's instability channel, then the target's update from its own disk radius and post-addition disk fraction (`M:503-522`) | as c06 (the block holds the radii) |
| `sage26_starburst_feedback` | `collisional_starburst_recipe` (c06) | with `BulgeSizeOn 3`: the instability channel's update after the SF and bulge/channel stores and before the feedback, from `call.old_disk_radius` (`M:760`, `:770-791`), and the nested minor-merger instability recheck with its own updates (`M:806-811`) | as c06 |
| `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption` | `deal_with_galaxy_merger` (c06) | with `BulgeSizeOn 3`: the synchronisation after the burst, the remnant radius from the post-burst target and the satellite, and its major, disk-dominated minor and spheroid-dominated minor assignment (`M:293-330`; the static remnant routine `M:135-212` through `oracle_c06.c`'s extended transcription) | as c06, plus the remnant record ("The Merger Remnant Radius") |

The size block is the six members the two routines read or write: `MergerBulgeMass`, `InstabilityBulgeMass`, `DiskScaleRadius`, `BulgeRadius`, `MergerBulgeRadius` and `InstabilityBulgeRadius`, every other member poisoned. The direct-routine inputs set all six explicitly, deliberately stale radii included. The two public routines' fixtures live with the module whose package code calls them first: `get_bulge_radius` with `sage26_set_disk_scale_radius` (the central's synchronisation) and `update_instability_bulge_radius` with `sage26_disk_instability` (its primary channel).

**c05's size block.** With `BulgeSizeOn 3`, every galaxy of a c05 routine also reads and records `MergerBulgeMass`, `BulgeRadius`, `MergerBulgeRadius` and `InstabilityBulgeRadius` after its fourteen fields (`SIZE_MEMBERS` in `oracle_c05.c`), the end-state comparison of every composition check and two-sided test includes them, and a gas-unstable fixture's post-transfer markers cover the three radii as well as the three masses. A mode-0 c05 fixture reads, records and compares exactly what it did before.

### Locals: Check Kinds, Two-Sided Validation and the Narrowing Limitation

Neither public routine passes a local to a SAGE26 function (`update_instability_bulge_radius` passes only the galaxy index to `get_bulge_radius`, `X:423`), so every local is **tail-checked**: the transcription (`transcribe_get_bulge_radius`, `transcribe_update_instability_bulge_radius`, each statement cited) runs from the value to the routine's end, and the oracle refuses a fixture unless the fully transcribed composition and the one with SAGE26's own nested `get_bulge_radius` both reproduce SAGE26's own end state and return bit for bit. Each local is written with c06's markers (`_absent`, the value, `_checked`, `_check_kind` 2, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`) and one more, `_narrowing_limited`:

| Routine | Locals | `_narrowing_limited` |
|---|---|---|
| `get_bulge_radius` | `M_merger`, `M_instability`, `M_total` (`X:318-320`); `M_merger_sun`, `log_R_merger_kpc` (`:344-345`); `R_merger`, the merger radius the average uses (`:342`, `:346`); `R_disc` (`:353`); `M_inst_sun`, `log_R_inst_kpc` (`:358-359`); `R_instability`, the instability radius the average uses (`:351`, `:355`, `:360`); `R_bulge` (`:366`) | 0: each reaches the double return, which the fixture records |
| `update_instability_bulge_radius` | `M_old`, `R_old` (`X:399-400`); `R_disc_kpc`, `R_new_contribution_kpc`, `R_new_contribution` (`:404-408`); `R_old_kpc`, `M_new`, `R_new_kpc` (`:413-415`); `R_new` (`:416`, `:419`) | 1 |

The flags are `total_nonpositive`, `merger_mass_zero`, `instability_mass_zero`, `merger_fallback` and `instability_fallback` (0 none, 1 the disk fallback, 2 Shen) for `get_bulge_radius`, and `returned_early` (1 mode, 2 `delta_mass <= 0`, 0 ran) and `incremental` for the update; -1 where not evaluated.

**The narrowing limitation.** Every local of `update_instability_bulge_radius` reaches the state only through `R_new`'s store into the float `InstabilityBulgeRadius` (`X:422`); the closing `get_bulge_radius` reads that float and its double return is discarded (`X:423`). One double-to-float narrowing cannot make both neighbouring doubles of a value differ from the stored float (round to nearest moves at most one of them across a rounding boundary), so these locals are named `_narrowing_limited 1` and are not claimed as two-sided validated; their two-sided test is still run and recorded, and is one-sided at most on every fixture. They are checked through the composition, and the package's locals are compared with them bit for bit. The recorded `intermediate.*` values come from the oracle's transcription; `return.*` and the `galaxy[0]` fields come from SAGE26's own call, and the two are kept apart.

### Driver Transcription

`central_disk_and_bulge_radius` (`tr_sync_*`) transcribes the two statements of the FoF central branch that set the radii (`B:297-298`): the float store of `get_disk_radius`'s double result, then `get_bulge_radius`, whose return is discarded. The rest of the branch (`B:285-295`, `B:300`) is c06's `join_progenitors`. The composition with SAGE26's own `get_bulge_radius` gives the recorded state, and the one with the transcribed `get_bulge_radius` must reproduce it bit for bit; that shows nothing about `B:297-298` itself, which a mutation sweep covers (this slice's validation record). The oracle requires galaxy 0 to be Type 0, as the package synchronises only the FoF central. No local is carried: the two statements form none.

### The Merger Remnant Radius

`deal_with_galaxy_merger` with `BulgeSizeOn 3` (`merge_s3_*`, owned by `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption`, run by `oracle_c06`) is SAGE26's own routine end to end (`M:228-333`): after the addition, the black hole and the burst it synchronises the target's bulge radius (`M:293`), forms the remnant radius from the already combined, post-burst target and the still-populated satellite (`M:297`, `calculate_merger_remnant_radius`, `M:135-212`), and assigns it: a major merger runs `make_bulge_from_burst`, stores it into `MergerBulgeRadius` and synchronises (`M:299-312`); a minor merger onto a target disk-dominated before the addition only synchronises, discarding it (`M:318-325`); onto a spheroid-dominated one it stores and synchronises (`M:326-330`). The recorded galaxies are those of the mode-0 fixtures (the satellite's, target's and FoF central's identities and thirty floats, the three radii among them, and the target's histories).

The remnant routine is static, so its locals come from `transcribe_merger_remnant_radius` in `oracle_c06.c`, extended to every branch with each statement cited, and are **checked through SAGE26's own `deal_with_galaxy_merger`**: the transcribed composition must reproduce SAGE26's end state bit for bit (tail-checked; the routine passes no local to a SAGE26 function). The check constrains the remnant only where it reaches the state, on the major and spheroid-dominated branches, and then only through the float `MergerBulgeRadius`. A mode-3 output appends, after the c06 record:

| Field | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `intermediate.remnant_total_nonpositive` | `M_tot <= 0.0`, which returns 0 (`M:142`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_g1_mass_positive`, `intermediate.remnant_g2_mass_positive` | each progenitor's float `StellarMass + ColdGas > 0` (`M:153`, `:168`); else its radius is 0 (`:160`, `:173`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_r1_fallback`, `intermediate.remnant_r2_fallback` | `R1 <= 0.0` takes `R2` (`M:177`), then `R2 <= 0.0` takes `R1` (`:178`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_both_radii_zero` | `R1 <= 0.0` after both, which returns 0 (`M:179`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_energy_nonpositive` | `E_final <= 0.0`, the mass-weighted fallback (`M:202-205`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_M1`, `_M2`, `_M_tot` | the widened float sums and their double sum (`M:138-140`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_R1_disk_half`, `_R1_bulge_half`, `_M1_disk`, `_M1_bulge`, `_R1_progenitor` | g1's terms (`M:149-157`) and its radius as `M:158` or `:160` form it |
| `intermediate.remnant_R2_disk_half`, `_R2_bulge_half`, `_M2_disk`, `_M2_bulge`, `_R2_progenitor` | the same for g2 (`M:164-173`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_R1`, `_R2` | the radii after the fallbacks (`M:177-178`) |
| `intermediate.remnant_E_init`, `_E_orb`, `_f_gas`, `_E_rad`, `_E_final` | the energies (`M:185-199`) |
| `intermediate.new_merger_radius_narrowing_limited`, `intermediate.old_disk_radius_narrowing_limited` | 1: the two c06 values reach the state only through one float store each |

A flag is -1 where the path does not evaluate it, and each local carries c06's markers (`_absent`, `_checked`, `_check_kind` 2, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`) and `_narrowing_limited` 1: its only path to the state is `new_merger_radius`'s store into the float `MergerBulgeRadius` (`M:307`, `:328`), the narrowing limitation above, so none is claimed as validated two-sided. Their two-sided test is run and recorded and is one-sided at most on every `merge_s3_*` fixture. The locals are transcription-derived; `galaxy[*]` and the c06 record's values are SAGE26's own, and the two are kept apart.

### Fused Multiply-Adds

Read with `objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces (`model_misc.o` and `model_mergers.o`, whose machine code is the reference build's), scalar and vector:

| Object, function | Line | Offset | Instruction | Expression | In mode 3 | In the oracle | In the package |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_misc.o`, `get_bulge_radius` | `X:345` | `0x558` | scalar `fmadd` | `0.56 * log10(M_merger_sun) - 5.54` | yes | transcribed, fused (`oracle_c07.c:616`) | explicit `fma()` |
| same | `X:359` | `0x630` | scalar `fmadd` | `0.56 * log10(M_inst_sun) - 5.54` | yes | transcribed, fused (`oracle_c07.c:636`) | explicit `fma()` |
| same | `X:366` | `0x660` | scalar `fmadd` | `M_merger * R_merger` fused onto the rounded `M_instability * R_instability` | yes | transcribed, fused (`oracle_c07.c:645`) | explicit `fma(M_merger, R_merger, M_instability * R_instability)` |
| `model_misc.o`, `update_instability_bulge_radius` | `X:415` | `0x6f8` | scalar `fmadd` | `R_old_kpc * M_old` fused onto the rounded `R_new_contribution_kpc * delta_mass` | yes | transcribed, fused (`oracle_c07.c:706`) | explicit `fma(R_old_kpc, M_old, R_new_contribution_kpc * delta_mass)` |
| `model_misc.o`, `get_bulge_radius` | `X:264`, `:279-294` | `0x3d0`, `0x44c`, `0x478` | scalar `fmadd` | the Shen relations of modes 1 and 2 | no (modes 1 and 2) | not transcribed | not ported |
| `model_mergers.o`, `deal_with_galaxy_merger` (inlined remnant radius) | `M:158` | `0x338` | scalar `fmadd` | `M1_disk * R1_disk_half` fused onto the rounded `M1_bulge * R1_bulge_half` | yes | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_merger_remnant_radius`) | explicit `fma(M1_disk, R1_disk_half, M1_bulge * R1_bulge_half)` |
| same | `M:171` | `0x388` | scalar `fmadd` | `M2_disk * R2_disk_half` fused onto the rounded `M2_bulge * R2_bulge_half` | yes | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_merger_remnant_radius`) | explicit `fma(M2_disk, R2_disk_half, M2_bulge * R2_bulge_half)` |
| same | `M:204` | `0x400` | scalar `fmadd` | `M1 * R1` fused onto the rounded `M2 * R2` (the `E_final <= 0` fallback) | yes | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_merger_remnant_radius`) | explicit `fma(M1, R1, M2 * R2)` |

Neither routine has a vector fused instruction, and the remnant radius has none. The fused instructions of `oracle_c07.o` are exactly the four transcribed `X` sites, and those of `oracle_c06.o` are still exactly the twelve sites of the c06 table (thirteen since c08, which adds `M:802`), the three remnant sites among them, each with SAGE26's operands fused (checked in their disassembly with line tables). The package writes each site as an explicit `fma()` (`shared/sage_bulge_size_physics.h` for `X`, `shared/sage_merger_physics.h`'s `mimic_sage_merger_remnant_radius()` for `M`), which rounds once on any platform with a correctly rounded `fma`. `X:366` only separates where `R_merger` is a double fallback radius: a float merger radius times the float-widened mass is exact in double, so the fusion changes nothing there.

One fixture separates each fused site from an unfused one. The unfused results come from an experiment build with `-ffp-contract=off` added to the flags for every object and main, recorded in this slice's validation record and never written as a fixture:

| Site | Separating fixture | Fused (the fixture) | Unfused |
|---|---|---|---|
| `X:345` | `gbr_merger_shen_fallback` | `return.R_bulge` `0x3f11500c0bd7a56e` | `0x3f11500c0bd7a573` |
| `X:359` | `gbr_probe_instability_shen_fallback` | `return.R_bulge` `0x3f11500c0bd7a56e` | `0x3f11500c0bd7a573` |
| `X:366` | `gbr_combined_fused` | `return.R_bulge` `0x3f3e8f7bff9fa3e9` | `0x3f3e8f7bff9fa3e7` with `X:345` still fused (`0x3f3e8f7bff9fa3eb` with both unfused) |
| `X:415` | `uibr_fused` | `InstabilityBulgeRadius` `0x39bc9c93` | `0x39bc9c94` |
| `M:158` | `merge_s3_fused_progenitor_target` | `MergerBulgeRadius` and `BulgeRadius` `0x3b84b8d8` (`new_merger_radius` `0x3f70971b10000000`) | `0x3b84b8d9` (`0x3f70971b10000001`) |
| `M:171` | `merge_s3_fused_progenitor_satellite` | `MergerBulgeRadius` and `BulgeRadius` `0x3b441202` (`new_merger_radius` `0x3f6882404fffffff`) | `0x3b441203` (`0x3f68824050000001`) |
| `M:204` | `merge_s3_probe_fused_energy_fallback` | `MergerBulgeRadius` and `BulgeRadius` `0x3afd78b1` (`new_merger_radius` `0x3f5faf1610000001`) | `0x3afd78b0` (`0x3f5faf1610000000`) |

The first two are pure single-channel bulges, so the `X:366` numerator has a zero product and cannot contribute; `uibr_fused` has no merger mass and no fallback, so only `X:415` can. Each remnant fixture leaves one remnant site able to separate: `merge_s3_fused_progenitor_target`'s satellite has no bulge (a zero `M:171` addend) and `E_final > 0` (no `M:204`); `merge_s3_fused_progenitor_satellite`'s target has a float `M_disk` of 0 after the addition (`M:158` adds onto an exact product); `merge_s3_probe_fused_energy_fallback`'s target has no disk radius and its satellite no bulge. None has cold gas left in the target after the addition, so no burst star, black-hole growth or other fused site of the c06 table runs, and in the experiment build only the named site's double changes (`R1_progenitor`, `R2_progenitor`, or only `new_merger_radius`). A fused site separates only where its product lies in a lower binade than the sum: an addend that lies on the sum's grid makes the fused and unfused results equal, which the scratch searches that found these inputs had to avoid.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

Each `.in` states what it exercises and whether it is **R1-reachable** (or, in mode 0, R0-reachable) or a **constructed routine-domain probe**. Names carry the marks: `_probe_` names a probe, `tr_` a source-reviewed transcription fixture, `r1_snap32`/`r1_snap63` a fixture whose galaxies take a real galaxy's values from the R1 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R1/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `870acea288250919624ae8a4b984a4dde7106e6355cdb972cf4880ff04635ca0`) at snapshot 32 or 63: end-of-snapshot output values used as plausible call inputs, not reconstructed call-time state, with each `.in` naming the rows and what is constructed. Every public routine has an R1-derived input at both snapshots (`gbr_r1_*`, `uibr_r1_*`, `di_s3_r1_*`, `add_s3_r1_*`, `burst_s3_r1_*`, `merge_s3_r1_*`), and so does the synchronisation (`tr_sync_r1_*`). The `*_fused`, `*_rounding*` and `*_mass_rounding` inputs were found by scratch searches recorded in each `.in`'s comments and the validation record: one per fused site, the post-store recovery of `M_old` (`uibr_m_old_post_store_rounding`, where recovering it from the pre-store mass changes the stored radius), the left-to-right solar-mass conversion of both Shen fallbacks and the left-to-right `R_old_kpc`. The size routines and channels add 62 fixtures: 42 through `oracle_c07` (21 `gbr_*`, 15 `uibr_*`, 6 `tr_sync_*`) and 20 through the c05 and c06 mains (6 `di_s3_*`, 6 `add_s3_*`, 8 `burst_s3_*`).

The merger remnant radius adds 28 `merge_s3_*` fixtures through `oracle_c06`, owned by `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption`: six R1-derived mergers, a major, a spheroid-dominated minor and a disk-dominated minor at each of snapshots 32 and 63 (`merge_s3_r1_snap<N>_*`); the three branches again with deliberately stale radii on both galaxies (`merge_s3_major`, `merge_s3_minor_spheroid`, `merge_s3_minor_disk_dominated`), a minor merger whose target is disk-dominated before the addition and not after it (`merge_s3_minor_disk_morphology_flips`, where the pre-addition flag selects the branch); and the two branch boundaries of the assignment, a mass ratio exactly at `ThreshMajorMerger`, which the strict `>` makes minor (`merge_s3_ratio_equal_threshold`), and a target disk exactly half its stars, which is not disk-dominated (`merge_s3_minor_disk_equal_half`); every branch of the remnant radius, a baryon-free satellite (`merge_s3_satellite_empty`, R1-reachable) and the probes for the rest: `M_tot <= 0` with both sums 0 and with `M_tot` exactly 0 (`merge_s3_probe_remnant_total_nonpositive`, `merge_s3_probe_total_exactly_zero`), a target sum of 0 and a negative one (`merge_s3_probe_wind_empties_target`, `merge_s3_probe_target_sum_negative`), a positive target sum with a zero radius (`merge_s3_probe_target_no_radius`) and with a negative one (`merge_s3_probe_target_radius_negative`), both radii zero (`merge_s3_probe_both_radii_zero`, `merge_s3_probe_wind_empties_satellite_no_radius`, the latter exactly at `M:179`'s boundary) or negative (`merge_s3_probe_both_radii_negative`), and `E_final < 0` and exactly 0 (`merge_s3_probe_fused_energy_fallback`, `merge_s3_probe_energy_exactly_zero`); one separating fixture per fused remnant site (above); and two inputs where evaluating a float sum of the remnant in double changes the stored radius (`merge_s3_rounding_float_sums_major`, `merge_s3_rounding_float_sums_spheroid`). A mutation sweep of the extended transcription (the validation record) kills every mutant but five, each shown equivalent there. `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c07 outputs together with every earlier group's.

## The c08 Group: Molecular Gas, the H2 Star-Formation Law and the Commits' Hydrogen Caps

c08 covers what SAGE26's `SFprescription 1` switches on: the Blitz & Rosolowsky (2006) molecular fraction with SAGE26's radial integration, the H2-based star-formation law `sfr_br06`, the pre-SF H I with its new H2 input, the hydrogen caps SAGE26 applies after the star-formation commit and after the reheating, and those of the merger family: the black-hole accretion's cap and the burst's hydrogen refresh ("The Merger Family's Hydrogen Sites" below). `H` is `R/src/model_h2_chemistry.c` and `S` is `R/src/model_starformation_and_feedback.c`. The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R2.par`: `SFprescription 1` (`R2.par:48`) with `BulgeSizeOn 3` (`:109`) and every other flag and parameter `R1.par`'s (`R2.par` differs from `R1.par` only in `SFprescription` and its output directory); `R2.par` sets none of the H2 integration parameters, so they are SAGE26's defaults, `H2RadialIntegrationOn 1`, `H2RadialNBins 25` and `H2RadialRMaxFactor 5.0` (`R/src/core_read_parameter_file.c:99-101`), and `StarburstColdGasOn` is its default 1 (`:109`).

The oracle code is split between a new main and additions to `oracle_c05.c` and, for the merger family's sites, `oracle_c06.c`. **`oracle_c08.c`** holds the two public H2 routines, called directly, and the source-reviewed transcriptions of their locals; `build.sh` compiles it with the same flags and links it against the same twelve objects into `output/oracle/oracle_c08`, and `regenerate.sh` runs it on every fixture whose `routine` line names a c08 routine (the `C08_ROUTINES` list). **`oracle_c05.c`** gains the configuration reader below, a transcription of `sfr_br06` inside its composed `starformation_and_feedback`, a transcription of `update_from_star_formation`, the post-reheating cap in its `update_from_feedback` transcription (which aborted on hydrogen tracking before), and the c08 records of those routines. Every addition is reached only with `SFprescription 1`, except the composition check of `update_from_star_formation`'s transcription, which every wrapper fixture now also passes; `regenerate.sh` reproduces all 519 earlier fixtures byte for byte. The fixtures of the two public H2 routines and of the wrapper live with `sage26_calculate_star_formation`, whose package code calls the H2 routines; the isolated cap fixtures live with `sage26_apply_star_formation_supernova`.

### Flags and Parameters

The c05 configuration reader (`read_sf_prescription`) reads `params.SFprescription` as 0 (R0, R1) or 1 (R2) and refuses any other value, naming the parameter. For 1 it requires, explicitly and at exactly these values, `params.H2RadialIntegrationOn` 1, `params.H2RadialNBins` 25 and `params.H2RadialRMaxFactor` 5.0, and `params.StarburstColdGasOn` 1 (which `starformation_and_feedback`'s flag reader pins for every configuration already); any other value is refused, naming the parameter. For 0 it reads nothing more, so every earlier fixture is read unchanged. `H2DiskAreaOption` stays poisoned: only the single-slab branch (`S:225-236`) and the `StarburstColdGasOn 0` burst fuel (`R/src/model_mergers.c:625-629`) read it, neither supported, so the poisoned state never supplies an H2 parameter. `starformation_and_feedback`, `update_from_star_formation`, `update_from_feedback` and `check_disk_instability` accept `SFprescription` 1 (`check_disk_instability` and the wrapper's gas-unstable continuation since "The Merger Family's Hydrogen Sites" below); the sequence still requires 0. `oracle_c08` reads the same configuration for the radial integration and requires `SFprescription` 1: the integration is supported only as `sfr_br06` calls it. `calculate_molecular_fraction_BR06` reads no parameter.

### Routines

| Owning module | Routine (`routine` line) | What runs | Recorded |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | `calculate_molecular_fraction_BR06` | SAGE26's own routine (`H:117-125`) on `call.gas_surface_density_msun_pc2`, `call.stellar_surface_density_msun_pc2` and `call.disk_scale_length_pc`; its checked transcription for the locals | `return.f_mol`; the branch flags; the locals of the routine and its two static helpers |
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | `calculate_molecular_fraction_radial_integration` | SAGE26's own routine (`H:134-290`) on galaxy 0 with a NULL SFR-output pointer, as `sfr_br06` calls it (`S:222`); its checked transcription for the locals | `return.H2_code_units`; `galaxy[0].H2gas` and `galaxy[0].H2DepletionTime_Gyr`; the branch flags; the scalar locals; every annulus (`intermediate.bin[<i>].*`, `i` from 0 to 24) |
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | `starformation_and_feedback` (c05) | with `SFprescription 1`: SAGE26's wrapper with `sfr_br06` (`S:193-252`), its pre-SF H I with the new H2, and the two commits' caps | as c05 with the size block (`BulgeSizeOn 3`) and `H2DepletionTime_Gyr` on every galaxy, then the c08 flags and values below |
| `sage26_apply_star_formation_supernova` | `update_from_star_formation` (c05) | with `SFprescription 1`: SAGE26's own routine, whose post-SF cap (`S:1131-1135`) its transcription must reproduce | as c05, then the cap's flags and `max_h_sf` |
| `sage26_apply_star_formation_supernova` | `update_from_feedback` (c05) | with `SFprescription 1`: SAGE26's own routine, whose post-reheating cap (`S:1169-1173`) its transcription must reproduce | as c05, then the cap's flags and `max_h_fb` |

The radial integration's galaxy sets `DiskScaleRadius`, `ColdGas`, `MetalsColdGas` (read by the disk-metallicity hoist `H:183-184`, which the BR06 branch never uses), `StellarMass`, `BulgeMass`, `H2gas` and `H2DepletionTime_Gyr`, every other member poisoned; the oracle refuses a fixture on which SAGE26's routine writes any member but `H2gas`. A wrapper fixture of `SFprescription 1` also reads and records `H2DepletionTime_Gyr` on every galaxy: `sfr_br06` passes no SFR-output pointer, so SAGE26 leaves it as it was (`H:279-285`), −1 on every R2 galaxy.

### Transcriptions, Check Kinds and Two-Sided Validation

`calculate_stellar_scale_height_BR06` (`H:48-60`) and `calculate_molecular_fraction_BR06_from_hstar` (`H:68-101`) are static and cannot be linked, so the locals of both public routines come from **source-reviewed transcriptions** in `oracle_c08.c` (`transcribe_stellar_scale_height_BR06`, `transcribe_molecular_fraction_BR06_from_hstar`, `transcribe_molecular_fraction_BR06`, `transcribe_radial_integration`), each statement cited to its line, composed into the public routine. The oracle refuses a fixture unless the transcribed public routine reproduces SAGE26's own return and every galaxy member bit for bit. The radial transcription restates only the BR06 path with a NULL pointer; the GD14, KD12, KMT09 and K13 blocks (`H:161-205`, `:229-259`) and the K13 SFR and depletion time (`:268-272`, `:279-285`) sit behind prescription tests or the pointer and are not transcribed. In `oracle_c05.c`, `transcribe_sfr_br06` (`S:195-252`) calls SAGE26's own public radial integration, `transcribe_update_from_star_formation` restates `S:1121-1135`, and `transcribe_update_from_feedback` gains `S:1169-1173`; the wrapper's two compositions (every routine transcribed, and SAGE26's own `update_from_star_formation`, `update_from_feedback` and `check_disk_instability`) must each reproduce SAGE26's end state.

No c08 routine passes a local to a SAGE26 function (the helpers are static and transcribed; `log10`, `pow`, `expf` and `sqrt` are libm), so every c08 value is **tail-checked**. Each is written with c06's markers (`_absent`, the value as the float or double it is, `_checked`, `_check_kind` 2, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`) and c07's `_narrowing_limited`. A float local is perturbed to its neighbouring floats (`nextafterf`), a double one to its neighbouring doubles. The c05 values of a prescription-1 wrapper fixture keep c05's record exactly as on every earlier wrapper fixture.

| Routine | Values | `_narrowing_limited` |
|---|---|---|
| `calculate_molecular_fraction_BR06` | `log_h_star`, `h_star_pc` (`H:56-57`); `effective_sigma_stars`, `pressure` (`:73-80`); `pressure_ratio`, `R_mol` (`:93-94`) | 0 |
| same | `f_mol` (`:98`), the double whose only path to the float return is its narrowing (`:100`) | 1 |
| `calculate_molecular_fraction_radial_integration` | `h`, `rs_pc`, `M_gas_total`, `M_disk_star`, `sigma_gas_0`, `sigma_star_0`, `R_MAX`, `dr`, `log_h_star`, `h_star_br06` (`H:138-178`); per annulus `r`, `exp_factor`, `sigma_gas_r`, `sigma_star_r`, `f_mol_r`, `dM_H2`, `M_H2_total` (`:212-265`) | 0 |
| `starformation_and_feedback` (`sfr_br06`) | `rs_pc` (`S:215`), `H2gas_radial` (H2gas after the radial integration, `S:222`), `H2gas_sf` (after the cap, `S:239-242`, the H2 the SF step hands on), `H1gas_sf` (after the H I block, `S:1045`) | 0 |
| same | `h2_budget`, the double `ColdGas * HYDROGEN_MASS_FRAC` the cap compares with and, binding, narrows into H2gas (`S:239-240`) | 1 |
| `update_from_star_formation`, `update_from_feedback` (and the wrapper) | `max_h_sf` (`S:1132`), `max_h_fb` (`S:1170`) | 0 |

The flags are `scale_length_nonpositive` (`H:120`), `pressure_branch` (`H:72`), `stellar_floor_binds` (`H:74`) and `pressure_nonpositive` (`H:83`) for the BR06 routine; `returned_early` (`H:141`), `disk_stars_positive` (`H:151`) and per annulus `bin[<i>].skipped` (`H:220`) and `bin[<i>].stellar_floor_binds` for the radial integration; `br06_rs_nonpositive` (`S:216`), `br06_radial_returned_early`, `br06_h2_cap_binds` (`S:239`) and `br06_sf_from_h2` (`S:244`) for `sfr_br06`; and `hydrogen_caps_sf_tracked`, `h2_cap_sf_binds`, `h1_cap_sf_binds` (`S:1131-1134`) and `hydrogen_caps_fb_tracked`, `h2_cap_fb_binds`, `h1_cap_fb_binds` (`S:1169-1172`) for the caps; -1 where the path does not evaluate them. Where a cap binds, the capped field equals the bound (`max_h`, or `h2_budget` narrowed).

Most annulus values are not validated two-sided: one annulus contributes a twenty-fifth of a float sum, so a one-ulp change of one of its floats is absorbed by the accumulator's rounding unless it sits near a rounding boundary; `M_H2_total` after an annulus, the running sum, is validated far more often. The pre-cap and post-cap H2 and the handed-on H I reach the end state directly and are validated on most fixtures. `f_mol` and `h2_budget` are narrowing-limited (one double-to-float narrowing is their only path), recorded by name and never claimed as validated two-sided; their two-sided test is run and recorded, and is one-sided at most.

### Fused Multiply-Adds

Read with `objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces, scalar and vector (`model_h2_chemistry.o`, `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `oracle_c05.o`, `oracle_c08.o`):

| Object, function | Line | Offset | Instruction | Expression | On R2 | In the oracle | In the package |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_h2_chemistry.o`, `calculate_molecular_fraction_BR06` (inlined height) | `H:56` | `0x50` | scalar `fmadd` | `-0.23 + 0.8 * log10(R*)` | yes (the public routine) | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_stellar_scale_height_BR06`, inlined into `transcribe_molecular_fraction_BR06`) | explicit `fma(0.8, log10(R*), -0.23)` (`shared/sage_h2_physics.h`) |
| `model_h2_chemistry.o`, `calculate_molecular_fraction_radial_integration` (inlined height) | `H:56` (via `:177-178`) | `0x35c` | scalar `fmadd` | the same, once per call | yes | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_stellar_scale_height_BR06`, inlined into `transcribe_radial_integration`) | the same helper |
| same (inlined K13 depletion time) | `H:56` (via `:309`) | `0x61c` | scalar `fmadd` | the same, in the K13 hydrostatic limit | no (non-NULL SFR pointer) | not transcribed | not ported |
| same | `H:170`; `:188-189`; `:194-195`; `:210` (hoisted K13 terms); `:378` (inlined KD12); `:247`; `:271` | `0x2c8`; `0x42c`, `0x44c`, `0x460`; `0x4e8`, `0x50c`, `0x514`; `0x6f4`, `0x6fc`; `0x80c`; `0x9b8`; `0xb8c` | scalar `fmadd` | the GD14, KMT09, K13 and KD12 fits and the K13 SFR | no (prescriptions 4-7, non-NULL pointer) | not transcribed | not ported |
| `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `starformation_and_feedback` | `S:1036` | `0x728` | scalar `fnmsub` | `ColdGas * HYDROGEN_MASS_FRAC - H2gas`, now with the BR06 H2 | yes | transcribed, fused (the H I block of `compose_wrapper`) | explicit `fma(ColdGas, 0.74, -H2gas)` (`sage26_calculate_star_formation.c`) |
| same (inlined `update_from_star_formation`) and `update_from_star_formation` | `S:1123`, `:1124` | `0xdc8`, `0xde0`; `0x20a8`, `0x20c0` | vector `fmla.2d` | c05's four fused stores | yes | transcribed, fused (`transcribe_update_from_star_formation`, two `fmla.2d`), and SAGE26's own | explicit `fma()` (`shared/sage_sf_feedback_physics.h`, unchanged) |

`sfr_br06` and both cap sites have no fused instruction; the BR06 annulus mass and its accumulation are float products and a float sum, not fused (`H:263-265`). The height's `pow(10.0, log_h_star)` (`H:57`) is a call to `__exp10` in SAGE26's object and in `oracle_c08.o` (clang's simplification of `pow` with base 10); `R_mol`'s `pow` (`H:94`) is a call to `pow` in both. The package writes `pow(10.0, ...)`, which Mimic's `-O2` build also turns into `__exp10` and its `-O0` unit-test build does not; for every float argument from −37 to 31 (the range a positive float `R*` gives), `(float)pow(10.0, x)` and `(float)__exp10(x)` are the same float (an exhaustive check, the validation record), although the doubles differ for 2265150 of them. The fused instructions of `oracle_c08.o` are exactly the two inlined copies of the transcribed height, and those of `oracle_c05.o` are c05's five scalar sites and the two `fmla.2d` of the `update_from_star_formation` transcription (at c08; c10 adds four, "The c10 Group, Part 3"), each with SAGE26's operands (checked in the disassembly with line tables).

One fixture separates each c08 site from an unfused evaluation, from an experiment build with `-ffp-contract=off` added to the flags for every object and main (the validation record), never written as a fixture:

| Site | Separating fixture | Fused (the fixture) | Unfused |
|---|---|---|---|
| `H:56`, `0x50` | `br06_probe_fused_height` | `return.f_mol` `0x38e862a8` (`log_h_star` `0xc0bfc683`) | `0x38e862b0` (`0xc0bfc684`) |
| `H:56`, `0x35c` | `radial_probe_fused_height` | `H2gas` `0x0f09cda6` (`log_h_star` `0xc0bfc683`) | `0x0f09cdab` (`0xc0bfc684`) |
| `S:1036`, `0x728` | `sf_br06_rounding_atomich_fused` | `H1gas` `0x3e0c0acd` | `0x3e0c0ace` |

The height's fusion is observable only through its narrowing to the float `log_h_star`: an exhaustive scan of every positive finite float `R*` finds exactly one, `0x338656be` (6.25563956e-08 pc), for which the fused and unfused doubles narrow to different floats. Both height fixtures use it (a constructed routine-domain probe), the radial one through `DiskScaleRadius` `0x294da982` and a gas disk of 3e-27 code units whose annuli sit where the molecular fraction is sensitive. The H I fixture's galaxy was found by a scratch search over `DiskScaleRadius` and `ColdGas` with SAGE26's own radial H2 (8337886 trials); in the experiment build only its H I values change.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R2-reachable** or a **constructed routine-domain probe**; that line is authoritative. `_probe_` in a name is a naming aid only: every fixture named with it is a probe, but 22 of the 35 c08 probes carry no `_probe_` (for example `br06_gas_negative`, `br06_scale_length_negative`, `sf_br06_rs_negative_di_off`, `ufsf_r2_h2_at_h1_above`). `r2_snap32`/`r2_snap63` names a fixture whose galaxies take a real galaxy's values from the R2 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R2/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `c6af6c9385d41adcccbd5f19e064323486706b0965183f61ebded290331f6714`) at snapshot 32 or 63: end-of-snapshot output values used as plausible call inputs, not reconstructed call-time state. Every public routine has an R2-derived input at both snapshots: the radial integration (`radial_r2_*`), the BR06 routine (`br06_r2_*`, the arguments the radial integration passes for one annulus of an R2-derived radial fixture, as `oracle_c08` records them), the wrapper (`sf_br06_r2_*`) and both commits (`ufsf_r2_snap*`, `uff_r2_snap*`). c08 adds 73 fixtures: 47 owned by `sage26_calculate_star_formation` (14 `br06_*` and 18 `radial_*` through `oracle_c08`, 15 `sf_br06_*` through `oracle_c05`) and 26 owned by `sage26_apply_star_formation_supernova` (14 `ufsf_r2_*` and 12 `uff_r2_*` through `oracle_c05`).

They cover: the radial integration's early return on a zero or negative scale radius and on zero or negative cold gas, without writing `H2gas` (`radial_returned_early_*`); the gas cutoff at, one float below and one float above `1e-3f` in the last annulus (`radial_gas_cutoff_*`, found by a scratch search over `ColdGas` ulps); zero and negative disk-star mass with the stellar floor in every annulus, and a disk floored only in its outer annuli (`radial_disk_stars_zero`, `radial_probe_disk_stars_negative`, `radial_stellar_floor_mixed`); every annulus below the cutoff (`radial_all_bins_skipped`); the BR06 routine's scale-length guard, zero and negative gas (pressure 0), the stellar floor below, at and above the float 0.1 and for zero and negative stars, a pressure that narrows to 0 (`br06_*`); `sfr_br06`'s non-positive own `Vvir` and `rs_pc` (H2gas 0), the H2 cap binding on a stale H2gas when the cold gas is gone and not binding everywhere else, `H2gas > 0 && tdyn > 0` failing on H2 (`sf_br06_all_bins_skipped`, `sf_br06_coldgas_zero_stale_h2`) and on tdyn (`sf_br06_probe_vvir_infinite`), the H2 caps binding at both commits under a renormalisation and the H1 cap binding after the reheating (`sf_br06_probe_renorm_h2_caps`, `sf_br06_probe_h1_cap_fb`), and a stale H2gas of −0.0 the strict cap keeps (`sf_br06_probe_coldgas_zero_h2_negative_zero`); and, at each commit, H2gas and H1gas each above, exactly at and below the bound, independently, both below with their sum above (the caps never act on the sum), no cold gas (bound 0), a −0.0 cold gas (bound +0.0, not −0.0), an H1gas of −0.0 at a zero bound, zero-mass events, a bound whose narrowing rounded up (`ufsf_r2_*`, `uff_r2_*`). Not constructed: an `H2gas > ColdGas * 0.74` from a fresh radial integration (the annulus sum of `0.74 f_mol` over 25 annuli to five scale radii is at most 0.96 of the hydrogen, so the cap binds only on a value the integration left), negative cold gas in the wrapper (SAGE26 itself aborts at `S:1158-1160`, the reheated mass clamped to the negative gas), the height's own guard (`H:52`, which neither public routine reaches with a non-positive length) and `h_star_pc > 0.0` failing (`H:72`), which only a NaN scale length does, since every positive float length gives a positive normal height, and a NaN would be indistinguishable from an absent local in the package's record. The `*_rounding_*` fixtures pin roundings a port could miss: the float disk-star subtraction, the height's `pow` (not `powf`), `expf` (not `exp`), the pressure's division (not a multiplication by the inverse), and the commit's fused cold-gas store and metal grouping. A mutation sweep of the transcriptions (the validation record) kills 60 of 79 mutants; each of the 19 survivors is shown equivalent there, most by an exhaustive or executed check. `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c08 outputs together with every earlier group's.

### The Merger Family's Hydrogen Sites

With `SFprescription 1` SAGE26 also caps the hydrogen after black-hole accretion and refreshes it at the end of every burst (`M` is `R/src/model_mergers.c`). These sites are reached through the c06 routines and through the gas-unstable continuation of the c05 instability and wrapper, so the oracle code is additions to `oracle_c06.c` and `oracle_c05.c`; no new main and no `build.sh` or `regenerate.sh` change is needed (the routines are already in `C05_ROUTINES` and `C06_ROUTINES`). Every addition is reached only with `SFprescription 1`, and every record is written after every earlier field, so `regenerate.sh` reproduces all 592 earlier fixtures byte for byte.

**Flags.** `oracle_c06.c`'s merger-family reader (`read_merger_config`) reads `params.SFprescription` 0 or 1 with `read_sf_prescription`, `oracle_c05.c`'s reader restated: for 1 it requires `H2RadialIntegrationOn` 1, `H2RadialNBins` 25 and `H2RadialRMaxFactor` 5.0, which no c06 routine reads on a supported path (only the `StarburstColdGasOn 0` fuel, `M:606-679`, refused, calls the radial integration) but which keep the configuration R2's whole; `H2DiskAreaOption` stays poisoned. c05's `check_disk_instability` reads it the same way. The rest of the flags are as before, and `BulgeSizeOn` is 3 on every R2 fixture.

**The sites.** Each is ported under SAGE26's own guard at its site, never unified:

| Site | Guard | What it does | Transcribed in |
|---|---|---|---|
| `M:366-372`, `grow_black_hole` after the accretion and the negative-metal floor, before `quasar_mode_wind` | `SFprescription != 0 && SFprescription != 2` | `H2gas` and `H1gas` each capped, strictly and on its own, at the float `max_h_bh = (ColdGas > 0.0f) ? ColdGas * 0.74 : 0.0f`; the wind may then zero `ColdGas` with no refresh after it | `transcribe_grow_black_hole` |
| `S:1169-1173`, the burst's `update_from_feedback` | `sf_prescription_tracks_h2` | the post-reheating cap of c05's transcription, restated in c06's copy (which aborted on hydrogen tracking before) | `transcribe_update_from_feedback` (c06) |
| `S:1131-1135`, the burst's `update_from_star_formation` | `sf_prescription_tracks_h2` | SAGE26's own routine in the burst; the oracle records whether it changed `H2gas` or `H1gas` (an observation: a binding strict cap always changes the field) | not transcribed (c05's `transcribe_update_from_star_formation` covers it) |
| `M:798-803`, the burst after its feedback, before the instability recheck | `sf_prescription_tracks_h2` | `H2gas` capped against the double `ColdGas * 0.74`; then `H1gas` overwritten, whatever it held, with the fused double `ColdGas * 0.74 - H2gas`; then a negative stored `H1gas` floored to 0; no ionisation correction | `transcribe_collisional_starburst_recipe` |

The nested recheck's black-hole growth and burst are SAGE26's own `check_disk_instability` in every composition (c06's convention), so their sites are checked through the end state, and the recheck's black-hole growth is also **observed**: before SAGE26's recheck runs, `observe_recheck_growth` restates the recheck's unstable gas fraction (`D:54-66`, `D:90`, `D:101`, as c05's transcription does; the stellar transfer writes no field `grow_black_hole` reads) on a copy of the state the recheck receives and runs SAGE26's own `grow_black_hole` on that copy; after the recheck, the copy's `BlackHoleMass` and `QuasarModeBHaccretionMass` must equal SAGE26's bit for bit (the recheck's burst writes neither), or the fixture is refused. The observation records whether that growth ran, whether it changed `H2gas` and `H1gas` (a binding strict cap always does) and the two fields after it. The restated fraction reaches the check only through those two floats, a narrowing limitation of the observation's input, by name. On the gas-unstable continuation of c05's `check_disk_instability` and of the prescription-1 wrapper (admitted now; the wrapper still refuses it at prescription 0) the continuation is SAGE26's own `grow_black_hole` and `collisional_starburst_recipe`, and the oracle records the forming galaxy's state after `grow_black_hole` and before the burst as `intermediate.after_bh.galaxy[<p>].*` (the fourteen written fields, the size members, on the wrapper `H2DepletionTime_Gyr`, and the two black-hole fields), only where the gas branch ran.

**The black-hole cap is end-state invariant** on every path that reaches it, a limitation distinct from the narrowing limitation: SAGE26 follows every black-hole growth with a burst (`M:263` then `M:285` in a merger, `D:103` then `D:106` in the instability and its recheck), whose star-formation commit re-caps `H2gas` and `H1gas` at the hydrogen of a cold gas never larger than the growth left (a bound never looser) and whose refresh overwrites `H1gas`; so a fixture's end state is the same whether or not the growth capped, and the only evidence of the cap on the merger, instability and recheck paths is its intermediates: the `bh_*` flags and `max_h_bh` of the merger's transcribed growth, `intermediate.after_bh.*` on the instability path, and the recheck observation above. The standalone `grow_black_hole` fixtures (`bh_r2_*`), where no burst follows, are the only ones whose end state shows it. The package tests assert each intermediate against the port: the quasar oracle test at the routine boundary, the resolver test through a copy of the quasar consumer's merger growth required equal to the consumer's result, the instability and wrapper tests through the state after the quasar consumer, and the burst oracle test against the burst's `recheck_bh_caps` locals.

Two equivalences follow from SAGE26's order and are pinned by the record, not by the end state. In a burst the post-reheating cap and the refresh's `H2gas` cap act on the same cold gas: after the float cap, `H2gas` exceeds the double budget only where the float bound rounded up above it and `H2gas` equals it, and then the refresh stores the same float, so the refresh's `H2gas` cap is value-inert and the post-reheating cap's `H1gas` half is overwritten at `M:801`. Both are ported and their flags asserted.

**Records.** Written only for `SFprescription 1`, after every earlier field: for `grow_black_hole` and `deal_with_galaxy_merger` the flags `bh_hydrogen_caps_tracked`, `bh_h2_cap_binds`, `bh_h1_cap_binds` and the value `max_h_bh`; for `collisional_starburst_recipe` and `deal_with_galaxy_merger` the flags `burst_sf_commit_h2_capped`, `burst_sf_commit_h1_capped` (observations), `hydrogen_caps_fb_tracked`, `h2_cap_fb_binds`, `h1_cap_fb_binds`, `burst_hydrogen_refresh_tracked`, `burst_refresh_h2_cap_binds`, `burst_refresh_h1_floored` and the values `max_h_fb`, `burst_h2_budget` and `burst_h1_refresh`, then the recheck observation, `burst_recheck_bh_grown` (-1 where the recheck did not run, 0 where it ran without growing the black hole, 1 where it did) and, where it is 1, `burst_recheck_bh_h2_capped`, `burst_recheck_bh_h1_capped` (observations) and the floats `burst_recheck_after_bh.H2gas` and `burst_recheck_after_bh.H1gas` (SAGE26's own values). Flags are -1 where the path does not evaluate them. No value is passed to a SAGE26 function, so each is **tail-checked** (`_check_kind` 2) and carries c06's markers and c07's `_narrowing_limited`: `max_h_bh` and `max_h_fb` are floats, perturbed to their neighbouring floats, not narrowing-limited; `burst_h2_budget` (compared with `H2gas` and, binding, narrowed into it) and `burst_h1_refresh` (narrowed into `H1gas`; the floor reads the float) are doubles whose only path to the state is one narrowing, so they are `_narrowing_limited 1`, recorded by name and never claimed as validated two-sided. The values are transcription-derived; `galaxy[*]`, `intermediate.c06.*` and `intermediate.after_bh.*` are SAGE26's own.

| Owning module | Routine | c08 fixtures |
|---|---|---|
| `sage26_quasar_mode` | `grow_black_hole`, `quasar_mode_wind` | 18: `bh_r2_snap32_central`, `bh_r2_snap63_central` (R2-derived), `H2gas` and `H1gas` each one float above, exactly at and one float below the bound in seven combinations (`bh_r2_h2_<a>_h1_<b>`), both below with their sum above (`bh_r2_sum_above_both_below`), the accretion emptying the cold gas (bound 0, `bh_r2_probe_accretion_empties_coldgas`), `-0.0` kept at a zero bound (`bh_r2_probe_signed_zero_at_zero_bound`), no cold gas (the guard never evaluated, `bh_r2_no_coldgas`), the cap binding before a wind that zeroes the cold gas (`bh_r2_cap_then_wind_ejects_cold`; `bh_r2_snap32_central`'s wind does the same on a real galaxy), a bound whose narrowing rounded up (`bh_r2_probe_bound_rounded_up`); the wind on its own (`qw_r2_snap32_central`, `qw_r2_snap63_central`, `qw_r2_cold_ejected_hydrogen_kept`) |
| `sage26_starburst_feedback` | `collisional_starburst_recipe` | 16: `burst_r2_snap32_central`, `burst_r2_snap63_central` (R2-derived), a zero-star burst replacing a stale `H1gas` of 0 (`burst_r2_zero_star_stale_h1_replaced`), an ionisation-reduced `H1gas` restored (`burst_r2_ionised_h1_restored`), stale hydrogen above every bound (`burst_r2_stale_hydrogen_above_bounds`), the refresh's cap binding on a rounded-up bound with a negative remainder floored (`burst_r2_probe_refresh_cap_binds_h1_floored`), `H2gas` exactly at the double budget (`burst_r2_probe_refresh_h2_at_budget`), the hydrogen exactly at the commits' bounds (`burst_r2_probe_hydrogen_at_commit_bounds`), signed zeros (`burst_r2_probe_signed_zero_hydrogen`), a subnormal cold gas whose remainder narrows to `-0.0`, kept by the float floor (`burst_r2_probe_subnormal_h1_negative_zero`), the fused-site separator (`burst_r2_rounding_h1_refresh_fused`), a merger burst whose recheck reaches its gas branch and whose growth binds the `H1gas` cap (`burst_r2_mode0_minor_recheck_unstable`), and four c06 templates with R2's flags: the feedback's ejection and metal caps binding with stale hydrogen (`burst_r2_metals_cap_binds`), the renormalisation with stale hydrogen (`burst_r2_renorm_active`), a negative trigger with the stars floored (`burst_r2_probe_negative_fraction`) and a host `Vvir` of zero (`burst_r2_probe_host_vvir_zero`) |
| `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption` | `deal_with_galaxy_merger`, `merger_pass` | 6: `merge_r2_snap32_minor`, `merge_r2_snap63_minor`, `merge_r2_snap63_major` (R2-derived), the accretion cap binding on the target's stale hydrogen, which the addition leaves as it was (it sums the cold gas, not `H2gas` or `H1gas`, `M:456-543`; `merge_r2_bh_caps_bind`), the nested recheck's gas branch, whose growth binds the `H1gas` cap (`merge_r2_recheck_unstable`), and two chained mergers in one bin (`tr_pass_r2_two_mergers_one_bin`) |
| `sage26_disk_instability` | `check_disk_instability` (c05) | 3: `di_r2_snap32_central_unstable`, `di_r2_snap63_central_unstable` (R2-derived, cold gas raised by 25%), and the accretion cap binding on the instability path (`di_r2_bh_caps_bind`) |
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | `starformation_and_feedback` (c05) | 2: `sf_br06_r2_snap32_central_unstable`, `sf_br06_r2_snap63_central_unstable` (R2-derived, cold gas raised by 25%: the wrapper's gas-unstable continuation, the refresh restoring the H I the disk SF reduced for ionisation) |

Every input states its construction and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is R2-reachable (16) or a constructed routine-domain probe (29), 45 in all; the line's leading classification decides, so `burst_r2_zero_star_stale_h1_replaced` ("constructed routine-domain probe ...; a zero-fraction instability burst is R2-reachable only as a limit") is a probe. By module: quasar 5 and 13, starburst 4 and 12, resolver 3 and 3, instability 2 and 1, star formation 2 and 0. The R2-derived inputs take a real galaxy's values from the R2 reference output (SHA-256 `c6af6c9385d41adcccbd5f19e064323486706b0965183f61ebded290331f6714`) at snapshots 32 and 63, every public routine at both. The exact boundaries came from scratch scans recorded in each input's comments (a first oracle run for the bound, exhaustive float scans for the exact budget and the fused separator). A mutation sweep of the extended transcriptions (the validation record) kills every mutant but four, each an exact equivalence, and a sweep of the recheck observation kills every mutant that changes it but four, each an exact equivalence on the oracle's domain or a removal of the check itself.

**Fused multiply-adds.** Read with `objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces, scalar and vector: on these paths SAGE26's `model_mergers.o` fuses one new site, the refresh's `H1gas` at `M:802` (scalar `fnmsub` at offset `0x10b8`, `ColdGas * 0.74 - H2gas` with the product unrounded); the accretion cap (`M:367-368`) and the refresh's budget and compare (`M:799-800`) are an `fmul` and an `fcvt` with no fusion, and the guards compile to `tst w8, #0xfffffffd` (zero for 0 and 2). The fused instructions of `oracle_c06.o` are now c06's twelve and `M:802` (`transcribe_collisional_starburst_recipe`); those of `oracle_c05.o` are unchanged. The package writes `M:802` as `fma(ColdGas, 0.74, -H2gas)` (`shared/sage_starburst_physics.h`).

| Site | Separating fixture | Fused (the fixture) | Unfused (`-ffp-contract=off` experiment build) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `M:802`, `0x10b8` | `burst_r2_rounding_h1_refresh_fused` | `H1gas` `0x31f5c28f` (`burst_h1_refresh` `0x3e3eb851eaa7b6e0`) | `0x31f5c290` (`0x3e3eb851f0000000`) |

A zero-star burst moves no gas, so only the refresh's fused site can separate there: `ColdGas` `0x3facf921` and `H2gas` its float bound, which rounded down below the double budget, leave a remainder of a few ulps of the budget whose rounding the fusion decides.

## The c09 Group: FIRE Supernova Feedback on the Disk Path

c09 covers what SAGE26's `FIREmodeOn 1` switches on in the disk star-formation path's `compute_sn_feedback` (`S:732-889`, static): the Muratov et al. (2015) velocity and redshift scaling (`S:743-753`), the capped reheating loading and its `MassLoading` record (`S:759-765` with `capped_eta_reheat`, `X:141-149`), and the Hirschmann et al. (2016) energy-based ejection (`S:783-792` with `sn_energy_coupling`, `X:118-124`); `X` is `R/src/model_misc.h`, where both helpers are header-inline, and `MAX_SN_ENERGY_COUPLING` is 2.0 (`X:31`). The burst's FIRE budget (`R/src/model_mergers.c:690-752`) is c09's second slice ("The Burst's FIRE Budget" below). The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R3.par`: `FIREmodeOn 1` (`R3.par:55`) and every other flag and parameter `R2.par`'s (`SFprescription 1`, `BulgeSizeOn 3`, `FeedbackReheatingEpsilon 2.9`, `FeedbackEjectionEfficiency 0.3`, `R3.par:48`, `:109`, `:69-70`).

The oracle code is additions to **`oracle_c05.c`**: no new main, and `build.sh` is unchanged. A new routine, `starformation_and_feedback_fire` (added to `regenerate.sh`'s `C05_ROUTINES`), is SAGE26's public `starformation_and_feedback` with `FIREmodeOn` 0 or 1, recomposed and checked as c05's `starformation_and_feedback` is ("The c05 Group"). The routine `starformation_and_feedback` and every other c05 routine still require `FIREmodeOn 0`, so all 637 earlier fixtures are read and written byte for byte as before. The fixtures of the disk FIRE path live with `sage26_calculate_supernova_feedback`, whose oracle test runs the disk chain and asserts the feedback module's boundary; the compositions the chained test of `sage26_calculate_star_formation` consumes live with that module.

### Flags and Parameters

The configuration reader for the new routine reads `params.FIREmodeOn` as 0 or 1 and refuses any other value, naming the parameter (`read_fire_mode_on`); every other flag is read as for `starformation_and_feedback`. It also reads every galaxy's stored `galaxy[<i>].SnapNum` (`i32`) and, for each distinct one, `params.ZZ[<s>]` (`f64`), explicitly (`read_fire_snapshots`). Every other `ZZ` entry stays poisoned, so the poisoned state never supplies a redshift. `compute_sn_feedback` reads `ZZ[galaxies[p].SnapNum]` (`S:745`): the snapshot the galaxy carries, not the current halo's. The reader refuses a `SnapNum` outside `[0, ABSOLUTEMAXSNAPS)`, an index SAGE26 would read out of bounds, so no fixture holds one. The package refuses an index outside its redshift table instead (campaign decision D35, `shared/sage_fire_feedback_physics.h`). A `FIREmodeOn 0` fixture of this routine reads the same fields as its `FIREmodeOn 1` twin; SAGE26 then reads neither the snapshot nor the redshift.

Every galaxy also reads and records the float `MassLoading` (`FIRE_MEMBERS`, after the c08 `H2DepletionTime_Gyr`). The input value is a deliberately stale one: the R3 row's own stored value, or the template row's for a constructed fixture. The FIRE path overwrites it before the renormalisation, also when no stars form (`S:762-764`), and `FIREmodeOn 0` leaves it as it was. The gas-unstable continuation is refused for this routine: its burst belongs to c09's second slice, so every fixture is a disk that is stable after star formation.

### Routines

| Owning module | Routine (`routine` line) | What runs | Recorded |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sage26_calculate_supernova_feedback` | `starformation_and_feedback_fire` | SAGE26's wrapper (`S:995-1110`) with R3's flags, composed and checked as c05's; `compute_sn_feedback`'s FIRE path transcribed (`S:739-792`), with SAGE26's own `capped_eta_reheat` and `sn_energy_coupling` or their transcriptions (`transcribe_capped_eta_reheat`, `transcribe_sn_energy_coupling`) | as `starformation_and_feedback` (the c05 and c08 records), `MassLoading` on every galaxy, then the c09 flags and values below |
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | `starformation_and_feedback_fire` | the same | the same |

### Transcriptions, Check Kinds and Two-Sided Validation

`compose_wrapper`'s step 4 transcribes `compute_sn_feedback` with `FIREmodeOn 1`, each statement cited to its line. The scaling reads the forming galaxy's float `Vvir` widened to double and the redshift of its stored snapshot (`S:745-752`). The loading is `FeedbackReheatingEpsilon * fire_scaling`, passed to `capped_eta_reheat` with the unfloored velocity; it is stored as the float `MassLoading` and multiplied by the stars (`S:763-765`). The renormalisation is c05's. For a positive velocity, the ejection is `E_FB = coupling * 0.5 * stars * (EtaSNcode * EnergySNcode)` and `E_lift = 0.5 * reheated_mass * vc * vc`, and the ejected mass is their difference over `0.5 * vc * vc` when `E_FB > E_lift` (`S:787-792`). Two compositions must each reproduce SAGE26's end state, `MassLoading` included, or the oracle refuses the fixture: (a) every routine transcribed, the helpers included; and (b) SAGE26's own `update_from_star_formation`, `update_from_feedback`, `check_disk_instability`, `capped_eta_reheat` and `sn_energy_coupling`, fed the transcribed `compute_sn_feedback`'s values.

| Value | Line | Check kind |
|---|---|---|
| `fire_redshift` (`z_fire`, `ZZ[SnapNum]`), `fire_v_term`, `eta`, `coupling`, `E_FB`, `E_lift` | `S:745`, `:749-751`, `:762`, `X:121-124`, `S:789-791` | tail |
| `fire_scaling` (passed to `sn_energy_coupling`, `S:789`), `eta_raw` (the argument of `capped_eta_reheat`, `S:763`) | `S:743-753`, `:763` | continuation |
| `eta_max` (`capped_eta_reheat`'s local), `eps_eff` (`sn_energy_coupling`'s local) | `X:147-148`, `X:120` | tail |

The pre- and post-renormalisation stars and reheated mass and the ejection before and after its floor are c05's carried values (`stars_pre_renorm`, `reheated_mass_pre_renorm`, `renorm_fac`, `stars`, `reheated_mass`, `ejected_mass_pre_floor`, `ejected_mass`), recorded as before. Each c09 value is written, after every earlier record, with c06's markers (`_absent`, the `f64` value, `_checked`, `_check_kind`, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`) and c07's `_narrowing_limited`. The flags are `intermediate.fire_mode` and `fire_scaling_valid` (`S:747`), `fire_vc_floored` (`S:748`), `fire_high_velocity` (`S:749`), `eta_cap_vvir_nonpositive` (`X:144`), `eta_cap_binds` (`X:149`), `coupling_capped` (`X:121`) and `fire_energy_ejects` (`S:792`), each -1 where the path does not evaluate it. Where a cap binds, the capped value equals its bound: `eta` equals `eta_max`, and `coupling` equals 2.0.

**The narrowing limitation.** On a call with no stars (`stars_pre_renorm` 0), the reheated mass and `E_FB` are 0 whatever the loading and the scaling. `fire_redshift`, `fire_v_term`, `fire_scaling`, `eta_raw`, `eta_max` and `eta` then reach the state only through `MassLoading`, one double-to-float narrowing, which cannot make both neighbouring doubles differ from the stored float. They are therefore `_narrowing_limited 1` on such a fixture, `fire_zero_stars_mass_loading` and the zero-velocity probes. They are recorded by name and never claimed as validated two-sided; their two-sided test is still run and recorded. On every other fixture the loading also reaches the reheated mass, so `_narrowing_limited` is 0. Most values are nonetheless not validated two-sided, for c05's reason: the float stores absorb a one-ulp change. `fire_probe_validate_residual` is built so that two stores keep a double's exact residual. The satellite's `OutflowRate` holds `-(float)` of its reheated mass and the central's `EjectedMass` holds `-(float)` of the ejection, at an interval found by a scan where each residual lies below `2^24` ulps of its double. On it the redshift, the velocity term, the raw and capped loadings, `E_FB`, the reheated mass and the ejection are validated two-sided. When both caps bind, `E_FB` and `E_lift` differ only by rounding, which makes the ejection sensitive to every value upstream (`fire_energy_above_caps_bind`). The recorded c09 values are transcription-derived; `galaxy[*]`, `MassLoading` included, are SAGE26's own, and the two are kept apart.

### Fused Multiply-Adds and the Simplified pow

Read with `objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces (`model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, whose machine code is the reference build's), scalar and vector. `compute_sn_feedback` is inlined into `starformation_and_feedback`, with `capped_eta_reheat` and `sn_energy_coupling` inlined into it.

| Object, function | Line | Offset | Instruction | Expression | In the oracle | In the package |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `starformation_and_feedback` | `S:743-792` (the scaling, the cap, the coupling and the two energies), `X:118-124`, `X:141-149` | `0x858`-`0x9fc` | **none fused**: `fmul`, `fdiv`, `fadd`, `fsub`, `fcsel` and two `bl _pow` | every FIRE expression; `E_FB`, `E_lift` and their difference keep separate roundings (`fmul` at `0x9c0`-`0x9e0`, `fsub` at `0x9ec`, `fdiv` at `0x9f8`); the cap's `2.0 * esn` is an `fadd d3, d3, d3` (`0x93c`, exact) | transcribed, unfused (the fused instructions of `oracle_c05.o` are still exactly c05's seven sites and none in the c09 transcription; c10 adds four, "The c10 Group, Part 3") | one C expression per SAGE26 statement (`shared/sage_fire_feedback_physics.h`) |
| same | `S:750` | `0x8cc` | `bl _pow` | `pow(vc_floored / 60.0, -3.2)` | transcribed, `pow` | `pow` (never `powf`) |
| same | `S:752` | `0x90c` | `bl _pow` | `pow(1.0 + z_fire, 1.25)` | transcribed, `pow` | `pow` |
| same | `S:751` | `0x8fc` | `fdiv d11, d1, d0` (**simplified**) | `pow(vc_floored / 60.0, -1.0)`, which clang compiles as `1.0 / x` | transcribed as the source writes it, compiled to the same `fdiv` (`oracle_c05.o` offset `0x7304`) | the division `1.0 / (vc_floored / FIRE_V_CRIT_KMS)` |

No c09 site fuses, so no c09 fixture separates a fused from an unfused result. An experiment build with `-ffp-contract=off` added to the flags for every object and main (the validation record, never written as a fixture) changes no c09 value and no state field on any of the 33 c09 fixtures; it changes only `intermediate.atomicH`, c08's fused `S:1036` site, on 26 of them.
