**The simplified site.** `S:751` is a call of `pow` in the source but a division in the object: clang replaces `pow(x, -1.0)` by `1.0 / x`. macOS `pow(x, -1.0)` is not always `1.0 / x`. An exhaustive check over every float `Vvir` from 60 to +inf, with `x = (double)Vvir / 60.0`, finds 1352565 of 1024458753 where the two differ in the last bit (the validation record). Mimic's `-O2` build makes the same simplification, but the unit tests' `-O0` build calls `pow`. The package therefore writes the division, which is correctly rounded at every optimisation level, as SAGE26's object computes it (a decision of the campaign's PM, recorded in this slice's validation record). `fire_pow_minus_one_division` separates the two forms: its `Vvir` `0x1.e00168p+5` (60.0011 km/s) at redshift 0 gives `fire_v_term` `0x3fefffe80011fff3` by division and `0x3fefffe80011fff2` by macOS `pow`, and the SN module's unit test `test_fire_high_velocity_division` pins the division on any platform.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R3-reachable** (7) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (26). `fire_r3_snap<N>_*` names a fixture whose galaxies take a real galaxy's values from the R3 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R3/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `ab278992616f7137b526e5bd3a43045f2e753e29d84997fc69350255c38f570c`) at snapshot 12, 32 or 63: end-of-snapshot values used as plausible call inputs, not reconstructed call-time state, with the stored `SnapNum` the output snapshot and its redshift SAGE26's `1/AA - 1`. Every constructed fixture starts from the R3 group at snapshot 32 (central row 4, satellite row 5) and changes only the fields its comment names. c09 adds 33 fixtures through `oracle_c05`:

| Owning module | Fixtures |
|---|---|
| `sage26_calculate_supernova_feedback` | 23 probes: the velocity below, at and above 1 km/s and 60 km/s, zero and negative (`fire_vvir_*`), and below 1 km/s at redshift 0, where the floor decides the loading below its cap (`fire_vvir_below_1_redshift_zero`); the redshift 0, high (14.09), negative and `-0.0` (valid, `S:747`) (`fire_redshift_*`); the simplified `pow` (`fire_pow_minus_one_division`); `E_FB` equal to (`fire_energy_equal_caps_bind`, both caps binding, exact), above by rounding alone (`fire_energy_above_caps_bind`) and below `E_lift` (`fire_energy_below`, `fire_vvir_below_1`); the loading cap binding at high redshift while the coupling does not, so the reheating consumes the coupled energy and nothing is ejected (`fire_eta_cap_binds_high_z`); `eta_raw` exactly at `eta_max` and the coupling exactly at 2 (`fire_eta_at_cap`, `fire_coupling_at_cap`, redshifts found by scans of the doubles near the analytic roots); the coupling capped with the renormalisation binding (`fire_coupling_capped_renorm`); a zero-star call writing `MassLoading` (`fire_zero_stars_mass_loading`); the two-sided validation probe (`fire_probe_validate_residual`) |
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | 10: R3-derived satellites and centrals at snapshots 32 and 63 (`fire_r3_snap32_*`, `fire_r3_snap63_*`), the `FIREmodeOn 0` twin of the snapshot-63 satellite (`fire0_r3_snap63_satellite`), an R3 central at snapshot 12 whose loading cap binds (`fire_r3_snap12_central_eta_cap`) and one at snapshot 63 with `Vvir` below 33 km/s whose coupling is capped (`fire_r3_snap63_low_vvir_central`), all R3-reachable; the host/central distinction, with the satellite's own velocity and stored snapshot (31) driving the scaling and the central's (32) not, and the reheated gas and the ejection cap on the central's hot gas, in both modes (`fire_host_central_differ`, `fire0_host_central_differ`); and R2's post-SF and post-reheating hydrogen caps binding with `FIREmodeOn 1` (`fire_h2_caps_fb_bind`) |

The largest c09 output has 489 fields and the largest input 214 (a fixture whose two galaxies carry different stored snapshots, so two `params.ZZ` entries), counted as the reader counts them (every typed statement after the format and routine lines), within `oracle_c05`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048), so no limit changed. Not constructed: a negative FIRE ejection, since the floor (`S:880-882`) is unreachable on the FIRE path (the ejection is `+0.0` or a positive quotient); a `capped_eta_reheat` call with a non-positive velocity and a non-zero raw loading, since the scaling is 0 there, so the guard (`X:144`) returns the same `+0.0` the cap would; a NaN velocity or redshift; and an out-of-range `SnapNum`, which the package refuses (D35). A mutation sweep of the c09 transcriptions (the validation record) runs every mutant on the 33 fixtures. `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c09 outputs together with every earlier group's.

### The Burst's FIRE Budget

c09's second slice covers what `FIREmodeOn 1` switches on in every burst: `collisional_starburst_recipe`'s FIRE budget (`M:690-752`, `M` is `R/src/model_mergers.c`), reached directly, through `deal_with_galaxy_merger` (chained mergers included, through the pass), through the instability's own burst (`check_disk_instability`, `D:106-108`, `D` is `R/src/model_disk_instability.c`) and through the post-minor-merger recheck (`M:806-811`). The budget is the disk path's with three differences, each transcribed: the scaling reads the **target's** (`merger_centralgal`'s) float `Vvir` and stored `SnapNum` (`M:694-695`), never the satellite's or the FoF central's; the capped loading is multiplied by the stars with **no** `MassLoading` write (`M:707-710`); and the renormalisation guard is `stars + reheated > gas_for_starburst` with no positive-sum clause (`M:721`). The rung flags are `R3.par`'s, as for the disk path.

The oracle code is additions to **`oracle_c06.c`** and **`oracle_c05.c`**: no new main, `build.sh` unchanged, and `regenerate.sh` gains the new routine names in `C06_ROUTINES` and `C05_ROUTINES` (and `:?` guards on its two `rm` of variable paths). Every addition is reached only by the new routine names, and every record is written after every earlier field, so `regenerate.sh` reproduces all 670 earlier fixtures byte for byte.

| Owning module | Routine (`routine` line) | What runs |
|---|---|---|
| `sage26_starburst_feedback` | `collisional_starburst_recipe_fire` | c06's `collisional_starburst_recipe` with `FIREmodeOn` 0 or 1 |
| `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption` | `deal_with_galaxy_merger_fire` | c06's `deal_with_galaxy_merger` with `FIREmodeOn` 0 or 1 |
| `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption` | `merger_pass_fire` | c06's `merger_pass` transcription with `FIREmodeOn` 0 or 1, SAGE26's own `deal_with_galaxy_merger` in it |
| `sage26_disk_instability` | `check_disk_instability_fire` | c05's `check_disk_instability` with `FIREmodeOn` 0 or 1, its continuation SAGE26's own `grow_black_hole` and `collisional_starburst_recipe` |
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | `starformation_and_feedback_fire` | Slice 1's routine, now admitting the gas-unstable continuation (SAGE26's wrapper `S:1088`, its burst SAGE26's own) |

**Flags and input.** `oracle_c06.c`'s reader reads `params.FIREmodeOn` 0 or 1 for the `*_fire` routines (`read_fire_mode_on`, refusing any other value by name; every other c06 routine still requires 0) and, after the galaxies, `params.ZZ[<s>]` once for each distinct stored `galaxy[<i>].SnapNum` (`read_fire_snapshots`, refusing an index outside `[0, ABSOLUTEMAXSNAPS)`); every other `ZZ` entry stays poisoned. `oracle_c05.c`'s `check_disk_instability_fire` reads them as Slice 1's wrapper does. Every galaxy also reads and records the float `MassLoading` (c06's `BLOCK_FIRE`, after its other blocks; c05's `FIRE_MEMBERS`), a deliberately stale input value that no burst writes; a `collisional_starburst_recipe_fire` fixture records every galaxy, an uninvolved satellite included, so its `MassLoading` and reservoirs are asserted unchanged. A `FIREmodeOn 0` twin reads the same input.

**Transcription and check kinds.** `transcribe_collisional_starburst_recipe` runs `M:690-752` with `FIREmodeOn 1`, each statement cited: the scaling (`M:692-703`), the loading `FeedbackReheatingEpsilon * fire_scaling` passed to SAGE26's own `capped_eta_reheat` or its transcription and multiplied by the stars (`M:706-710`), c06's renormalisation (`M:721-725`) and, for a positive target `Vvir`, the coupling from SAGE26's own `sn_energy_coupling` or its transcription, `E_FB`, `E_lift` and the ejection (`M:728-736`), then c06's floor. `transcribe_capped_eta_reheat` and `transcribe_sn_energy_coupling` restate `X:141-149` and `X:118-124` (`X` is `R/src/model_misc.h`), as `oracle_c05.c`'s do. The values and flags are recorded with Slice 1's names (`intermediate.fire_mode`, the seven flags, the ten values), so the package tests share one checker. Check kinds as for the disk path: `fire_scaling` and `eta_raw` continuation-checked (passed to SAGE26's helpers), the rest tail-checked; `eta_max` and `eps_eff` are validated in the composition that transcribes the helpers (`STAGE_FIRE_HELPER`), the others in the burst's. Every composition must reproduce SAGE26's end state, `MassLoading` included, or the fixture is refused. Each value's `_narrowing_limited` is 0: a burst never writes `MassLoading`, so the values reach the state through the reheated and ejected masses and the stores they feed, or, on a zero-star burst, not at all (recorded and checked, validated in neither direction there). `burst_fire_probe_validate_residual` keeps the residuals of two stores (the host's `OutflowRate` holds `-(float)` of its reheated mass and its `EjectedMass` `-(float)` of the ejection, at a cold gas found by a scan where each residual lies within `2^23` ulps of its double), so the redshift, the velocity term, the scaling, the raw and capped loading, `eps_eff`, the coupling and `E_FB` are validated two-sided there. `burst_fire_probe_validate_caps_bind` does the same with both caps binding and `E_FB` a rounding step above `E_lift` (a fraction of 0.01, so no renormalisation absorbs the loading, at a cold gas found by a scan where either neighbouring double of the loading changes the reheated mass), so `eta_max` (through the binding loading) and `E_lift` (through an ejection of a few ulps of `E_FB`) are validated two-sided there: every c09 value of the burst is validated on at least one fixture.

**Observations of SAGE26's nested bursts.** The recheck's burst runs inside SAGE26's own `check_disk_instability`, and the instability's and the wrapper's continuation bursts are SAGE26's own `collisional_starburst_recipe`; no transcription reaches their locals. Each is observed by counterfactuals: SAGE26's routine runs again on a copy of the state it received with a copy of the run parameters changed in one member, `FIREmodeOn 0` (`*_fire_mode_sensitive`) or the redshift of the bursting galaxy's stored snapshot replaced by itself plus 1.0 (`*_fire_redshift_sensitive`), and the result is compared with SAGE26's end state. Within these routines only the burst reads `FIREmodeOn` and `ZZ` (`M:693-694`, `:707`, `:730`), so a 1 shows that the nested burst took the FIRE branch and read its galaxy's stored-snapshot redshift. The records are `intermediate.burst_recheck_fire_*` (the recheck, on `collisional_starburst_recipe_fire` and `deal_with_galaxy_merger_fire`, -1 where the recheck did not run or `FIREmodeOn` is 0), `intermediate.nested_burst_fire_*` (the instability's and the gas-unstable wrapper's burst; on the wrapper written only where the gas branch ran, so Slice 1's stable fixtures are unchanged) and `intermediate.pass_fire_*` (a `merger_pass_fire` pass, re-run with `FIREmodeOn 0` or with each merger target's redshift replaced). They are observations of SAGE26, not port values: the package tests assert the nested bursts' wiring from their own replay ("Package tests" below) and count these.

**Fused multiply-adds and the simplified pow.** Read with `objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces (`model_mergers.o`, whose machine code is the reference build's), scalar and vector, at `collisional_starburst_recipe`:

| Object, function | Line | Offset | Instruction | Expression | In the oracle | In the package |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_mergers.o`, `collisional_starburst_recipe` | `M:692-752` (the scaling, the cap, the coupling and the two energies), `X:118-124`, `X:141-149` | `0xd14`-`0xf18` | **none fused**: `fmul`, `fdiv`, `fadd`, `fsub`, `fcsel`, `fcvt` and two `bl _pow` | every FIRE expression; `E_FB` (`fmul` at `0xea0`-`0xeb4`), `E_lift` (`0xeb8`-`0xec0`) and their difference (`fsub` at `0xecc`, `fdiv` at `0xed8`) keep separate roundings; the cap's `2.0 * esn` is `fadd d3, d3, d3` (`0xe0c`, exact) | transcribed, unfused (the fused instructions of `oracle_c06.o` are still exactly c06's twelve sites and c08's `M:802`, none in the c09 transcription) | one C expression per SAGE26 statement (`shared/sage_fire_feedback_physics.h`) |
| same | `M:699` | `0xd9c` | `bl _pow` | `pow(vc_floored / 60.0, -3.2)` | transcribed, `pow` | `pow` (never `powf`) |
| same | `M:701` | `0xdd8` | `bl _pow` | `pow(1.0 + z_fire, 1.25)` | transcribed, `pow` | `pow` |
| same | `M:700` | `0xdc8` | `fdiv d13, d1, d0` (**simplified**) | `pow(vc_floored / 60.0, -1.0)`, compiled as `1.0 / x` | transcribed as the source writes it, compiled to the same `fdiv` (`oracle_c06.o` offset `0x7874`) | the division `1.0 / (vc_floored / FIRE_V_CRIT_KMS)`, Slice 1's helper |

No burst FIRE site fuses, so no fixture separates a fused from an unfused result. An experiment build with `-ffp-contract=off` added to the flags for every object and main (the validation record, never written as a fixture) changes no c09 value and no `MassLoading` on any of the 41 fixtures it was run on (all below but the later `burst_fire_probe_validate_caps_bind`); it changes only fields of the known fused sites: `intermediate.atomicH` (`S:1036`) on the three wrapper fixtures, `intermediate.burst_h1_refresh` (`M:802`) on six bursts and the remnant radius's locals (`M:158`, `:171`, `:204`) on `merge_fire0_target_differs`. The object reads the target's float `Vvir` and tests it (`0xd44`-`0xd50`) before it loads `ZZ[SnapNum]` (`0xd54`-`0xd5c`), so with a non-positive `Vvir` it reads no redshift; the source reads it first (`M:694`). Both give the same result for every in-range `SnapNum`; for one outside the table the package refuses (campaign decision D35) whatever the velocity.

**Fixtures.** Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R3-reachable** (10) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (32). `*_r3_snap32_*` and `*_r3_snap63_*` take real galaxies' values from the R3 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R3/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `ab278992616f7137b526e5bd3a43045f2e753e29d84997fc69350255c38f570c`; every public routine at both snapshots), with the stored `SnapNum` the output snapshot and `ZZ[SnapNum] = 1/AA - 1`. The inputs were written by a tool from c08's R2 inputs and c06's layouts (the validation record); the exact ties came from scans of the doubles near the analytic roots (`eta_raw == eta_max` at 200 km/s, `eps_eff == 2` at 100 km/s) and of the cold gas (`E_FB` equal to, above and below `E_lift` by rounding alone with both caps binding), and the nested bursts' inputs were tuned (a target's `Vmax`, the chained satellites' masses, the R3 row and its cold-gas factor) until the observations showed each nested burst taking the FIRE branch: the quasar wind of a large unstable fraction otherwise blows out the cold gas first, and the nested burst forms no stars. c09's second slice adds 42 fixtures:

| Owning module | Fixtures |
|---|---|
| `sage26_starburst_feedback` | 29 (`burst_fire_*`): R3 centrals at snapshots 32 and 63 in an instability burst and R3 satellites as a minor merger's host in their real groups (`burst_fire_r3_*`, 4); a Type 1 host whose FoF central and merging satellite differ from it in `Vvir` and stored `SnapNum`, its recheck's burst observed FIRE- and redshift-sensitive, and its `FIREmodeOn 0` twin (`burst_fire_host_central_satellite_differ`, `burst_fire0_*`); the host's `Vvir` below, at and above 1 and 60 km/s (60 takes the division), zero and negative; the stored-snapshot redshift 0, high (14.09), negative and `-0.0`; the loading cap binding while the coupling does not, so nothing is ejected (`burst_fire_eta_cap_binds`, `ZZ[12]`); `eta_raw` exactly at `eta_max` and the coupling exactly at 2; the coupling capped with the burst renormalisation binding; both caps binding with `E_FB` equal to, above and below `E_lift` by rounding; no cap binding with an ejection; a zero-star burst; the two two-sided validation probes |
| `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption` | 6: R3 satellites merging into their real centrals at snapshots 32 and 63 (`merge_fire_r3_*`, major mergers); `merge_target_not_fof_central`'s layout with the target, the satellite and the FoF central differing in `Vvir` and stored `SnapNum`, its recheck's burst observed, and its `FIREmodeOn 0` twin (`merge_fire_target_differs`, `merge_fire0_target_differs`); a merger whose recheck's burst is observed (`merge_fire_recheck_unstable`); two chained mergers onto the same target in one bin, both satellites' stored snapshot differing from the target's (`tr_pass_fire_two_mergers_one_bin`) |
| `sage26_disk_instability` | 4: R3 centrals at snapshots 32 and 63, gas-unstable with their cold gas raised (`di_fire_r3_*_central_unstable`); a gas-unstable satellite whose FoF central differs from it in `Vvir` and stored `SnapNum`, and its `FIREmodeOn 0` twin (`di_fire_satellite_central_differ`, `di_fire0_*`) |
| `sage26_calculate_star_formation` | 3: R3 centrals at snapshots 32 and 63 whose disk is gas-unstable after the FIRE feedback (`sf_fire_r3_*_central_unstable`), and a forming satellite whose FoF central differs from it (`sf_fire_satellite_unstable_central_differ`): the disk path writes `MassLoading` and the continuation's burst leaves it |

The largest output has 741 fields (`merge_fire_target_differs`) and the largest input 347, within the mains' `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048), so no limit changed. Not constructed: a negative FIRE ejection (the floor, `M:746-748`, is unreachable on the FIRE path: the ejection is `+0.0` or a positive quotient); a `capped_eta_reheat` call with a non-positive velocity and a non-zero raw loading (the scaling is 0 there, `M:696`, so the guard `X:144` returns the same `+0.0` the cap would); the positive-sum clause's absence as a separate case, which no input can show: the burst gas is floored at zero (`M:683`), so `stars + reheated > gas_for_starburst` implies a positive sum, and a `-0.0` cold gas gives `-0.0 > -0.0`, false either way; a NaN velocity or redshift; and an out-of-range `SnapNum`, which the package refuses (D35). A mutation sweep of the extended transcriptions and observations (the validation record) runs every mutant on these fixtures. `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c09 outputs together with every earlier group's.

**Package tests.** The oracle tests of the four owning modules dispatch the new routines. Each gives the burst module the fixture's `FIREmodeOn` and redshift table, asserts every recorded galaxy's `MassLoading` equal to its input, and asserts each burst path's FIRE wiring: the burst read its host's own stored `SnapNum`, float `Vvir` and that snapshot's redshift (`sage26_oracle_fire.h`, `oracle_c09_check_burst_wiring`). The direct burst asserts it from the transaction's record, with the burst's c09 values against the oracle's; the merger, instability and wrapper-continuation paths run the package's modules and replay the burst consumer's call of the shared transaction on copies, which must end in `process()`'s state (the c08 pattern); and the recheck's burst, where the oracle observes it taking the FIRE branch, asserts the same wiring and the host's path-invariant scaling, loadings and coupling from the transaction's `recheck_fire` record.

## The c10 Group: The CGM Regime Classifier

c10 covers what SAGE26's `CGMrecipeOn 1` switches on. Its first part is the classifier, `determine_and_store_regime` (`G` = `R/src/model_regimes.c`, `:63-100`; declared public at `R/src/model_regimes.h:19-20`), which SAGE26's driver calls once per FoF group between progenitor joining and `infall_recipe` (`R/src/core_build_model.c:407-415`). The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R4.par`: `CGMrecipeOn 1` (`R4.par:54`; every other line as `R3.par`), `Hubble_h 0.73` (`:40`), and SAGE26's default `MShockMsun` 6.0e11 (`R/src/core_read_parameter_file.c:114`), which `R4.par` does not set. The package's classifier is `sage26_determine_regime`.

### Flags and Parameters

The routine reads `params.MShockMsun` and `params.Hubble_h`, each fixture input field of those names; `CGMrecipeOn` is the caller's gate (`R/src/core_build_model.c:410`), not read by the routine, so no fixture carries it: every c10 fixture is a `CGMrecipeOn 1` call. Per galaxy the routine reads `mergeType` and the float `Mvir` and writes `Regime`; the fixtures also carry `Type` (for the Mimic side, where a skipped row is a `Type` 3 row) and the input `Regime` (to show a skipped row keeps it and a classified row's is overwritten, never reused).

### The Test-Only `rand()` Provider

SAGE26 draws its deviate as `(double)rand() / (double)RAND_MAX` (`G:94`). `oracle_c10.c` defines `int rand(void)` itself; a definition in a linked object takes precedence over the C library's for the SAGE26 objects' calls, so in `output/oracle/oracle_c10`, and only there, SAGE26's `rand()` returns the fixture's queue (`rand.queue_length`, `rand.queue[k]`, each a value in `0..RAND_MAX`) in order. It refuses a call outside a SAGE26 call or past the queue's end, and the oracle refuses a fixture whose queue SAGE26 does not consume exactly, so a fixture's queue states exactly its draws. SAGE26's source is not edited. `nm -m` shows `_rand` defined in `oracle_c10`'s own `__TEXT,__text` and undefined (from `libSystem`) in every other oracle main; Mimic's binaries define and call no `rand`. `RAND_MAX` on the oracle platform is 2147483647, recorded in every output as `intermediate.RAND_MAX`.

Which rows draw is observed from SAGE26's own code: after the call over all rows, the oracle calls the routine once per row (`ngal` 1, the row's own address) on a fresh copy of the input with the queue rewound, and records which queue entry each call consumed (`intermediate.galaxy[i].draw_index`, -1 for none). The loop body shares no state between rows other than the `rand()` stream, so the per-row calls must reproduce the full call's `Regime` for every row and consume the queue in the same order; the oracle refuses the fixture otherwise. It also refuses a fixture unless every byte of every galaxy other than `Regime` is unchanged by SAGE26's call: classification moves no gas.

### Transcription and Check Kind

The routine's locals cannot be read from SAGE26's call, so `Mshock` (`G:71`), `mass_ratio` (`:74`), `x` (`:85`), `hot_fraction` (`:89`) and the deviate (`:94`) come from a source-reviewed transcription (`transcribe_determine_and_store_regime`), each statement cited, which takes its deviates from the same queue with SAGE26's expression applied to the queue value and never calls `rand()`. The oracle refuses a fixture unless the transcription reproduces SAGE26's `Regime` for every row with the same rows drawing the same queue entries, so every recorded local is **tail-checked**: the transcription runs from it to the routine's end and reproduces SAGE26's result. Two-sided validation (D25) is not meaningful here: the routine's only output is the integer `Regime`, which an ulp of a local changes only at a tie of the deviate and `hot_fraction`; instead the package's oracle test asserts each local bit for bit itself, alongside the comparison's outcome (`deviate_below_hot_fraction`, `deviate_equals_hot_fraction`).

Per row the output records `galaxy[i].Regime` (SAGE26's call), `intermediate.galaxy[i].skipped` (`mergeType > 0`, `G:67`), `drew`, `draw_index`, and for a classified row `Mshock` and `mass_ratio`, for a drawing row `x`, `hot_fraction`, `deviate` and the two comparison flags; and `intermediate.draws_consumed`. A NaN row's `x` and `hot_fraction` are the transcription's NaN bit patterns: SAGE26's object divides `log10(mass_ratio)` by `-0.1` instead of negating `x` (below), which carries a NaN of the other sign into `exp`, and no output can observe a NaN's sign here, since only the ordered comparison reads it (false either way). For a finite `x` the two forms are equal bit for bit: round-to-nearest division is sign-symmetric.

A mutation sweep of the transcription (the validation record) compiled eleven mutants with the oracle's flags: the zero-ratio test `<=` to `<`, `delta_log_M` 0.2, the sigmoid's sign, the comparison `<` to `<=`, the shock-mass conversion inverted and without `Hubble_h`, the skip test `>= 0` and dropped, the deviate divided by `RAND_MAX + 1`, the queue read backwards, and a doubled ratio. Each was refused by at least one fixture.

### Fused Multiply-Adds

None. No expression of `G:63-100` has a multiply feeding an add or subtract, and SAGE26's object has no fused instruction in the routine: `llvm-objdump -d -l` of `model_regimes.o` (the `build.sh` object, whose machine code equals the reference build's) shows, for `determine_and_store_regime` (`0x0`-`0xec`), only `fmul` and `fdiv` for `Mshock` (`G:71`, `0xb0`, `0xb4`), `fcvt` and `fdiv` for the ratio (`:74`, `0xbc`, `0xc0`), `fcmp` and `b.hi` (greater or unordered, so a NaN ratio draws) for the zero test (`:77`, `0xc4`, `0xc8`), the `log10` call and `fdiv` by `-0.1` (`:85-89`, `0x64`, `0x68`), the `exp` call, `fadd` and `fdiv` (`:89`, `0x6c`-`0x74`), the `rand` call, `scvtf` and `fdiv` by 2147483647.0 (`:94`, `0x78`-`0x80`), and `fcmp` with `cset mi` (ordered less than) (`:95`, `0x84`, `0x88`); no `fmadd`, `fmsub`, `fnmadd`, `fnmsub`, `fmla` or `fmls`. The package's object has none either. The division by `-0.1` is the form SAGE26's object computes for `exp(-x)`; it equals the source form bit for bit for every finite `x`, so the package writes the source form.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

The nine fixtures belong to `sage26_determine_regime`. Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R4-reachable** (2) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (7). `r4_snap32_rows` and `r4_snap63_rows` take the `Type` and `Mvir` of five galaxies (a central above, near and below the shock mass, a satellite, and a central with `Mvir` 0) from the R4 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R4/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `2b606ee7ac82559e32c7c91161f1edb44f89288ec57016e80da884e118ed0807`) at snapshot 32 and 63: end-of-snapshot values used as plausible call inputs, with deviates chosen either side of each row's `hot_fraction`, not SAGE26's libc trajectory.

| Fixture | Cases |
|---|---|
| `ratio_above_at_below_deviate_below`, `_above` | Centrals at mass ratio 2, at the float nearest `Mshock` (no float equals the double 43.8) and at 1/2, each with the `rand()` value whose deviate is the nearest below (Regime 1) or above (Regime 0) its `hot_fraction` |
| `ratio_one_exact_h05` | The exact ratio 1 (`Hubble_h` 0.5 makes `Mshock` 30 exactly; `Mvir` 30): `x` 0 and `hot_fraction` 0.5, with the two nearest deviates either side |
| `deviate_equals_hot_fraction` | The tie: a float `Mvir` (`0x4258c31c`) whose `hot_fraction` is exactly 1537505481 / `RAND_MAX`, found by a scratch search over every float from `Mshock` to 1.5 `Mshock`; the tie gives Regime 0 (`G:95` is strict), the neighbouring `rand()` values 1 and 0 |
| `mvir_zero_negative_nan` | `Mvir` +0, -0 and -1 (no draw, Regime 0; a classified row's input Regime 1 overwritten), NaN (draws queue entry 0, Regime 0 even with the deviate 0), then a row above `Mshock` drawing entry 1 |
| `mvir_infinite_deviate_one` | `Mvir` +inf: `hot_fraction` exactly 1, so the deviate `RAND_MAX / RAND_MAX` = 1 ties (Regime 0) and the next lower gives 1 |
| `every_type_orphan_skipped` | A `Type` 0 central, a `Type` 1 satellite and a `Type` 2 orphan (`Mvir` 0, as SAGE26 sets at joining, `R/src/core_build_model.c:324-325`) classified; three `Type` 3 rows with `mergeType` 1, 2 and 4 skipped (`G:67`), keeping their input Regime even with `Mvir` above `Mshock` |
| `r4_snap32_rows`, `r4_snap63_rows` | R4-reachable rows, above |

Every branch of the routine is covered: the skip (`G:67`), the no-draw branch (`:77-78`) for +0, -0, negative and orphan `Mvir`, the draw branch (`:79-95`) for finite, NaN and infinite ratios, and both outcomes of the comparison (`:95`) plus its two ties. The exact ratio-1 tie (`hot_fraction` 0.5) is not constructible here: 0.5 is not `q / RAND_MAX` for any integer `q` with the platform's odd `RAND_MAX`, so the package's behavioural test asserts it through its deviate seam instead. A `Type` 3 row with `mergeType` 0 is not constructed: SAGE26 sets `Type` 3 only together with a nonzero `mergeType` (`R/src/core_build_model.c:241-243`), and the package skips exactly its `Type` 3 rows. The largest c10 output has 52 fields and the largest input 30, within `oracle_c10`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048). `regenerate.sh` runs a fixture whose `routine` line is `determine_and_store_regime` (the `C10_ROUTINES` list) through `output/oracle/oracle_c10`, rewriting the c10 outputs together with every earlier group's, which it reproduces byte for byte.

**Package test.** `test_unit_sage26_determine_regime_oracle.c` runs each fixture through `sage26_determine_regime`'s `process()` with `CGMrecipeOn` 1 and the fixture's `MShockMsun` and `Hubble_h`, with a fixture row SAGE26 skips mapped to a `Type` 3 row. The module's test seam stands in for the keyed draw: its k-th deviate request returns `(double)rand.queue[k] / (double)RAND_MAX` with the recorded `intermediate.RAND_MAX`, SAGE26's own expression, so the package receives SAGE26's deviates in request order, and the seam reports which rows requested one. The test asserts every recorded field bit for bit. The package's production keyed draw is tested separately (`models/sage26/shared/_tests/test_unit_sage_keyed_draw.c`), never through these fixtures.

## The c10 Group, Part 2: Regime-Aware Infall, Stripping and Reincorporation

c10's second part covers what `CGMrecipeOn 1` changes in four c03 routines: `infall_recipe`, `add_infall_to_hot` and `strip_from_satellite` (`I` = `R/src/model_infall.c`) and `reincorporate_gas` (`G` = `R/src/model_reincorporation.c`), with SAGE26's regime-aware deposit helper `add_gas_to_hot_reservoir` (`X` = `R/src/model_misc.h`, `:76-85`); `B` is `R/src/core_build_model.c`. The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R4.par`: `CGMrecipeOn 1` (`R4.par:54`), and `ReionizationOn 1`, `BaryonFrac 0.17`, `ReIncorporationFactor 0.19` and `Reionization_z0`/`zr` 8/7 as R3 (`R4.par:51`, `:39`, `:72`, `:85-86`). The package's modules are `sage26_prepare_infall_budget`, `sage26_apply_infall`, `sage26_satellite_stripping` and `sage26_reincorporation`; their regime-aware deposit is `shared/sage_cgm_routing.h`.

### Where the Code Is

The oracle code is additions to **`oracle_c03.c`** (the section "THE C10 GROUP: CGMrecipeOn 1 (REGIME-AWARE ROUTING)"): no new main, and `build.sh` and `regenerate.sh` are unchanged, since every `cgm_*` fixture names a c03 routine and runs through `output/oracle/oracle_c03`. Each of the four readers reads `params.CGMrecipeOn` and refuses any value but 0 and 1 (SAGE26 accepts only those, `R/src/core_read_parameter_file.c:526`). With 0 the reader, the call and the output are exactly as before, so every earlier fixture is reproduced byte for byte. With 1:

| Routine | Reader also takes | Output also records |
|---|---|---|
| `infall_recipe` | every galaxy's `Regime` (SAGE26 reads the central's, `I:164`) | the branch flags `central_regime_cgm` (`I:164`), `cgm_metals_cap_binds` (`I:184`), `cgm_gas_negative_clears` (`I:188`), `cgm_metals_floor_binds` (`I:192`, -1 when `I:188` cleared both) |
| `add_infall_to_hot` | the central's `Regime`, `CGMgas`, `MetalsCGMgas` | the CGM pair; the flags `ejected_withdrawal_ran` (`I:357`), `ejected_metals_floor_binds` (`I:360`), `ejected_exhausted` (`I:363`), `reservoir_cgm` (`I:372`), `selected_metals_debit_ran` (`I:376`/`:390`), `selected_metals_floor_binds` (`I:379`/`:393`), `selected_gas_cleared` (`I:384`/`:398`); the locals below |
| `reincorporate_gas` | the central's `Regime`, `CGMgas`, `MetalsCGMgas` | the CGM pair; the flags `guards_passed` (`G:48`), `reincorporation_cap_binds` (`G:54`), `reservoir_cgm` (`X:78`); the locals below |
| `strip_from_satellite` | every galaxy's `Regime` (the satellite's at `I:250`, the central's at `I:251`) | the flags `stripped` (`I:246`), `take_from_cgm` (`I:250`), `give_to_cgm` (`I:251`), `gas_cap_binds` (`I:259`), `metals_cap_binds` (`I:260`); the locals below |

The c03 outputs of `infall_recipe` and `strip_from_satellite` already record both pairs of every galaxy. A flag is -1 where the path does not evaluate it.

### Transcriptions, Compositions and Check Kinds

Each routine's `CGMrecipeOn 1` path is a **source-reviewed transcription**, every statement cited to its SAGE26 line: `transcribe_infall_recipe_cgm` (`I:67-213`), `compose_add_infall_to_hot` (`I:351-414` with the call-site share, `B:488`), `compose_reincorporate_gas` (`G:44-66`, `X:76-85`) and `compose_strip_from_satellite` (`I:225-273`). `do_reionization` is SAGE26's own in the compositions. The oracle refuses a fixture unless:

- SAGE26's call changed no byte of any galaxy outside the recorded fields;
- the transcription reproduces SAGE26's call in every recorded field bit for bit (and, for `infall_recipe`, the return value);
- for each continuation-checked local, the composition that feeds the transcribed value into SAGE26's own function reproduces it too: SAGE26's `add_infall_to_hot` from `infallingGas_per_step`, SAGE26's `add_gas_to_hot_reservoir` (header-inline in `X`, so the oracle calls SAGE26's code) from `reincorporated` and from `metals_reincorporated`.

The recorded locals, with c06's markers (`intermediate.<name>_absent`; for a present local the value, `_checked`, `_check_kind` 1 continuation or 2 tail, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`):

| Routine | Local | SAGE26 | Type | Kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `add_infall_to_hot` | `infallingGas_per_step` (its value is c03's line) | `B:488` | double | continuation |
| | `infallingGas_after_ejected` (the residual entering the selected pair) | `I:362-368` | double | tail |
| | `metallicity_ejected` | `I:358` | float | tail |
| | `metallicity_selected` | `I:377`, `:391` | float | tail |
| `reincorporate_gas` | `Vcrit` | `G:46` | double | tail |
| | `reincorporated_pre_cap` | `G:50-52` | double | tail |
| | `reincorporated` | `G:54-55` | double | continuation |
| | `metallicity` | `G:57` | double | tail |
| | `metals_reincorporated` (`metallicity * reincorporated`, the helper's argument) | `G:64` | double | continuation |
| `strip_from_satellite` | `satBaryons` | `I:238-241` | double | tail |
| | `strippedGas_pre_cap` | `I:243-244` | double | tail |
| | `metallicity` | `I:256` | double | tail |
| | `strippedGasMetals_pre_cap` | `I:257` | double | tail |
| | `strippedGas` | `I:259` | double | tail |
| | `strippedGasMetals` | `I:260` | double | tail |

`infall_recipe` has no local the package carries: its return value is the package's `InfallingGas`, compared directly, and its migrations are float-plus-float stores with no double local. Two-sided validation (campaign decision D25) replaces a local by its upward and, separately, its downward neighbour (`nextafter` for a double, `nextafterf` for a float local) in its composition and records whether at least one recorded field changes. Every local of the table is validated two-sided on at least one fixture; the `cgm_validate_*` fixtures were built for it, each by an exact construction recorded in its `.in` (an exact cancellation: a share one double ulp above the negative of the CGM gas; a metallicity that rounds below `M / G` with the gas exactly consumed; a stripped mass within 2^-31 of the source gas, found by exact emulation of `I:238-244` over the satellite's `Mvir` and `StellarMass`; a reincorporated mass one double ulp below the ejected gas with metallicity 1/8). No local is narrowing-limited here. Where a cap binds (`gas_cap_binds`, `metals_cap_binds`, `reincorporation_cap_binds`) the recorded `strippedGas`, `strippedGasMetals` or `reincorporated` equals the reservoir it was capped to, and the package's test asserts it equal to that input reservoir.

### Fused Multiply-Adds

Read with `llvm-objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces (their machine code equals the reference build's, "Staged Source Equals the Reference Build's Source"). Every fused instruction on these routines' paths:

| Object | Offset | Line | Instruction | Expression | Package form |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_infall.o` (`infall_recipe`) | `0x2d8` | `I:129` | `fnmsub` | `reionization_modifier * BaryonFrac * Mvir - S`, the budget's finish | one expression (unchanged) |
| `model_infall.o` (`strip_from_satellite`) | `0x688` | `I:243` | `fnmadd` | the stripping excess | one expression (unchanged) |
| same | `0x758` | `I:267` | `fmadd` | `MetalsCGMgas += strippedGas * metallicity` | one expression |
| same | `0x784` | `I:270` | `fmadd` | `MetalsHotGas += strippedGas * metallicity` | one expression |
| `model_infall.o` (`add_infall_to_hot`) | `0x808` | `I:359` | `fmadd` | `MetalsEjectedMass += infallingGas * metallicity` | one expression (unchanged) |
| same | `0x954` | `I:378` | `fmadd` | `MetalsCGMgas += infallingGas * metallicity` | one expression (`*metals += ...`) |
| same | `0x8a0` | `I:392` | `fmadd` | `MetalsHotGas += infallingGas * metallicity`, `CGMrecipeOn 1` hot branch | the same expression |
| same | `0x900` | `I:406` | `fmadd` | the same, `CGMrecipeOn 0` branch | the same expression |
| `model_reincorporation.o` | `0xb8` | `G:61` | `fmsub` | `MetalsEjectedMass -= metallicity * reincorporated` | one expression (unchanged) |

Not fused: the central's migrations (`I:166-175`) are single-precision `fadd` of two floats (`0x200`, `0x210`, `0x308`, `0x318`), and the reincorporation's destination is an `fmul` at `G:64` (`0xc4`) followed by a separate double `fadd` in the inlined helper (`X:82`, `0xe4`): the metal product is a completed double argument, never fused into the credit. The fused instructions of `oracle_c03.o`'s c10 transcriptions are exactly the transcribed sites `I:129`, `I:243` (attributed by the line table to the inlined override helper), `I:267`, `I:270`, `I:359`, `I:378`, `I:392` and `G:61`; the `CGMrecipeOn 0` branch `I:406` is not transcribed. The package's objects (Mimic's `-O2`, arm64) fuse the same expressions and no others in these modules (`sage26_apply_infall.o`: the ejected and the selected metal debits; `sage26_satellite_stripping.o`: the excess and the two credits; `sage26_prepare_infall_budget.o`: the budget; `sage26_reincorporation.o`: `G:61` only).

### Mutation Sweep

A mutation sweep of the four transcriptions (this slice's validation record) compiled 61 mutants with the oracle's flags and ran each on every `CGMrecipeOn 1` fixture: inverted regime tests at `I:164`, `I:372`, `I:250`, `I:251` and `X:78`, the source and destination regimes swapped, the satellites' CGM zeroed and the satellite CGM pooled into the central at `CGMrecipeOn 1`, wrong members, dropped stores and zeroings, sign flips of each metal debit, a double metallicity at each float-metallicity debit, the float `Rvir / Vvir` widened, the metal product rounded to float, and every ordering comparison made non-strict. 57 are refused by at least one fixture. Two (`I:376` and `I:390` made `>=`) change only the recorded `selected_metals_debit_ran` flag: with zero metals the metallicity is 0 and the debit adds a signed zero that leaves ±0 unchanged, so they are equivalent in state. Two survive and are equivalent: the caps `G:54` and `I:259` made `>=`, which at equality assign the same, same-signed value. The `-0.0`, subnormal and exact-equality probes (`cgm_*negative_zero*`, `*_narrow_to_negative_zero_*`, `cgm_probe_vvir_equals_vcrit`, `cgm_strip_zero_excess_negative_source`) were added until every other strict/non-strict mutant was refused.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

63 fixtures, named `cgm_*`: 11 of `sage26_prepare_infall_budget`, 25 of `sage26_apply_infall`, 18 of `sage26_satellite_stripping` and 9 of `sage26_reincorporation`, alongside each module's c03 fixtures. Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R4-reachable** (37) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (26). `cgm_r4_snap32_*` and `cgm_r4_snap63_*` take real galaxies from the R4 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R4/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `2b606ee7ac82559e32c7c91161f1edb44f89288ec57016e80da884e118ed0807`) at snapshot 32 (a CGM-regime group) and 63 (a hot-regime group): end-of-snapshot values used as plausible call inputs. The `add_infall_to_hot` and `reincorporate_gas` R4 fixtures take their central's reservoirs and budget from the oracle's own output of the group's `infall_recipe` fixture, and say so; the Snap_63 satellite is a CGM-regime satellite holding only hot gas, so it is stripped from its empty CGM pair (real R4 evidence of the regime/reservoir disagreement).

| Routine | Cases |
|---|---|
| `infall_recipe` | the central in Regime 0 and 1 with both pairs non-empty and satellites that keep their CGM, one in the reservoir its regime does not use (`cgm_central_regime0_group`, `_regime1_group`, `cgm_central_not_first_regime0`); the CGM metals cap, the negative-CGM clear and the metals floor (`cgm_central_regime0_metals_above_cgm`, `_negative_cgm_clears`, `_negative_metals_floor`); a negative CGM on the hot-regime path, where no CGM check can bind (`cgm_central_regime1_negative_cgm`); `-0.0` metals on each path; the R4 groups |
| `add_infall_to_hot` | positive infall into each pair; negative infall absorbed by the ejected gas, exhausting it into each pair, with no ejected gas; each metal rule on each pair: the debit, the floor with and without the clear, the floor of a debit that narrows to `-0.0`, no debit for zero, `-0.0` or tiny negative metals, the clear of non-positive metals; exact consumption of the ejected or selected gas with the floor binding; zero infall; the validation constructions; the R4 centrals |
| `strip_from_satellite` | all four source/destination combinations with the gas cap binding and not (`cgm_strip_{cgm,hot}_to_{cgm,hot}[_gas_capped]`); a satellite whose regime names its empty pair (`cgm_strip_regime_disagrees_empty_source`, and the real Snap_63 satellite); metals above gas with the metals cap binding, to each destination; `-0.0` source metals; no excess, a zero excess against a negative source, and an orphan the call site does not strip; the validation construction; the R4 satellites |
| `reincorporate_gas` | each destination with the cap binding and not; below Vcrit; Vvir exactly equal to a float-exact Vcrit (`cgm_probe_vvir_equals_vcrit`, a non-R4 `ReIncorporationFactor`); the validation construction; the R4 centrals |

Every unselected reservoir is recorded and unchanged. Not constructed, each for its reason: a `Regime` other than 0 or 1 at `CGMrecipeOn 1` (the classifier assigns 0 or 1 to every row SAGE26 evolves before `infall_recipe` reads it, "The c10 Group"); a binding CGM safety check on the hot-regime path of `infall_recipe` (the migration zeroes the CGM pair first, `I:174-175`); `ReionizationOn 0` and `ReIncorporationFactor <= 0` (not R4, as for c03). The largest c10 input has 114 fields and the largest output 73, within `oracle_c03`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (512) and the reader's limit (2048). `regenerate.sh` rewrites these outputs with every other group's, which it reproduces byte for byte.

**Package tests.** Each module's oracle test runs its `cgm_*` fixtures through `process()` with the module's `CGMrecipeOn` set to 1 and the fixture's `Regime` and CGM pairs, asserts every recorded field bit for bit, asserts the oracle's reservoir selections (`reservoir_cgm`, `take_from_cgm`, `give_to_cgm`, `central_regime_cgm`) against the input regimes, asserts every unselected pair unchanged against its input, checks each local's markers and asserts a capped local equal to its reservoir; a local the package does not carry is consumed unasserted, and the test prints the run's tallies of continuation-checked, tail-checked, validated, binding and absent locals.

## The c10 Group, Part 3: Regime-Aware Feedback, Yields, the Wind and Disruption (c05 and c06)

c10's third part covers what `CGMrecipeOn 1` changes in the c05 routines: the shared supernova commit `update_from_feedback` (`S:1146-1251`, `S` = `R/src/model_starformation_and_feedback.c`), whose CGM branch (`S:1175-1223`) selects the FoF central's CGM pair for its Regime 0 and its hot pair otherwise; the disk yield's escaping metals (`S:1093-1109`), routed by `add_metals_to_hot_reservoir` (`X:67-74`, `X` = `R/src/model_misc.h`) on the FoF central; and, on a gas-unstable disk, the instability's continuation (`D:90-108`, `D` = `R/src/model_disk_instability.c`): `grow_black_hole` and its `quasar_mode_wind` (`M:346-441`, `M` = `R/src/model_mergers.c`), whose second test selects the WIND HOST's (the forming galaxy's) pair (`M:406-428`), and the mode-1 burst, whose commit (`M:791`) and yield (`M:813-830`) route by the FoF central. The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R4.par`: `CGMrecipeOn 1` (`R4.par:54`) and every other line as `R3.par` (`SFprescription 1` `:48`, `FIREmodeOn 1` `:55`, `BulgeSizeOn 3` `:109`, `DiskInstabilityOn 1` `:52`, `AGNrecipeOn 2` `:49`, `FracZleaveDisk 0` `:83`). The package's sites are `mimic_sage_update_from_feedback` (`shared/sage_sf_feedback_physics.h`), `sage26_apply_metal_enrichment`, `mimic_apply_black_hole_growth` and `mimic_apply_quasar_mode_wind` (`shared/sage_agn_physics.h`) and `mimic_apply_collisional_starburst` (`shared/sage_starburst_physics.h`), with `shared/sage_cgm_routing.h`.

### Where the Code Is

The oracle code is additions to **`oracle_c05.c`**: no new main, and `build.sh` and `regenerate.sh` are unchanged, since both routines were already `C05_ROUTINES`. The configuration reader `read_cgm_recipe_on` reads `params.CGMrecipeOn` 0 or 1 and refuses any other value, naming it (SAGE26 accepts only those, `R/src/core_read_parameter_file.c:526`), for two routines only: `update_from_feedback` and `starformation_and_feedback_fire` (`read_r0_flags`'s `allow_cgm`). Every other c05 routine, `check_disk_instability_fire` included, still requires 0. With 0 the reader, the call and the output are exactly as before, so every earlier fixture is reproduced byte for byte. With 1 (`cgm_block_recorded`):

| Routine | Reader also takes | Output also records |
|---|---|---|
| `update_from_feedback` | every galaxy's `Regime` (`i32`, refused unless 0 or 1) and CGM pair (`CGMgas`, `MetalsCGMgas`) | every galaxy's `Regime` and CGM pair (after the c05 fields); then, after every earlier field, `intermediate.cgm_recipe_on` and `feedback_reservoir_cgm` |
| `starformation_and_feedback_fire` | the same, for every galaxy | every galaxy's `Regime` and CGM pair, in the end state and in `intermediate.after_bh.*`; then, after every earlier record, the c10 record below |

The c10 record of a wrapper call: `intermediate.cgm_recipe_on`; the flags `feedback_reservoir_cgm` (`S:1175-1176`, the disk commit), `yield_reservoir_cgm` (`X:69` at `S:1102` or `S:1108`), `instability_wind_reservoir_cgm` (`M:406-407`, host `p`), `instability_wind_ejects_cold` (`M:397`), `instability_wind_ejects_selected` (`M:411` or `M:422`), `instability_burst_feedback_reservoir_cgm` (`S:1175-1176` inside the burst's commit, `M:791`) and `instability_burst_yield_reservoir_cgm` (`X:69` at `M:824` or `M:830`), each 1 for the CGM pair, 0 for the hot pair and -1 where the path does not reach the site; the wind's values `instability_wind_quasar_energy` (`M:393`), `instability_wind_cold_gas_energy` (`M:394`), `instability_wind_cgm_gas_energy` (`M:409`) and `instability_wind_hot_gas_energy` (`M:420`, the unselected pair's energy absent), with c06's markers; and, where the gas branch ran, four observations (below). A selection flag is computed by the transcription of its selection test at its call site.

**One set of names.** The carried values of `update_from_feedback` keep their c05 names on every branch: `metallicity_hot`, `metals_hot_to_eject` and `metals_hot_to_eject_capped` hold the SELECTED pair's values, SAGE26's `metallicityCGM`, `metalsCGM_to_eject` (`S:1187-1192`) on the CGM branch and `metallicityHot`, `metalsHot_to_eject` (`S:1211-1216`, `S:1235-1240`) on the hot ones, and `ejected_mass_capped` the ejection capped to the selected gas. A binding `ejected_cap_binds` or `metals_cap_binds` records the value equal to the SELECTED reservoir after the reheating and before the debit. The package's `struct MimicSageFeedbackLocals` names them `metallicity_selected`, `metals_to_eject`, `metals_to_eject_capped`, `selected_gas_at_cap` and `selected_metals_at_cap`, and the package tests map one onto the other; `yield_metals_to_hot` likewise holds the escaping metals whichever pair receives them.

### Transcriptions, Compositions and Check Kinds

Each statement is cited to its SAGE26 line in the code:

- `transcribe_update_from_feedback` gains the `CGMrecipeOn 1` branch, `S:1175-1223`: the selection by the central's Regime (`S:1175-1176`), the CGM branch (`S:1180-1198`) and the hot branch (`S:1204-1222`), each statement for statement, then `S:1249`. Its `CGMrecipeOn 0` path is untouched.
- `transcribe_add_metals_to_hot_reservoir` restates `X:67-74`; the composition that transcribes everything calls it at `S:1102` and `S:1108` (a `CGMrecipeOn 1` fixture only, `struct composition`'s `transcribe_routing`), the composition with SAGE26's own routines calls SAGE26's own header-inline helper.
- `transcribe_grow_black_hole_cgm` (`M:346-378`, with c08's hydrogen cap `M:366-372`) and `transcribe_quasar_mode_wind_cgm` (`M:390-429`) replace SAGE26's own `grow_black_hole` in the transcribed instability's continuation (`D:102-104`) on a `CGMrecipeOn 1` fixture; the burst (`D:106-108`) stays SAGE26's own `collisional_starburst_recipe`. Before the burst the transcription records the burst's two selections from the FoF central's Regime (no statement between them and their sites writes `Regime`).

As in c05, the oracle refuses a fixture unless (a) the composition with every transcription and (b) the composition with SAGE26's own `update_from_star_formation`, `update_from_feedback`, `check_disk_instability` (and so its own growth, wind and burst) and `add_metals_to_hot_reservoir` each reproduce SAGE26's `starformation_and_feedback` end state bit for bit, now including every galaxy's `Regime` and CGM pair; an `update_from_feedback` fixture requires its transcription to reproduce SAGE26's own call. The c05 carried values keep c05's record (`_absent`, the value, `_checked`, the three validation markers; c05 records no check kind) and the c08 and c09 values keep theirs. `yield_metals_to_hot` is fed to SAGE26's own `add_metals_to_hot_reservoir` in composition (b), so it is checked through SAGE26's continuation. The four wind values are **tail-checked** (`_check_kind` 2: the transcription passes none to a SAGE26 function, and the composition that contains them reproduces SAGE26's end state) and validated two-sided through the composition that transcribes the instability (`validate_wrapper_cgm_value`).

**Observations of SAGE26's nested growth and burst.** The continuation's burst is SAGE26's own, so its selections are not transcription results; and composition (b) runs SAGE26's own growth. Four counterfactuals of the fully transcribed composition, each flipping one galaxy's `Regime` during one SAGE26 call only and restoring it after, record whether SAGE26's end state then changes (`observe_nested_regime`): `instability_wind_host_regime_sensitive` (the host's Regime during SAGE26's own `grow_black_hole`), `instability_wind_central_regime_sensitive` (the FoF central's, during the same call), `instability_burst_central_regime_sensitive` (the central's, during SAGE26's own burst) and `instability_burst_host_regime_sensitive` (the host's, during the burst); -1 where the gas branch did not run and, for the two flips of the galaxy that is not the expected one, where the forming galaxy is its own FoF central. A 1 shows that call routes by that galaxy's Regime on that input; a 0 that it does not or that its routing makes no difference there (an empty pair, a zero transfer, a failing energy test). On the eight gas-unstable fixtures one of the two expected flips is 1 on each (8): the wind's host flip where the wind ejects (five fixtures), the burst's central flip where the wind ejects nothing and the burst forms stars (three); the other expected flip is 0 there because its routed transfer is zero (an empty selected pair, or no burst stars once the wind has taken the cold gas). The two other flips are 0 on every fixture where they are evaluated (14; -1 on the self-central fixture): SAGE26's wind routes by the host and its burst by the FoF central.

**Two-sided validation.** On the 36 fixtures, 514 c05 carried values are present (22 validated two-sided), 308 c08 and c09 values (34 continuation-checked, 274 tail-checked; 45 validated; 17 narrowing-limited, c08's `h2_budget` on every wrapper fixture, as before) and 24 c10 wind values (24 tail-checked, 0 validated); 101 values are absent on their paths. 17 fixtures record a binding clamp or cap (21 in all), each value equal to its reservoir at that point. No c10 wind value is validated two-sided: each reaches the state only through a strict comparison with another energy, so a one-ulp change of it changes the state only at an exact tie, and at a tie only in one direction (moving the value across the threshold one way, not the other). The wind's equality boundaries are covered directly by the c06 group's `quasar_mode_wind` fixtures; here the tests' outcomes (pass and fail of each, on each pair) are covered and asserted.

### Fused Multiply-Adds

Read with `/Library/Developer/CommandLineTools/usr/bin/llvm-objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces (`output/oracle/obj/model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `model_mergers.o` and `oracle_c05.o`; their SAGE26 machine code equals the reference build's, "Staged Source Equals the Reference Build's Source"). Every fused instruction at this part's sites:

| Object, function | Line | Offset | Instruction | Expression | In the oracle | In the package |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `update_from_feedback` | `S:1168` | `0x2198` | `fmsub d3, d2, d8, d3` | `MetalsColdGas -= metallicity * reheated_mass` (unchanged, c05's) | transcribed, fused (`oracle_c05.o` `0x8754`) | explicit `fma(-metallicity, reheated_mass, MetalsColdGas)` |
| same | `S:1176` (`S:1205` and `S:1229` merged) | `0x2234` | `fmadd d2, d2, d8, d3` | the hot pair's credit `MetalsHotGas += metallicity * reheated_mass`, both hot branches, attributed by the line table to `S:1176` | transcribed, fused: `S:1205` at `0x8874`, `S:1229` at `0x89f4` | explicit `fma(metallicity, reheated_mass, *selected_metals)` on the selected pair |
| same | `S:1181` | `0x22f0` | `fmadd d2, d2, d8, d3` | the CGM pair's credit `MetalsCGMgas += metallicity * reheated_mass` | transcribed, fused (`0x8b88`) | the same `fma` (the selected pair) |
| `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `starformation_and_feedback` | `S:1097` | `0xf50` | `fmadd d2, d2, d4, d3` | the retained yield `MetalsColdGas += Yield * (1.0 - FracZleaveDiskVal) * stars` (unchanged, c05's) | transcribed, fused (`0x8044`) | explicit `fma(Yield * (1.0 - FracZleaveDiskVal), stars, MetalsColdGas)` |
| same | `S:1100`, `S:1106` | `0xf5c`, `0xf60`; `0xf98` | **none fused**: `fmul` | the escaping metals `Yield * FracZleaveDiskVal * stars` (two products, left to right) and `Yield * stars` | transcribed, unfused | one expression, unfused |
| same (`add_metals_to_hot_reservoir` inlined) | `X:70`, `X:72` | `0xffc`-`0x1004`, `0x1014`-`0x101c`; `0xf7c`-`0xf84`, `0xfbc`-`0xfc4` | **none fused**: `fcvt`, `fadd`, `fcvt` | the selected metals plus the completed double, narrowed | transcribed, unfused | `mimic_sage_add_metals_to_hot_reservoir`, unfused |
| `model_mergers.o`, `grow_black_hole` | `M:352` | `0x818` | `fmadd d1, d1, d1, d3` | `1.0 + SQR(BH_GROWTH_V_KMS / Vvir)` (c06's site) | transcribed, fused (`0x92b0`) | explicit `fma(v_ratio, v_ratio, 1.0)` |
| same | `M:362` | `0x884` | `fmsub d0, d0, d8, d2` | `MetalsColdGas -= metallicity * BHaccrete` (c06's site) | transcribed, fused (`0x9300`) | explicit `fma(-metallicity, bh_accrete, MetalsColdGas)` |
| `model_mergers.o`, `quasar_mode_wind` | `M:393-428` | | **none fused** | the wind energy, the binding energies and the two tests | transcribed, unfused | one expression per statement |

The fused instructions of `oracle_c05.o` are exactly c05's seven sites (`S:1036`, `S:1097`, `S:1168`, `S:1229`, `D:79`, the two `fmla.2d` of `S:1123-1124`) and this part's four (`S:1181`, `S:1205`, `M:352`, `M:362`), each with SAGE26's operands; the listings, and the script that extracts every fused instruction with its line, are in this slice's validation record. `uff_cgm_rounding_metals_fused` and `uff_cgm_hot_rounding_metals_fused` separate the fused CGM and hot credits from an unfused one (executed mutants below).

### Mutation Sweep

A sweep of the extended transcriptions and records (this slice's validation record) compiled 46 mutants of `oracle_c05.c` with the oracle's flags and ran each on the 36 fixtures below: a mutant is refused where the oracle exits non-zero or writes an output that differs from the fixture's. 40 are refused: the selection inverted, read from the forming galaxy and its flag misrecorded at `S:1176`; the `CGMrecipeOn` test inverted; each pair's credit, cap, metallicity, metal cap and debit read from or written to the other pair; each cap made non-strict; the cap read before the reheating; the uncapped metal debit; the dropped `OutflowRate`; each fused credit unfused; the yield's selection inverted, misrecorded, written to the gas, or routed by the forming galaxy on either branch; the wind's selection inverted or read from the FoF central (and its flag); the CGM energy from the hot gas; either second test without the cold binding energy; the wind energy reduced by the cold ejection; the CGM ejection zeroing or moving the wrong pair; the dropped accretion record; each burst flag read from the host; each observation flipping the wrong galaxy. 6 survive: the three energy tests of the wind made non-strict (`M:397`, `M:411`, `M:422`), which differ only at an exact tie of two energies, not constructed through the composed disk path (the c06 group's direct wind fixtures cover them); and three branches of the transcribed growth that these fixtures do not reach, the accretion cap made non-strict (`M:355`, unreachable at R4's `BlackHoleGrowthRate`, since the accretion is at most 0.015 of the cold gas), the dropped metal floor (`M:363`, a negative cold metal mass after the accretion's debit) and the dropped hydrogen cap (`M:370`, `H2gas` above its bound after the accretion); c06's and c08's direct `grow_black_hole` fixtures cover all three, and every gas-unstable fixture checks this transcription against SAGE26's own growth bit for bit.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

36 fixtures: 17 `cgm_sf_*` of `sage26_calculate_star_formation` (routine `starformation_and_feedback_fire`) and 19 `uff_cgm_*` of `sage26_apply_star_formation_supernova` (routine `update_from_feedback`). Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R4-reachable** (9: the six real groups and three gas-unstable fixtures whose forming galaxy's cold gas is raised from its R4 row, as c05's unstable `r0_` fixtures raise theirs) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (27). `*_r4_snap32_*` and `*_r4_snap63_*` take real FoF groups from the R4 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R4/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `2b606ee7ac82559e32c7c91161f1edb44f89288ec57016e80da884e118ed0807`) at snapshots 32 and 63, both routines at both snapshots: end-of-snapshot values used as plausible call inputs, the rows named in each `.in`. Two are real evidence of the regime/reservoir disagreement: Snap_63's satellites 177 and 376 are in the CGM regime and hold only hot gas. The inputs were written by a tool (the validation record) from those rows; the constructed ones change only the fields their comments name.

| Routine | Cases |
|---|---|
| `starformation_and_feedback_fire` | real R4 groups, a satellite forming stars, with the central in Regime 0 (`cgm_sf_r4_snap32_cgm_group`, `_snap63_cgm_group`) and in Regime 1 with the satellite in Regime 0 (`_snap32_hot_group`, `_snap63_hot_group`); the central in Regime 0 holding both pairs with the satellite in Regime 1 (`cgm_sf_central_regime0_satellite_regime1`); the disk commit's ejection cap binding on each pair (`cgm_sf_ejected_cap_binds_cgm`, `_hot`); the retained yield's escaping metals into each pair, `FracZleaveDisk` 0.3 (labelled NOT R4) (`cgm_sf_yield_retained_to_cgm`, `_hot`); the gas-unstable continuation: the wind ejecting the host's cold gas and its CGM pair with the central in Regime 1 and the all-metals yield to the central's hot metals (`cgm_sf_unstable_wind_cgm_central_hot`), its hot pair with the central in Regime 0 and the all-metals yield to the central's CGM metals (`_wind_hot_central_cgm`), the cold gas only (the selected test failing, `_wind_cold_only`), the second test passing only with the original cold energy (`_wind_original_cold_energy`); the wind ejecting nothing so the burst's commit routes to the central's hot pair (`_burst_central_hot`) and CGM pair (`_burst_central_cgm`), with the burst's and the disk's escaping metals nonzero (`_burst_yield_frac_z_leave`, `FracZleaveDisk` 0.3); the forming galaxy its own FoF central (`cgm_sf_unstable_central_self`) |
| `update_from_feedback` | real R4 groups at Snap_32 (hot pair) and Snap_63 (CGM pair) (`uff_cgm_r4_snap32_hot_group`, `_snap63_cgm_group`); on the CGM pair (central Regime 0) and, `uff_cgm_hot_*`, on the hot pair at `CGMrecipeOn 1` (central Regime 1), the forming satellite always in the other regime and the central holding both pairs: neither cap binding (`caps_free`), the gas cap binding (`ejected_cap_binds`), both binding with the metal cap by rounding (`metals_cap_binds_rounding`), the metal cap binding to negative metals (`metals_cap_negative`), metals above the gas with the metal cap not binding (`metals_above_gas`: the metallicity clamps to 1, so the transfer is the capped ejection), both comparisons at equality (`caps_at_equality`), the cap after the reheating (`cap_after_reheating`) and the fused credit (`rounding_metals_fused`); the forming galaxy its own central (`uff_cgm_central_self`) |

Every unselected reservoir is recorded and unchanged: the central's hot pair during CGM feedback and CGM pair during hot feedback, the wind host's pair its Regime does not select, and both pairs of a forming satellite on the disk commit. Not constructed: a `Regime` other than 0 or 1 (the classifier assigns 0 or 1 to every evolved row before these routines run, "The c10 Group"); the `CGMrecipeOn 1` instability fixture (`check_disk_instability_fire` still requires 0: its routing sites, the wind's and the burst's, are reached through the composed wrapper here and directly through the c06 group's fixtures, and `sage26_disk_instability` itself selects no reservoir); a `CGMrecipeOn 1` fixture of `starformation_and_feedback` or of the sequence (the wrapper's `CGMrecipeOn 1` path is `starformation_and_feedback_fire`'s, R4 being a FIRE rung); the wind's exact energy ties (above); a binding metal cap on the composed disk path (covered by the `update_from_feedback` fixtures of the same shared commit). The largest input has 220 fields and the largest output 562, within `oracle_c05`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048). `regenerate.sh` rewrites these outputs with every other group's, which it reproduces byte for byte.

**Package tests.** `test_unit_sage26_apply_star_formation_supernova_oracle.c` passes the fixture's `CGMrecipeOn` to the shared commit with every galaxy's `Regime` and CGM pair, asserts every recorded field bit for bit, the locals by the name mapping above, the binding caps equal to the selected pair at the cap, the oracle's `feedback_reservoir_cgm` against the kernel's `reservoir_cgm` and both against the FoF central's input Regime, and the central's unselected pair and both pairs of every other galaxy unchanged. `test_unit_sage26_calculate_star_formation_oracle.c` runs the chain with the fixture's `CGMrecipeOn` as every module's parameter and the fixture's Regimes and CGM pairs, asserts the end state (Regime and the CGM pairs included) and the state after the black-hole growth bit for bit, and asserts each routing site against the oracle's flag AND against the expected galaxy's input Regime: the disk commit (the apply-step replay's kernel locals) and the disk yield (a replay of the enrichment step on copies through the shared routing helper, required to end in `process()`'s state) by the FoF central; the wind (a replay of the quasar module's growth on copies, required to end in `process()`'s state) by the wind host, with its two tests and four energies compared bit for bit; the instability burst's commit and yield (the c09 burst replay, its bundle carrying the fixture's `CGMrecipeOn`) by the FoF central. It asserts each galaxy's pair its Regime does not select unchanged, counts the observations, and prints the tallies.

### The c06 Part: Where the Code Is

c10's third part also covers what `CGMrecipeOn 1` changes in the c06 routines (`M` = `R/src/model_mergers.c`, `S` = `R/src/model_starformation_and_feedback.c`, `D` = `R/src/model_disk_instability.c`, `X` = `R/src/model_misc.h`, `B` = `R/src/core_build_model.c`): the quasar wind's second test on the WIND HOST's selected pair (`M:406-429`), reached directly (`quasar_mode_wind`), through `grow_black_hole` (the galaxy itself is the host) and through the merger (the target, `M:262-264`); the burst's commit (`S:1175-1223` at `M:791`) and yield (`X:67-74` at `M:824`, `M:830`) on the FoF central's selected pair; the post-minor-merger recheck (`M:806-811` into `D:54-108`), whose wind selects by the burst HOST's Regime and whose own burst's commit and yield by the FoF central's; and disruption's deposit (`X:76-85` at `M:851`) by the PASSED recipient's Regime, which the pass gives as the merger target (`B:539-543`, `B:575`), an orphan's subhalo central included. Merger addition stays CGM to CGM and hot to hot (`M:464-465`, `:496-497`). The rung flags are `par/R4.par`'s (`CGMrecipeOn 1` at `:54`, every other line `R3.par`'s). The package's sites are `mimic_apply_quasar_mode_wind` and `mimic_apply_black_hole_growth` (`shared/sage_agn_physics.h`), `mimic_apply_collisional_starburst` (`shared/sage_starburst_physics.h`), `mimic_sage_update_from_feedback` (`shared/sage_sf_feedback_physics.h`) and `mimic_sage_disrupt_satellite_to_ics` (`shared/sage_merger_physics.h`), with `shared/sage_cgm_routing.h`.

The oracle code is additions to **`oracle_c06.c`**: no new main, and `build.sh` and `regenerate.sh` are unchanged, since every routine was already in `C06_ROUTINES`. The configuration reader `read_cgm_recipe_on` reads `params.CGMrecipeOn` 0 or 1 and refuses any other value, naming it (`R/src/core_read_parameter_file.c:526`), for six routines (`cgm_routine`): `grow_black_hole`, `quasar_mode_wind`, `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS`, `collisional_starburst_recipe_fire`, `deal_with_galaxy_merger_fire` and `merger_pass_fire`. Every other c06 routine still requires 0. With 0 the reader, the call and the output are exactly as before, so every earlier fixture is reproduced byte for byte. With 1 (`cgm_block`) every galaxy also reads and records its `Regime` (`i32`, a new block `BLOCK_CGM` after `BLOCK_FIRE`, refused unless 0 or 1; the CGM pair is already among c06's reservoirs), and `grow_black_hole`, `quasar_mode_wind` and `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS` read `FIREmodeOn` 0 or 1 (R4's 1; none of them reads it). A CGMrecipeOn 1 `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS` fixture records every galaxy, so a FoF central that is not the recipient is asserted unchanged.

| Routine | Output also records (after every earlier field) |
|---|---|
| `quasar_mode_wind`, `grow_black_hole` | every recorded galaxy's `Regime`; `intermediate.cgm_recipe_on`; `wind_reservoir_cgm` (`M:406-407`), `wind_ejects_cgm` (`M:411`) and `cgm_gas_energy` (`M:409`) |
| `collisional_starburst_recipe_fire` | `Regime`; `cgm_recipe_on`; `burst_feedback_reservoir_cgm` (`S:1175-1176`), `burst_yield_reservoir_cgm` (`X:69`), the recheck's `recheck_wind_reservoir_cgm` (`M:406-407`, host), `recheck_wind_ejects_cold` (`M:397`), `recheck_wind_ejects_selected` (`M:411`, `M:422`), `recheck_feedback_reservoir_cgm` and `recheck_yield_reservoir_cgm` (the recheck burst's, FoF central); the recheck wind's `recheck_wind_quasar_energy`, `_cold_gas_energy`, `_cgm_gas_energy` and `_hot_gas_energy`; `recheck_gas_branch` (`D:90`; -1 where the recheck did not run) and, where it is 1, four observations (below) |
| `deal_with_galaxy_merger_fire` | both of the above: the merger's wind (the target) and its burst and recheck |
| `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS` | `Regime` of every galaxy; `cgm_recipe_on`; `disrupt_reservoir_cgm` (`X:78`, the passed recipient) |
| `merger_pass_fire` | `Regime`; `cgm_recipe_on`; per galaxy `intermediate.pass.galaxy[<i>].disrupt_reservoir_cgm` (`X:78` on the passed recipient at `M:851`, -1 where the row is not disrupted), `recipient_regime_sensitive` and `central_regime_sensitive` (observations, below) |

A selection flag is 1 for the CGM pair, 0 for the hot pair, -1 where the path does not reach the site, each computed by the transcription of its selection test at its call site. c06's `wind_ejects_hot` keeps its meaning, the second test on the hot pair, and is -1 where the CGM pair is selected (its energy `hot_gas_energy` absent); the CGM test is `wind_ejects_cgm`. The burst feedback's carried values keep their c06 names on both branches, as c05's do ("One set of names" above): `metallicity_hot`, `metals_hot_to_eject` and `metals_hot_to_eject_capped` hold the SELECTED pair's values, `ejected_mass_capped` the ejection capped to the selected gas after the reheating.

### The c06 Part: Transcriptions, Compositions and Check Kinds

Each statement is cited to its SAGE26 line in the code:

- `transcribe_quasar_mode_wind` gains the `CGMrecipeOn 1` branch, `M:406-429`: the host `gal`'s own Regime (`M:407`), the CGM energy and test (`M:409-417`) and the hot energy and test (`M:420-428`), each adding the ORIGINAL cold binding energy; its `CGMrecipeOn 0` branch (`M:430-441`) is untouched.
- c06's restated `transcribe_update_from_feedback` gains `S:1175-1223` (the FoF central's Regime, the CGM branch `S:1180-1198`, the hot branch `S:1204-1222`, then `S:1249`), as oracle_c05.c's does.
- `transcribe_add_metals_to_hot_reservoir` (`X:67-74`) and `transcribe_add_gas_to_hot_reservoir` (`X:76-85`) restate the routing helpers. A new composition member `routing` selects them, in the fully transcribed composition and on `CGMrecipeOn 1` fixtures only: the burst's yield (`M:824`, `M:830`) and the disruption's deposit (`M:851`). Every other composition calls SAGE26's own header-inline helpers, so `yield_metals_to_hot`, `total_gas` and `total_metals_gas` stay continuation-checked.
- `transcribe_recheck_cgm` restates `check_disk_instability` (`D:54-108`) as the burst's recheck calls it (`M:809`): the disk mass, `Mcrit` and its cap, the unstable fractions, the stellar transfer with SAGE26's own `get_metallicity` and `update_instability_bulge_radius`, then the gas branch with `transcribe_recheck_grow_black_hole` (`M:346-378`, with c08's hydrogen cap) and `transcribe_recheck_quasar_mode_wind` (`M:390-429`), whose values and tests go to the `recheck_*` slots. The recheck's own burst (`D:106-108`) stays SAGE26's `collisional_starburst_recipe`; its two selections are recorded from the FoF central's Regime before it (no statement between there and its sites writes `Regime`). The VERBOSE diagnostic of `D:91-99` is not transcribed. A new composition member `recheck` selects it, in the fully transcribed composition and the recheck values' validation composition only, on `CGMrecipeOn 1` fixtures; otherwise the burst calls SAGE26's own `check_disk_instability`, as before.

As in c06, the oracle refuses a fixture unless the fully transcribed composition (now with the CGM branches, the routing helpers and the recheck) and the composition with SAGE26's own sub-routines each reproduce SAGE26's end state in every recorded field, `Regime` included, bit for bit, and unless every validation composition does. The c10 values (`cgm_gas_energy` and the four recheck wind energies) are **tail-checked** (`_check_kind` 2: the transcription passes none to a SAGE26 function) and validated two-sided in the composition of the routine they are a local of (`cgm_gas_energy` with the wind transcribed, the recheck's with the burst and its recheck transcribed). The c06, c08 and c09 values keep their records.

**Observations of SAGE26's nested routines.** The recheck's own burst is SAGE26's, and composition (b) runs SAGE26's own recheck. Four counterfactuals of the fully transcribed composition, each flipping one galaxy's `Regime` during one SAGE26 call of the recheck only and restoring it after, record whether SAGE26's end state then changes (`observe_recheck_regime`): `recheck_wind_host_regime_sensitive` and `recheck_wind_central_regime_sensitive` (the host's, and the FoF central's, Regime during SAGE26's own `grow_black_hole`) and `recheck_burst_central_regime_sensitive` and `recheck_burst_host_regime_sensitive` (the FoF central's, and the host's, during SAGE26's own burst); -1 for the flips of the other galaxy where the host is its own FoF central. In a pass, each row that merges or disrupts is observed the same way: the pass runs again from the input with the recipient's (the merger target's) Regime flipped during that row's SAGE26 call only, and with the FoF central's (`write_pass_cgm_record`). A 1 shows the call routes by that galaxy's Regime on that input; a 0 that it does not or that its routing makes no difference there. On the eight fixtures whose recheck reaches its gas branch (six bursts, two mergers) one of the two expected flips is 1 on each (8): the wind host's where the recheck's wind ejects (two), the FoF central's during the burst where it forms stars (six); the other expected flip is 0 there because its routed transfer is zero. The two other flips are 0 on all 16 evaluations: SAGE26's recheck wind routes by the host and its burst by the FoF central. In the passes the recipient's (target's) flip is 1 on every one of the 7 rows that merge or disrupt; the FoF central's flip is 0 on the 2 disruptions into a Type 1 recipient (the deposit follows the recipient only) and 1 on the 2 orphan mergers (the burst routes by the central), -1 where the central is the recipient.

### The c06 Part: Two-Sided Validation and Counts

On the 52 fixtures, 1100 c06, c08 and c09 carried values are present (358 continuation-checked, 742 tail-checked; 26 validated two-sided; 204 narrowing-limited: c07's remnant radius locals and c08's refresh values, as before) and 38 c10 values (38 tail-checked, 0 validated two-sided); 127 values are absent on their paths; 187 further fields are the pass's driver records. 5 fixtures record a binding clamp or cap (7 in all), each value equal to its reservoir at that point. No c10 value is validated two-sided, for the reason c05's part gives: each reaches the state only through a strict comparison with another energy, so a one-ulp change changes the state only at an exact tie and there in one direction only. The ties are constructed directly instead: on each of the three tests (the cold test `M:397`, cold plus CGM `M:411` with the host in Regime 0, cold plus hot `M:422` with the host in Regime 1) a fixture puts the wind energy exactly at the threshold and one double below and above it (`qw_cgm_boundary_*`), the accreted mass found by an exact emulation of `M:393` over neighbouring doubles and the free mass stepped one float at a time until an exact tie existed. The count script and its output are in this slice's validation record.

### The c06 Part: Fused Multiply-Adds

Read with `/Library/Developer/CommandLineTools/usr/bin/llvm-objdump -d -l` from the objects `build.sh` produces (`output/oracle/obj/model_mergers.o`, `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `model_disk_instability.o` and `oracle_c06.o`; their SAGE26 machine code equals the reference build's). Every fused instruction at this part's sites:

| Object, function | Line | Offset | Instruction | Expression | In the oracle (`oracle_c06.o`) | In the package |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_starformation_and_feedback.o`, `update_from_feedback` | `S:1181` | `0x22f0` | `fmadd d2, d2, d8, d3` | the CGM credit `MetalsCGMgas += metallicity * reheated_mass` (the burst's commit, `M:791`) | transcribed, fused (`0x9854`) | explicit `fma` on the selected pair (`shared/sage_sf_feedback_physics.h`) |
| same | `S:1176` (`S:1205`, `S:1229` merged) | `0x2234` | `fmadd d2, d2, d8, d3` | the hot credit, both hot branches | transcribed, fused: `S:1205` at `0x94c4`, `S:1229` at `0x96b4` (c06's) | the same `fma` |
| `model_mergers.o`, `grow_black_hole` (the recheck's growth) | `M:352` | `0x818` | `fmadd d1, d1, d1, d3` | `1.0 + SQR(BH_GROWTH_V_KMS / Vvir)` | transcribed, fused in `transcribe_recheck_grow_black_hole` (`0xa4b4`) | explicit `fma(v_ratio, v_ratio, 1.0)` |
| same | `M:362` | `0x884` | `fmsub d0, d0, d8, d2` | `MetalsColdGas -= metallicity * BHaccrete` | transcribed, fused (`0xa504`) | explicit `fma(-metallicity, bh_accrete, MetalsColdGas)` |
| `model_disk_instability.o`, `check_disk_instability` (the recheck) | `D:79` | `0x104` | `fmadd d0, d0, d11, d1` | `MetalsBulgeMass += metallicity * unstable_stars` | transcribed, fused in `transcribe_recheck_cgm` (`0x9d2c`) | explicit `fma` (`shared/sage_disk_instability_physics.h`) |
| `model_mergers.o`, `quasar_mode_wind` | `M:393-429` | | **none fused** | the energies and the three tests | transcribed, unfused | one expression per statement |
| `model_mergers.o`, `collisional_starburst_recipe` (the yield's routing, `X:69-72` inlined) | `M:822`, `M:828`, `X:72` | `0x1184`-`0x11ec` | **none fused**: `fmul`, then `fcvt`, `fadd`, `fcvt` | the escaping metals, then the selected metals plus the completed double, narrowed | transcribed, unfused | `mimic_sage_add_metals_to_hot_reservoir`, unfused |
| `model_mergers.o`, `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS` (`X:78-83` inlined) | `M:848-849`, `X:82-83` | `0x14a0`-`0x14f0` | **none fused**: single-precision `fadd s` | the float gas and metal sums, then the deposit, which clang computes in single precision: `(float)((double)a + (double)b)` equals the float sum `a + b` for two floats (a double has more than `2 * 24 + 2` bits) | transcribed as the source writes it (a double sum, narrowed) | `mimic_sage_add_gas_to_hot_reservoir`, a double sum narrowed (equal, as above) |
