The fused instructions of `oracle_c06.o` are now 18: c06's twelve and c08's `M:802` (as before) and this part's five, `S:1181`, `S:1205` and the recheck's `M:352`, `M:362` and `D:79`, each with SAGE26's operands; the listing and the extraction script are in this slice's validation record. `burst_cgm_rounding_feedback_metals_cgm` and `_hot` separate the fused `S:1181` and `S:1205` credits from unfused ones (executed mutants below): the central's selected metals are minus the float just below the product, at a host cold-metal mass found by a scan where the product lies within `2^-29` of a float, so the credit cancels to a residual whose float keeps the product's rounding.

### The c06 Part: Mutation Sweep

A sweep of the extended transcriptions, records and observations (this slice's validation record) compiled 73 mutants of `oracle_c06.c` with the oracle's flags and ran each on the 52 fixtures below: a mutant is refused where the oracle exits non-zero or writes an output that differs from the fixture's. 64 are refused, among them: each selection inverted, read from the wrong galaxy (the wind's and the recheck wind's from the FoF central, the commit's and both recheck burst flags from the host, the yield from the host on either branch, the disruption from the FoF central, the pass's flag from the FoF central) and each flag misrecorded; the `CGMrecipeOn` tests inverted; the three wind tests made non-strict (`M:397`, `M:411`, `M:422`, refused by the `qw_cgm_boundary_*_tie` fixtures, the survivors of c05's sweep); each second test without the cold energy, with the wind energy reduced by the cold ejection and with the cold energy recomputed after it; the CGM energy from the hot gas; the wrong pair zeroed or ejected; each commit credit, cap, metallicity, metal cap and debit on the other pair; each cap made non-strict except below; the cap read before the reheating on each pair; the uncapped metal debit; the dropped `OutflowRate`; each fused credit unfused; the yield into the gas; the recheck's gas test non-strict, its growth or its accretion record dropped and its burst run in mode 0; each observation flipping the wrong galaxy, and the pass's flip not restored. 9 survive, each for its reason: the hot cap made non-strict (`S:1208`; an exact tie of the burst's ejection with the post-reheating hot gas is not constructed here, the same shared commit's tie being c05's `uff_cgm_hot_caps_at_equality`); the recheck wind's three tests made non-strict (`M:397`, `M:411`, `M:422`; their energies come from the recheck's own accretion, so no exact tie is constructed through the composed recheck, and the direct `qw_cgm_boundary_*` ties pin the same SAGE26 tests); the recheck growth's `M:362` and the recheck's `D:79` unfused (the float store absorbs the difference except at a cancellation, which would need the accretion to consume the cold gas, the `M:355` cap, unreachable at R4's `BlackHoleGrowthRate`, or a bulge metal mass cancelling a product formed inside the recheck); the recheck growth's hydrogen cap dropped (end-state invariant: the recheck's burst re-caps and refreshes the hydrogen, c08's observation); `Mcrit` uncapped in the recheck (equivalent: an uncapped `Mcrit` only makes the unstable masses negative instead of zero, and both fail `D:73` and `D:90`); and the recheck burst given `old_disk_radius` for `DiskScaleRadius` (equivalent: nothing writes the disk radius between `D:70` and `D:107`). Every recheck fixture still checks the transcribed recheck against SAGE26's own end state bit for bit.

Port mutants (the validation record), applied to the package in a scratch copy, each fail at least one of the three package oracle tests: the wind selecting by the FoF central's Regime instead of the host's; the recheck's burst given the host as its FoF central; the burst yield's selection inverted; the disruption routed by the FoF central's Regime instead of the passed recipient's; and `CGMrecipeOn` dropped from the disruption's argument, from the burst bundle, from the recheck growth's argument and from the quasar module's merger growth.

### The c06 Part: Fixtures and Regenerating

52 fixtures: 17 of `sage26_quasar_mode` (`qw_cgm_*`, `bh_cgm_*`), 18 of `sage26_starburst_feedback` (`burst_cgm_*`) and 17 of `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption` (`merge_cgm_*`, `dis_cgm_*`, `tr_pass_cgm_*`). Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R4-reachable** (12) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (40). `*_r4_snap32_*` and `*_r4_snap63_*` take real galaxies from the R4 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R4/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `2b606ee7ac82559e32c7c91161f1edb44f89288ec57016e80da884e118ed0807`) at snapshots 32 and 63, every public routine at both: end-of-snapshot values used as plausible call inputs, the rows named in each `.in` (the `quasar_mode_wind` fixtures take their accreted mass from the oracle's own output of the same row's `grow_black_hole` fixture). The real groups hold R4 evidence of the regime/reservoir disagreement: Snap_32's satellite rows 5 and 6 are in Regime 0 under a Regime 1 central; Snap_63's satellite row 652 is in Regime 1 holding only CGM gas, under a Regime 0 central. The inputs were written by a tool (the validation record) from those rows and from c06 and c09 layouts; the constructed ones change only the fields their comments name.

| Routine | Cases |
|---|---|
| `quasar_mode_wind` | the three strict energy boundaries, each at the tie and one double below and above (`qw_cgm_boundary_{cold,cgm,hot}_{below,tie,above}`): the cold test, cold plus CGM (host Regime 0) and cold plus hot (host Regime 1), the unselected pair on each holding a mass whose test would pass, so a misrouted test changes the outcome; the second test passing on the selected pair alone but failing with the ORIGINAL cold binding energy, on each pair (`qw_cgm_original_cold_energy_{cgm,hot}`); real R4 centrals in Regime 0 (Snap_32, the CGM pair ejected) and Regime 1 (Snap_63) |
| `grow_black_hole` | real R4 centrals at Snap_32 (Regime 0) and Snap_63 (Regime 1); the wind ejecting the host's cold gas and its CGM pair (Regime 0) or hot pair (Regime 1) with the other pair forty times larger kept (`bh_cgm_regime{0,1}_wind_ejects_*`); the host is the galaxy itself |
| `collisional_starburst_recipe_fire` | real R4 satellites as a minor merger's host in their real groups, host and central in different regimes, at Snap_32 and Snap_63; the FoF central in Regime 0 and 1 with the Type 1 host in the other, both holding both pairs, on every constructed fixture; the recheck's wind ejecting the host's cold gas and its selected pair by the HOST's Regime, each pair (`burst_cgm_recheck_wind_ejects_*`), the recheck's own burst forming stars into the FoF central's pair, each pair (`burst_cgm_recheck_burst_central_*`), the recheck wind's second test failing only by the original cold energy, each pair (`burst_cgm_recheck_wind_original_cold_energy_*`); the commit's ejection cap binding (`burst_cgm_ejected_cap_binds_*`, with the retained yield's escaping metals to each pair, `FracZleaveDisk` 0.3, labelled NOT R4), the metal cap binding too (`burst_cgm_metals_cap_binds_*`, found by a scan), the cap not binding only because it is read after the reheating (`burst_cgm_cap_after_reheating_*`); the all-metals yield of a major merger to each pair (`burst_cgm_yield_all_metals_*`; the recheck-wind fixtures also take it, their host's cold gas gone); the fused credits (`burst_cgm_rounding_feedback_metals_*`) |
| `deal_with_galaxy_merger_fire` | real R4 satellites merging into their real centrals (major) at Snap_32 and Snap_63; an orphan merging into a Type 1 target whose Regime differs from the FoF central's, the merger's wind ejecting the target's cold gas and its selected pair (`merge_cgm_wind_target_*_central_*`), and, with the template's ratio, the wind ejecting nothing and the recheck finding the target gas-unstable, its burst routing by the FoF central (`merge_cgm_recheck_target_*_central_*`); mixed reservoirs: a Regime 1 satellite merging into a Regime 0 FoF central, both holding both pairs, CGM added to CGM and hot to hot (`merge_cgm_mixed_reservoirs_major`) |
| `disrupt_satellite_to_ICS` | real R4 satellites disrupted into their real centrals at Snap_32 (Regime 1, hot pair) and Snap_63 (Regime 0, CGM pair); an orphan disrupted into its subhalo central, a Type 1 that is not the FoF central, the recipient in Regime 0 and 1 with the FoF central in the other, the FoF central's pairs unchanged (`dis_cgm_recipient_type1_*`) |
| `merger_pass_fire` | real R4 groups at substep 0 (the satellites' `deltaMvir` constructed so the gate passes): Snap_32, a merger into the Regime 1 central and a disruption into it; Snap_63, a disruption into the Regime 0 central; an orphan disrupted into, and merging into, its Type 1 subhalo central, the recipient in Regime 0 and 1 with the FoF central in the other (`tr_pass_cgm_orphan_{disrupt,merge}_recipient_*`), each observed |

Every unselected reservoir is recorded and asserted: the wind host's pair its Regime does not select; the FoF central's unselected pair on every burst and merger; the burst host's unselected pair where it is not the central; both pairs of every uninvolved galaxy; the merger target's unselected pair, which only the addition changes (CGM to CGM, hot to hot); both pairs of a FoF central that is not the disruption's recipient. Not constructed, each for its reason: a `Regime` other than 0 or 1 ("The c10 Group"); the exact ties of the recheck wind's tests and of the burst commit's hot cap, and the cancellations of the recheck's `M:362` and `D:79` (the mutation sweep above); a `grow_black_hole` accretion cap binding (`M:355`, unreachable at R4's `BlackHoleGrowthRate`; c06's and c08's fixtures cover it at R0 and R2); a `check_disk_instability_fire` fixture at `CGMrecipeOn 1` (`sage26_disk_instability` selects no reservoir; its continuation's routing sites are reached through c05's composed wrapper and through the recheck here). The largest new input has 350 fields and the largest output 782, within `oracle_c06`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048). `regenerate.sh` rewrites these outputs with every other group's, which it reproduces byte for byte.

**Package tests.** `test_unit_sage26_quasar_mode_oracle.c` passes the fixture's `CGMrecipeOn` to both helpers with the galaxy's `Regime`, and asserts the wind's selection against the oracle's `wind_reservoir_cgm` AND against the host's input Regime, `wind_ejects_cgm`, `cgm_gas_energy` bit for bit, and the host's unselected pair unchanged. `test_unit_sage26_starburst_feedback_oracle.c` puts the fixture's `CGMrecipeOn` in the bundle and asserts each routing site against the oracle's flag and the expected galaxy's input Regime: the commit and yield by the FoF central, the recheck's wind by the host (its two tests and four energies bit for bit), the recheck burst's commit and yield by the FoF central, with the gas branch matched; the unselected pairs unchanged; the observations counted. `test_unit_sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption_oracle.c` initialises the resolver and both consumers with the fixture's `CGMrecipeOn` (a `CGMrecipeOn 1` disruption fixture initialises them too), passes it to the consumer shadow's growth and the burst replay's bundle, and asserts the merger's wind (the shadow) by the target, its burst and recheck (the replay) by the FoF central and the target, the disruption entry's `reservoir_cgm` by the passed recipient, in a pass the oracle's disruption flag against the recipient's input Regime and each merger event's shadow wind and replay burst against the target's and the central's; the unselected pairs; and counts the observations. `modules/_tests/sage26_oracle_cgm.h` holds the helpers the three share.

## The c11 Group: CGMrecipeOn 1 Cooling, the Clustering-Mass Table and the CGM-On Radio AGN

c11 covers the cooling half of what SAGE26's `CGMrecipeOn 1` switches on: the Press-Schechter clustering-mass table, the hot regime's cold streams, bulk CGM cooling and the radio-mode AGN in both its hot and CGM forms (`C` = `R/src/model_cooling_heating.c`, `H` = `R/src/model_halo_properties.c`). The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R4.par`: `CGMrecipeOn 1` (`:54`), `AGNrecipeOn 2` (Bondi, `:49`), `RadioModeEfficiency 0.08` (`:74`), `Hubble_h 0.73` (`:40`), Millennium cosmology (`Omega` 0.25) and SAGE26's default `MShockMsun` 6.0e11 (`R/src/core_read_parameter_file.c:114`), which `R4.par` does not set. The package implements these routines as shared helpers in `models/sage26/shared/sage_cooling_physics.h`, which the cooling transaction `sage26_regime_aware_cooling` calls (Slice 2, below); the classic three-module path calls the same file's classic pieces with `CGMrecipeOn 0`.

### Routines

Every c11 routine is a **direct call** of a public SAGE26 routine, compiled into `oracle_c11.c`'s own main (`build.sh` builds `output/oracle/oracle_c11`; `regenerate.sh` selects it by the `C11_ROUTINES` list, although the Slice 1 fixtures are owned by two c04 modules); Slice 2's writer conversion is the one transcription-only routine (below). The static `agn_accretion_compute`, `do_AGN_heating_cgm` and `reset_cgm_diagnostics` cannot be called from another translation unit: the first two run inside the public routines below and their locals come from transcriptions; `reset_cgm_diagnostics` is reached only through the regime-aware wrapper, `cooling_recipe_regime_aware`, a c11 routine since Slice 2 (below).

| Owning module | Routine (`routine` line) | SAGE26 call | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sage26_calculate_cooling_budget` | `interpolate_clustering_mass` | `interpolate_clustering_mass(z, &params)` (`H:98-132`) | `return.log_mstar`; the table selection, clamps and index; `zz` and `fz`; the unfused sum and whether it differs |
| `sage26_calculate_cooling_budget` | `cooling_recipe_hot_streams` | `cooling_recipe_hot` (`C:135-315`) with `CGMrecipeOn 1`: the cold streams (`C:202-286`); with `AGNrecipeOn 2` it reaches `do_AGN_heating` (`C:587-627`) and the Bondi branch of the static `agn_accretion_compute` (`C:526-576`), with `AGNrecipeOn 0` (probes) it does not | `return.coolingGas`; the sixteen recorded fields; the stream, cap and AGN flags; every local below |
| `sage26_calculate_cooling_budget` (cooling cases), `sage26_radio_mode_heating` (AGN cases) | `cooling_recipe_cgm` | `cooling_recipe_cgm` (`C:317-391`) with `CGMrecipeOn 1`; with `AGNrecipeOn 2` it reaches the static `do_AGN_heating_cgm` (`C:635-680`), with 0 (a probe) it does not | as above |
| `sage26_radio_mode_heating` | `do_AGN_heating_cgm_on` | `do_AGN_heating` (`C:587-627`) on its own with `CGMrecipeOn 1` and `AGNrecipeOn 2`, a positive `call.coolingGas` and `call.x`, `call.rcool` | `return.coolingGas`; the recorded fields; the AGN flags and locals |

The recorded fields of `galaxy[0]` are `HotGas`, `MetalsHotGas`, `CGMgas`, `MetalsCGMgas`, `ColdGas`, `MetalsColdGas`, `BlackHoleMass`, `r_heat` (the package's `Rheat`), `tcool`, `tff`, `RcoolToRvir`, `CoolingRate`, `mdot_cool`, `mdot_stream` (floats) and `Cooling`, `Heating` (doubles). The inputs set those, `Type`, the stored `SnapNum` and `Mvir`, `Rvir`, `Vvir`; the diagnostics, `Cooling`, `Heating` and the mdot fields are input bit patterns no routine here writes (-11, -22, -33, -44, -55, -66, -77, -88), so the output shows exactly what each path writes. The params are the c04 units (`G`, `RadioModeEfficiency`, `Hubble_h`, `UnitEnergy_in_cgs`, `UnitTime_in_s`, `UnitDensity_in_cgs`), `AGNrecipeOn` (2, or 0 for a probe) and `CGMrecipeOn` (1, required). The hot-recipe fixtures also carry `params.Omega`, `params.MShockMsun`, `params.Snaplistlen`, `call.current_SnapNum` and the redshifts `params.ZZ_stored` (`ZZ[galaxy SnapNum]`) and `params.ZZ_current` (`ZZ[current_SnapNum]`); every other `ZZ` entry stays poisoned (NaN), so a read of the wrong snapshot would surface as NaN. `C:205` reads the stored progenitor snapshot, never the current one (campaign decisions D35 and D37), and the current snapshot is always a different one with a different redshift. The time inputs are as c04's (`call.Age_galaxy_snap`, `call.halo_age`, `call.effective_steps`, recorded as `intermediate.deltaT` and `intermediate.dt`).

### Transcriptions, Check Kinds and Two-Sided Validation

The locals come from **source-reviewed transcriptions** in `oracle_c11.c`, every statement cited to its SAGE26 line: `tr_cooling_recipe_hot` (`C:135-315` at `CGMrecipeOn 1`), `tr_cooling_recipe_cgm` (`C:317-391`), `tr_do_agn_heating` (`C:587-627`), `tr_do_agn_heating_cgm` (`C:635-680`), `tr_agn_accretion_compute` (`C:526-576`, Bondi) and `tr_interpolate_clustering_mass` (`H:98-132`). The cooling function and, inside the hot transcription, the clustering mass are SAGE26's own functions. The oracle refuses a fixture unless:

- SAGE26's call changed no byte of the galaxy outside the recorded fields;
- the full transcription reproduces SAGE26's call in every recorded field and the return value bit for bit, so every recorded local is **tail-checked** (marker 2);
- for `cooling_recipe_hot_streams`, SAGE26's own `do_AGN_heating`, fed the transcribed `x`, `rcool` and post-cap mass, reproduces the call's AGN outputs, and the post-cap mass and both mdot stores equal those of SAGE26's own call with `AGNrecipeOn 0` (`C:297`, `C:314`): `x`, `rcool`, `CoolingGas_postcap` and `z` (passed to SAGE26's `interpolate_clustering_mass`) are **continuation-checked** (marker 1); for `cooling_recipe_cgm` the post-cap mass equals SAGE26's `AGNrecipeOn 0` call likewise (its locals pass to no public routine, so all are tail-checked).

Each recorded local is written with c06's markers (`intermediate.<name>_absent`, the value, `_checked`, `_check_kind`, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`); a local the path does not form is not written. Two-sided validation (campaign decision D25) replaces the local in the transcription by its upward and, separately, its downward neighbouring double and records whether each replacement changes at least one recorded field or the return value.

| Routine | Locals (SAGE26 lines) |
|---|---|
| `cooling_recipe_hot_streams` | `tcool` (`C:142`, the float `Rvir / Vvir` widened), `lambda` (`:157`), `x` (`:163-164`), `rcool` (`:173`), `z` (`:205`), `M_shock` (`:210`), `mass_ratio` (`:211`), `log_mstar`, `Mstar` (`:222-223`), `fMstar` (`:224`), `ratio` (`:225-226`), `exponent` (`:239-241`, after the clamps), `f_stream_criterion` (`:230`/`:242`), `f_stream` (`:263-271`), `cold_stream_cooling` (`:277`), `hot_halo_cooling` (`:279-280`), `CoolingGas_precap` (`:285`), `CoolingGas_postcap` (`:288-292`), and through `do_AGN_heating` `CoolingGas_suppressed` (`:590-595`), `AGNaccreted`, `AGNheating` (`:559-571`) |
| `cooling_recipe_cgm` | `lambda` (`C:329`), `x` (`:332-333`), `rho0` (`:345`), `rho_eff` (`:346`), `tcool` (`:349`), `g_accel` (`:352`), `tff` (`:353`), `rcool` (`:360`), `CoolingGas_precap` (`:364`), `CoolingGas_postcap` (`:366-370`), and through `do_AGN_heating_cgm` `CoolingGas_suppressed`, `AGNaccreted`, `AGNheating` |
| `do_AGN_heating_cgm_on` | `CoolingGas_suppressed`, `AGNaccreted`, `AGNheating` |
| `interpolate_clustering_mass` | `zz` (`H:122-124`), `fz` (`H:130`) |

Branch flags (`intermediate.<flag>`, -1 where the path does not evaluate it): `return_path` (as c04: 0 tail, 1 outer guard), `ratio_nonpositive` (`C:229`), `exponent_capped_high` and `_low` (`C:240-241`), `stream_cutoff` (`C:263`), `f_stream_capped_high` and `f_stream_floored` (`C:270-271`), `reservoir_cap` (0 none, 1 capped, 2 floored; `C:288-292`, `C:366-370`), `agn_entered` (`C:297`, `C:372`), `cgm_entered` (`C:321`), `lambda_positive` (`C:331`), `agn_suppressed` (the suppression formula ran), `agn_saturated` (the `CGMrecipeOn 1` saturation `r_heat >= 0.99 * rcool` zeroed the mass with `r_heat < rcool`; `C:591`, `C:639`), `agn_reservoir_positive` (`C:599`, `C:648`), `eddington_capped` (`C:557`), `accretion_capped` (`C:560`), `heating_capped` (`C:568-571`), `ratchet_raised` (`C:612-616`, `C:658-662`), `rheat_capped` (`C:623-624`, `C:663-664`), `recapped` (`C:675-677`), and for the table `uchuu` (`H:118`), `z_clamped_low`, `z_clamped_high` (`H:123-124`), `iz` and `iz_clamped` (`H:126-128`). Where a cap binds, the value after it equals the field it was capped to, and the package's test asserts it equal to that field.

Most locals are validated on few fixtures, for c05's reason: the recorded fields are floats and a one-ulp change of a double is absorbed by the float stores unless a store is sensitive. Some locals cannot be validated on any input: `exponent` when the high clamp binds (the cutoff then zeroes `f_stream` or the cold stream of about 1e-300 is absorbed in the sum), `f_stream_criterion` under the cutoff, and every local of a path whose result is overwritten (the heating cap rewrites `AGNaccreted`); their markers are informative only. The validation record gives the run's counts.

### Fused Multiply-Adds and the `pow(10.0, x)` Form

Read with `llvm-objdump -d -l` (and `-r` for the calls) from the reference build's objects (`workspace/e01-reference/src/src`, whose machine code equals `build.sh`'s, "Staged Source Equals the Reference Build's Source"). Every fused instruction on these routines' paths:

| Object, function | Offset | Line | Instruction | Expression | Package form |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_halo_properties.o`, `interpolate_clustering_mass` | `0x3ac` | `H:131` | `fmadd d0, d1, d2, d0` after `fmul d0, d3, d0` (`0x3a8`) | `table[iz] * (1 - fz) + table[iz + 1] * fz`: the second product rounded, the first fused | explicit `fma(table[iz], 1.0 - fz, table[iz + 1] * fz)` |
| `model_cooling_heating.o`, `cooling_recipe_hot` | `0x6c8` | `C:302` | `fmadd` | `Cooling += 0.5 * coolingGas * Vvir * Vvir` | one expression (`mimic_sage_cooling_energy`, unchanged from the classic apply module) |
| same, `do_AGN_heating` | `0x858` | `C:609` | `fmsub` | `MetalsHotGas -= metallicity * AGNaccreted` | one expression (unchanged) |
| same | `0x8b8` | `C:619` | `fmadd` | `Heating += 0.5 * AGNheating * Vvir * Vvir` | one expression (unchanged) |
| same, `cooling_recipe_cgm` (inlined `do_AGN_heating_cgm`) | `0xbf8` | `C:656` | `fmsub` | `MetalsCGMgas -= metallicity * AGNaccreted` | one expression |
| same | `0xc80` | `C:667` | `fmadd` | `Heating += 0.5 * AGNheating * Vvir * Vvir` | one expression |
| same, `cooling_recipe_cgm` | `0xcb4` | `C:377` | `fmadd` | `Cooling += 0.5 * coolingGas * Vvir * Vvir` | one expression (`mimic_sage_cooling_energy`) |
| same, `cool_gas_onto_galaxy` | `0xfb0`, `0xfd8` | `C:697`, `C:699` | `fmadd`, `fmsub` | the classic transfer's metal credit and debit | one expression (unchanged) |
| same, `cooling_recipe_regime_aware` | `0x168`, `0x190`, `0x200`, `0x228` | `C:481`, `:483`, `:493`, `:495` | `fmadd`, `fmsub` | the wrapper's metal credits and debits | one compound assignment each in `sage26_regime_aware_cooling.c` (Slice 2, below) |

Nothing else fuses in these routines: the stream arithmetic (`C:205-285`) and the CGM times and mass (`C:322-370`) have no fused instruction; the CGM recipe's `Rvir * Rvir` is a single-precision `fmul` (`0xa80`) widened before the division, its `2.0 * Rvir` an `fadd` of the widened float with itself (`0xa8c`), and the constant integrals `I_M` and `I_cool / I_M`, `4.0 * M_PI` and `1.5 * 0.59` are folded at compile time (`0xa00`-`0xa64`). The table's `iz = (int)(zz / 0.5)` compiles to `fadd d1, d0, d0` and `fcvtzs` (`0x360`), equal to the source division for every `zz` in [0, 15]. SAGE26's object also computes both `pow(10.0, x)` of the streams (`C:222`, `C:242`) as calls of `__exp10` (the `ARM64_RELOC_BRANCH26` relocations attributed to those lines), and macOS's `__exp10` need not equal its `pow` in the last bit, so the package writes `__exp10` there on Apple platforms (`mimic_sage_exp10`) and `pow` elsewhere; the two `pow` calls with a variable base (`C:225`, `:226`; relocations at `0x524` and `0x53c`) stay `pow`.

`oracle_c11.o` fuses exactly the transcribed sites (since Slice 2: `C:302` at `oracle_c11.c` line 939, `C:377` at 1026, `C:609` at 753, `C:619` at 764, `C:656` at 792, `C:667` at 807, and `H:131` at 1300, inlined twice; Slice 2 adds the wrapper's four metal sites, below) and no others. In the package, the classic modules' objects (Mimic's `-O2`, arm64) fuse exactly what they fused at the starting commit: `sage26_radio_mode_heating.o` the hot metal debit and the Heating accumulation, `sage26_apply_cooling.o` the Cooling accumulation and the transfer's metal credit and debit, `sage26_calculate_cooling_budget.o` nothing. `cm_fused_differs_mill` and `cm_fused_differs_uchuu` are inputs where SAGE26's fused interpolation differs from the unfused sum (`intermediate.fused_differs_from_unfused` 1), so a port that rounds both products fails them.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

73 fixtures: 52 owned by `sage26_calculate_cooling_budget` (20 `cm_*` table fixtures, 21 `hs_*` hot-recipe fixtures, 11 `cgm_*` CGM-cooling fixtures) and 21 by `sage26_radio_mode_heating` (11 `agn_cgmon_*` standalone `do_AGN_heating` fixtures and 10 `cgm_agn_*` fixtures of the CGM AGN reached through `cooling_recipe_cgm`), alongside each module's c04 fixtures. Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R4-reachable** (11) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (62). The `*_r4_snap32_*` and `*_r4_snap63_*` fixtures take real galaxies from the R4 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R4/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `2b606ee7ac82559e32c7c91161f1edb44f89288ec57016e80da884e118ed0807`): end-of-snapshot values used as plausible call inputs, the stored `SnapNum` the output snapshot minus one and the current snapshot the output snapshot, with their `ZZ` from the file's `snapshot_redshifts`; the `cm_r4_*` fixtures take those stored redshifts. The `agn_cgmon_r4_*` fixtures take `x`, `rcool` and the post-cap mass from the oracle's own output of the `hs_r4_*` fixture of the same galaxy, and the constructed `agn_cgmon_*` fixtures take `x` from `hs_sigmoid_no_cutoff`'s output; each says so. Exact equalities (`fMstar == M_shock`, `mass_ratio == 1`, the Uchuu window's edges, the fused-differs redshifts) were found by a scratch search linked against SAGE26's own `model_halo_properties.o`, recorded in the validation record.

| Routine | Cases |
|---|---|
| `interpolate_clustering_mass` | both tables at their endpoints, a grid point, an interior point, the floor and the last interval; `z` below 0 and above 15; `Omega` one double inside and outside the 0.01 window at both ends (`cm_omega_*`); the fused-differs inputs; the R4 stored redshifts of snapshots 31 and 62 |
| `cooling_recipe_hot_streams` | the R4 hot-regime centrals of Snap_32 and Snap_63 (the Snap_32 and large Snap_63 centrals are real cases of the 0.99 saturation); `AGNrecipeOn 0`; the cutoff at `fMstar == M_shock` and one step below, at `mass_ratio` exactly 1 and above; the sigmoid without cutoff; `ratio` underflowing to 0; both exponent clamps; stored and current redshifts that would select different streams; the outer guard for `HotGas` 0 and `Vvir` 0; a subnormal `HotGas`; zero metals; the reservoir cap; the Eddington and accretion caps; the heating cap binding and not |
| `cooling_recipe_cgm` (budget) | the R4 CGM-regime centrals of Snap_32 and Snap_63; `AGNrecipeOn 0`; `CGMgas` 0 and `Vvir` 0 (not entered, stale diagnostics); a subnormal `CGMgas`; zero metals; a NaN cooling function that fails `lambda > 0` (`CGMgas` and its metals +inf, so the float metallicity is inf / inf); the cap; `Mvir` 0 and `Rvir` 0 (no guard the source lacks) |
| `cooling_recipe_cgm` (radio) | `r_heat` the largest float below and the smallest at or above `0.99 * rcool` (`rcool` = `Rvir`); `r_heat` above `rcool` (zeroed, then capped inside the block); the re-cap to the post-accretion CGM; the ratchet reaching `Rvir` exactly (the strict cap does not bind); the Eddington cap alone and with the heating cap; the accretion cap; two small R4 CGM centrals without a black hole |
| `do_AGN_heating_cgm_on` | `r_heat` either side of `0.99 * rcool`; `r_heat` above `rcool`; `HotGas` 0 with `r_heat` above `Rvir` (capped outside the block); the ratchet beyond `Rvir`, capped; the Eddington cap alone and with the heating cap; the accretion cap alone; the heating cap not binding; the R4 centrals |

Not constructed, each for its reason: the hot recipe's `lambda <= 0` and `rho_rcool <= 0` returns at `CGMrecipeOn 1` (shared with `CGMrecipeOn 0` and covered by c04's `hot_*` fixtures; a NaN cooling function does not take `lambda <= 0`, and SAGE26 then aborts at its `XASSERT`, `C:308`); `f_stream` above 1 or below 0 after the sigmoid (`1 / (1 + 10^e)` lies in [0, 1] for every clamped `e`, so `C:270-271` never bind); the CGM cooling floor (`coolingGas < 0`, `C:368`), which needs a negative `dt` (the oracle's `dt` is SAGE26's positive `deltaT / effective_steps`; the package's behavioural test covers the floor with a negative `dt`); the CGM ratchet exceeding `Rvir` (with `rcool = Rvir` and the heating capped to the cooling mass, `(AGNheating / coolingGas) * rcool <= Rvir`); and `AGNrecipeOn` 1 and 3, which reach the shared accretion only through the classic modules' unchanged code and keep their c04 coverage. The largest c11 input has 38 fields and the largest output 183, within `oracle_c11`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (512) and the reader's limit (2048). `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c11 outputs with every other group's, which it reproduces byte for byte.

### Mutation Sweep

A mutation sweep of the transcriptions (the validation record) compiled 41 mutants of `oracle_c11.c` with `build.sh`'s flags and ran each on every c11 fixture: wrong constants (`35.9`, `0.59` in each recipe, the factor 3, `2/3`, the divisor `0.15`, `alpha`, `r0`'s `0.1`, the Bondi `0.375`, the 0.99 saturation and the Uchuu constant), float/double boundaries (the float `Rvir / Vvir`, the float metallicity, `HotGas / Rvir` and `Rvir * Rvir` widened), the current instead of the stored snapshot, `Hubble_h` dropped or inverted, the mdot stores swapped, the hot conversion in the CGM diagnostic, `tff` dropped, each cap and clamp removed or loosened, wrong reservoirs in the CGM accretion, and the interpolation unfused. Each mutant runs on every c11 fixture; it is killed when the oracle refuses a fixture (a transcription, continuation or post-cap check fails) or, where the oracle accepts every fixture, when any output it writes differs byte for byte from the committed one (a recorded local, flag or marker). 34 are refused by the oracle. Five more are killed by the record alone, their recorded state equal to SAGE26's but a recorded local or flag different: `ratio <= 0` made `< 0` (at `ratio` 0 the clamped sigmoid also gives exactly 1, but `ratio_nonpositive` and the exponent differ), the low exponent clamp moved to -200 (`1 + 10^e` rounds to 1 for every `e` below about -17, but the recorded exponent differs), the high clamp moved to 200 (the cutoff zeroes `f_stream`, but the exponent differs), and the table's high `z` clamp and `iz` clamp loosened (the last two entries of both tables are the same literal 1.0000, but `zz`, `iz` and `fz` differ). Two survive, equivalent in every recorded output: the reservoir cap made `>=` (it assigns the same value at equality) and the ratchet test made `<=` (at `r_heat == rcool` the suppression has zeroed the mass, so the ratchet's `coolingGas > 0` fails). The package's behavioural tests assert the clamps through the helpers' recorded locals.

**Package tests.** The oracle tests of `sage26_calculate_cooling_budget` and `sage26_radio_mode_heating` run their c11 fixtures through the shared helpers directly (`modules/_tests/sage26_oracle_cooling.h`): the table helper; the hot budget with `CGMrecipeOn 1`, the hot AGN and the tail step by step, and `mimic_sage_cooling_recipe_hot` on a fresh copy, which must agree (the helpers write no `mdot_cool` or `mdot_stream`, campaign decisions D38 and D39; the test places their returned float stores where SAGE26 writes them, `C:282-283`, so the oracle's galaxy fields are compared with those returned values); the CGM budget, the CGM AGN and the tail, and `mimic_sage_cooling_recipe_cgm` likewise; the hot AGN on its own. They assert every recorded field, the return value, every flag the helpers report and every recorded local bit for bit (the flags of caps inside the accretion and of the `f_stream` clamps, which the helpers do not report, are consumed unasserted), assert each binding cap's value equal to its field, and print the run's tallies.

### Slice 2: The Regime-Aware Wrapper and the Written mdot Rates

c11 Slice 2 adds the package's cooling transaction, `sage26_regime_aware_cooling` (campaign decision D38), and the written mdot rates (campaign decision D39), with two routines in `oracle_c11.c`:

| Owning module | Routine (`routine` line) | SAGE26 code | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sage26_regime_aware_cooling` | `cooling_recipe_regime_aware` | a **direct call** of `cooling_recipe_regime_aware` (`C:420-506`) with `CGMrecipeOn 1`; it reaches `cooling_recipe_hot`, `cooling_recipe_cgm`, their AGN calls and the static `reset_cgm_diagnostics` (`C:413-418`) | `return.total_cooling`; the sixteen recorded fields; the wrapper's flags and locals; the nested calls' flags and locals under `intermediate.hot.` and `intermediate.cgm.` |
| `sage26_finalize_cooling_heating` | `writer_mdot_conversion` | a **transcription** of the writer's two statements (`R/src/io/save_gals_hdf5.c:996-997`), compiled in the oracle main with SAGE26's types and flags: `save_gals_hdf5.c` is not in the link (it needs SAGE26's output buffers) | `output.mdot_cool`, `output.mdot_stream` (float); the same conversion regrouped as one constant factor and whether it differs |

The wrapper fixtures read the c11 hot-recipe fixtures' inputs (the c04 units, `AGNrecipeOn` 2 or 0, `CGMrecipeOn` 1, the cosmology `params.Omega`, `params.MShockMsun`, `params.Snaplistlen`, the stored and current snapshots and their `ZZ`, the time inputs and the galaxy block) and `galaxy[0].Regime`. `Regime` is not written by the wrapper; the oracle's check that SAGE26's call changed no byte outside the recorded fields confirms it. The writer fixtures read `params.UnitMass_in_g`, `params.UnitTime_in_s` and the raw float `galaxy[0].mdot_cool` and `mdot_stream`.

**The wrapper transcription** (`tr_cooling_recipe_regime_aware`, every statement cited to its line): the locals `cgm_cooling`, `hot_cooling`, the float `hot_tcool` and `hot_diagnostics_valid` (`C:422-425`), the mdot reset (`:433-434`), the regime branch (`:436-470`) with `tr_reset_cgm_diagnostics` (`:413-418`), the CGM then the hot application with SAGE26's own `get_metallicity` (`:475-496`), the total and `CoolingRate` (`:498-502`). The nested recipes are the Slice 1 transcriptions, factored into bodies that run on the wrapper's galaxy in place (`tr_cooling_recipe_hot_on`, `tr_cooling_recipe_cgm_on`; the refactor changed no arithmetic, and every Slice 1 output is reproduced byte for byte). The oracle refuses a wrapper fixture unless:

- SAGE26's call changed no byte of the galaxy outside the recorded fields;
- the full transcription reproduces SAGE26's call in every recorded field and the return value bit for bit, so every recorded local is **tail-checked** (marker 2);
- **sub-call identity**: SAGE26's own `cooling_recipe_hot`, called on the input with the mdot fields the wrapper reset, reproduces the transcription's post-hot galaxy and `hot_cooling`; and SAGE26's own `cooling_recipe_cgm`, called on the galaxy the transcription handed the CGM call, reproduces its post-CGM galaxy and `cgm_cooling`. These fix the nested states between the calls, which the end state alone could not: the CGM call reads the black-hole mass and `r_heat` the hot AGN left.

No wrapper local is continuation-checked: the application and the total pass to no public SAGE26 routine. Wrapper flags (`intermediate.<flag>`): `cgm_regime` (`Regime == 0`, `C:436`), `hot_called` (`:451-452`), `hot_diagnostics_valid` (`:453-456`), `cgm_called` (`:440-441`, `:460-461`), `reset_called` (`:442-443`, `:462-463`), `restored` (`:466-469`), `cgm_apply` and `hot_apply` (-1 not reached, 0 uncapped, 1 capped; `:475-478`, `:487-490`); `intermediate.hot_tcool` is the saved float. Wrapper locals: `hot_cooling_returned`, `cgm_cooling_returned`, `cgm_cooling_applied`, `cgm_metallicity`, `hot_cooling_applied`, `hot_metallicity`, `total_cooling`; the nested calls' locals and flags are those of the table above, prefixed `hot.` or `cgm.`.

**The writer transcription** (`run_writer_mdot_conversion`) writes `float = raw * UnitMass_in_g / UnitTime_in_s * SEC_PER_YEAR / SOLAR_MASS` with SAGE26's `macros.h` constants. Its form is read from `save_gals_hdf5.o` of the reference build (`workspace/e01-reference/src/src/io/save_gals_hdf5.o`, `llvm-objdump -d -l`), lines `:996` and `:997` at `0x2d64`-`0x2da8`: `fcvt d0, s0` (the float widened), `fmul d0, d20, d0` (`UnitMass_in_g`, loaded from the params at `0x1948`), `fdiv d0, d0, d19` (`UnitTime_in_s`, `0x1950`), `fmul d0, d0, d9` (`d9` = `0x417e16a300000000`, 3.155e7), `fdiv d0, d0, d10` (`d10` = `0x46d8842d67978cdb`, 1.989e33), `fcvt s0, d0`: left to right, no fused instruction, no constant folded. `oracle_c11.o` compiles the transcription to the same sequence (`fcvt`, `fmul`, `fdiv`, `fmul`, `fdiv`, `fcvt`), and `sage26_finalize_cooling_heating.o` (Mimic's `-O2`) to the same operations in the same order, vectorised over the two rates (`fcvtl`, `fmul.2d`, `fdiv.2d`, `fmul.2d`, `fdiv.2d`, `fcvtn`), with no fused instruction.

**Fused instructions** of Slice 2's sites:

| Object | Offset | Line | Instruction | Expression | Package form |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `model_cooling_heating.o`, `cooling_recipe_regime_aware` | `0x168`, `0x190` | `C:481`, `:483` | `fmadd`, `fmsub` | `MetalsColdGas += metallicity * cgm_cooling`, `MetalsCGMgas -= metallicity * cgm_cooling` | one compound assignment each (`sage26_regime_aware_cooling.o`: `fmadd`, `fmsub`) |
| same | `0x200`, `0x228` | `C:493`, `:495` | `fmadd`, `fmsub` | `MetalsColdGas += metallicity * hot_cooling`, `MetalsHotGas -= metallicity * hot_cooling` | one compound assignment each (`fmadd`, `fmsub`) |
| `save_gals_hdf5.o` | `0x2d64`-`0x2da8` | `W:996-997` | none | the mdot conversion | one expression, left to right (above) |

Nothing else in the wrapper fuses: the gas credits and debits (`C:480`, `:482`, `:492`, `:494`) are `fadd`/`fsub` of the widened float, and the total (`:498`) an `fadd`. `oracle_c11.o` fuses exactly the four transcribed metal sites (`oracle_c11.c` lines 1224, 1226, 1238 and 1240) besides the Slice 1 sites; `sage26_regime_aware_cooling.o` fuses exactly its four metal sites plus, inlined from `shared/sage_cooling_physics.h`, the Slice 1 sites (the clustering `fma`, the two AGN metal debits, the two `Heating` and the `Cooling` accumulations).

**Fixtures.** 26: 20 wrapper fixtures (`rac_*`, owned by `sage26_regime_aware_cooling`) and 6 writer fixtures (`mdot_writer_*`, owned by `sage26_finalize_cooling_heating`). Six wrapper fixtures are **R4-reachable**: the Slice 1 R4 galaxies (`cgm_r4_snap{32,63}_central`, Regime 0; `hs_r4_snap{32,63}_central`, Regime 1, the Snap_32 one with CGM gas as well) and two hot-regime centrals holding both hot and CGM gas (Snap_32 row 21308, GalaxyIndex 4000016000000019; Snap_63 row 33059, GalaxyIndex 7000134000000000), each with its stored and current `ZZ` from the R4 file header. The rest are constructed routine-domain probes; each `.in` says what it exercises. The inputs were written by `output/c11-s2-scratch/make_fixtures.py` (the slice's validation record); every output by the oracle.

| Routine | Cases |
|---|---|
| `cooling_recipe_regime_aware` | Regime 0 with CGM gas (R4) and without (the reset); Regime 1 with hot gas only (R4, and with AGN off), CGM gas only (hot diagnostics invalid, the CGM ones kept) and both (R4, and constructed: the hot `tcool` restored over the CGM value); the hot AGN's ratchet capped to `Rvir` saturating the CGM call (the CGM AGN reads the hot AGN's `r_heat`); stale diagnostics surviving a failed CGM guard (`Vvir` 0 in each regime, a NaN cooling function); hot diagnostics invalid with no CGM gas (the reset in the hot branch); both masses equal to their whole reservoirs with metals not proportional (the equality behaviour, the CGM metals above the gas); the hot application cap binding to the post-AGN reservoir; the order of the two applications deciding a float rounding; the 0 < f_stream < 1 streams; an unclassified `Regime` -1 |
| `writer_mdot_conversion` | the run units with R4-sized rates; never-assigned (zero) rates; inputs where the regrouped constant factor (`mdot_writer_grouping_differs`) and the division taken first (`mdot_writer_order_differs`) round to a different float; subnormal and the largest float rates |

Not constructed, each for its reason: the CGM application cap (`C:476-478`) binding, which no input reaches: the CGM recipe re-caps its returned mass to the post-accretion CGM gas (`C:675-677`) whenever its AGN runs, nothing changes `CGMgas` between the call and the application, and without AGN the recipe's own cap (`C:366-370`) bounds it; `hot_diagnostics_valid` false with `Vvir > 0` after a hot call, which needs a float `tcool <= 0`, i.e. `Rvir <= 0`, where SAGE26's hot recipe forms NaN and aborts at its `XASSERT` (`C:308`); the hot recipe's `lambda <= 0` and `rho_rcool <= 0` returns inside the wrapper (Slice 1's reason); and `dt <= 0` at the `CoolingRate` tail, which SAGE26 never passes (the package's initial boundary, where it does not run, is tested on every platform). The largest new output has 345 fields (`rac_regime_unclassified`), within `oracle_c11`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (512) and the reader's limit (2048).

**Counts** (the package test's tally): 20 wrapper fixtures, 480 locals compared, all tail-checked, 126 validated two-sided, 19 binding caps asserted equal to their field, 495 flags. As in Slice 1, most locals are validated on few fixtures because the recorded fields are floats; `total_cooling` is validated on every fixture (it reaches `CoolingRate` directly). The saved `hot_tcool` is a float store recorded as `f32`, not a double local, and is not tested two-sided: it equals the recorded `tcool` whenever it is restored.

**Mutation sweep** (the validation record): 21 mutants of the wrapper and writer transcriptions, compiled with `build.sh`'s flags and run on the 26 fixtures. 17 are refused by the oracle (the mdot reset removed, the regime test inverted, the reset removed, made unconditional or run on a failed CGM guard, the restoration or the `tff -1` removed, the wrong metallicity or reservoir in the CGM credit, the hot application cap removed or taken from the pre-AGN reservoir, the equality branch, the hot debit unfused, the total without the hot mass, `CoolingRate` not overwritten, the CGM call before the hot call, the hot application before the CGM one). Three are killed by the record alone: the CGM application cap removed (the cap is unreachable, as above, so the state is unchanged, but the recorded `cgm_apply` flag differs), and the writer regrouped or with the division first (killed by `mdot_writer_grouping_differs` and `mdot_writer_order_differs`, whose recorded outputs differ; the writer is a transcription, so no SAGE26 call refuses it). One survives, equivalent on every constructible input: "valid" taken as `tcool >= 0` (it differs only where `tcool` is 0 with `Vvir > 0`, not constructible, above).

**Package tests.** `sage26_regime_aware_cooling`'s oracle test runs the wrapper fixtures through `sage26_regime_aware_cooling_run` and asserts the return, every recorded field (SAGE26's mdot members are the package's raw `mdot_cool_raw` and `mdot_stream_raw`, D39), every wrapper and nested flag and local, and each binding cap, and prints the tallies. `sage26_finalize_cooling_heating`'s oracle test runs the writer fixtures through `process()` on one live galaxy, with a positive interval and again without one, and asserts the written floats on both paths, and that the package never computes the regrouped value where it differs.

## The c12 Group: The Halo Concentration

c12 covers what SAGE26's `ConcentrationOn 3` switches on: the NFW concentration inverted from `Vmax/Vvir` (`H` = `R/src/model_halo_properties.c`), which progenitor joining stores for a galaxy that was a Type 0 or 1 and becomes its FoF central (`R/src/core_build_model.c:290-295`). The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R5.par`: `ConcentrationOn 3` (`:57`), every other line as `R4.par`; R0-R4 run with `ConcentrationOn 0` (`R4.par:57`). The package's helpers are `models/sage26/shared/sage_halo_concentration.h`, which `sage26_set_halo_concentration` calls; this group's fixtures belong to that module. The two public routines are called directly ("Routines" to "Mutation Sweep" below); the joining statement itself is made by c06's joining transcription, and its fixtures and the package's composition are under "Joining" below.

### Routines

Both are **direct calls** of public SAGE26 routines (`H:288-319` and `H:327-362`, declared at `R/src/model_halo_properties.h:25-27`; `model_halo_properties` is one of `build.sh`'s twelve objects), compiled into `oracle_c12.c`'s own main (`build.sh` builds `output/oracle/oracle_c12`; `regenerate.sh` selects it by the `C12_ROUTINES` list). A fixture holds up to 24 cases (`call.ncase`), each one call.

| Routine (`routine` line) | SAGE26 call | Inputs | Output per case `k` |
|---|---|---|---|
| `concentration_from_vmax_vvir` | `concentration_from_vmax_vvir(Vmax, Vvir)` | `case[k].Vmax`, `case[k].Vvir` (`f64`) | `return.case[k]` (`f64`), `output.case[k].Concentration` (`f32`, the joining's `(float)` store, `R/src/core_build_model.c:292-294`), the inversion's flags and locals |
| `get_halo_concentration` | `get_halo_concentration(k, call.z, galaxies, &params)`, the case's galaxy at index `k` as joining passes `ngal` | `params.ConcentrationOn` (0 or 3; any other is refused), `call.z` (passed as SAGE26 passes `ZZ[SnapNum]`; read by neither mode), `galaxy[k].Type` (its Type at the call, before joining) and the float `Vmax`, `Vvir`, `infallVmax`, `infallVvir` | `return.case[k]`; `output.case[k].Concentration` only when `intermediate.store_made` is 1 (`ConcentrationOn > 0`, the joining's gate, `R/src/core_build_model.c:291`); `selected_infall` (-1 mode 0, 0 the current pair, 1 the infall pair, `H:342`); the selected pair and the inversion's flags and locals |

Modes 1 and 2 (`H:334-337`, `:352-362`) are reached on no rung and have no fixtures. Every other struct member stays poisoned (NaN), so mode 3 reading `Mvir` or the redshift would surface; SAGE26's call must leave the galaxy array unchanged byte for byte, or the oracle refuses the fixture.

### Transcriptions, Check Kinds and Two-Sided Validation

The locals cannot be read from SAGE26's call, so they come from **source-reviewed transcriptions** in `oracle_c12.c`, each statement cited to its line: `tr_concentration_from_vmax_vvir` (`H:294`, `:296`, then `tr_from_ratio_sq`: `:301`, `:303`, `:306`, the loop `:308-317` and `tr_return`, `:318`) and `tr_get_halo_concentration` (`H:330-332`, `:339-349`). The oracle refuses a fixture unless, for every case, the full transcription reproduces SAGE26's return bit for bit and each recorded local reproduces it from where it is formed: `ratio_sq` through the transcribed bisection and the final `c_lo` and `c_hi` through the transcribed return statement (**tail-checked**, marker 2), and the selected `vmax` and `vvir` (each float widened, `H:343-347`) through SAGE26's own `concentration_from_vmax_vvir` (**continuation-checked**, marker 1).

Each local is written with c06's markers (`intermediate.case[k].<name>_absent`, the value, `_checked`, `_check_kind`, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`) and three more: `_store_changes_up` and `_store_changes_down` (whether the neighbouring double, in the local's composition, changes the float store) and `_narrowing_limited` (validated two-sided, but neither neighbour changes the float store: only the double return, which the joining narrows at once, shows it). Two-sided validation (campaign decision D25) replaces the local by `nextafter(v, +inf)` and, separately, `nextafter(v, -inf)` and records whether each changes the double return or the float store. Flags (`intermediate.case[k].<flag>`): `guard_returned` (`H:294`), `below_one` (`:306`, -1 when not evaluated), `iterations` (the steps taken: 60, or 0 after an early return), `c_lo_at_initial_bound` and `c_hi_at_initial_bound` (the bracket end never moved from 1.0 or 200.0, `:303`: the bound binds, and the package asserts the value equal to it; -1 without a bracket), and two observations of the transcription that are not SAGE26 state, `ties` (steps whose `f_mid` equalled `ratio_sq`, converged repeats included) and `first_tie_step` (-1 for none).

Few locals are validated with a float store changing: a one-ulp change of `ratio_sq` or of the selected pair moves the bisection's root by a few ulps at most, which the 60 steps usually absorb (the return is the bracket's midpoint), and a few-ulp change of the double return rarely crosses a float rounding boundary. `c_lo` and `c_hi` reach the return directly, so where they are validated they are mostly narrowing-limited. The validation record gives the counts.

### Fused Multiply-Adds

None on these paths. Read with `llvm-objdump -d -l` from the reference build's `model_halo_properties.o` (`workspace/e01-reference/src/src`, whose machine code equals `build.sh`'s, "Staged Source Equals the Reference Build's Source"): `concentration_from_vmax_vvir` (`0x504`-`0x5d0`) compiles the guard to two `fcmp ..., #0.0` with `b.ls` (Vmax first, then Vvir: the order is unobservable, the guard is pure; NaN passes), `ratio_sq` to one `fdiv` and one `fmul` of the quotient by itself (`:296`, `0x538`-`0x53c`), the ratio test to `fcmp` with `b.mi` (`:306`), and each step to `fadd` and `fmul` by 0.5 (`:309`), `fadd`, the `log` call, `fdiv`, `fsub` (`:310`), `fmul` by `A` (`0x3fcbacc4ef88b978`, 0.21621) and `fdiv` (`:311`), and `fcmp` with two `fcsel ... mi` (`:312-316`), counting 60 down (`:308`); the return is `fadd` and `fmul` by 0.5 (`:318`). `get_halo_concentration` (`0x5d4`-`0x91c`) inlines the same sequence on its mode-2 and mode-3 paths, the mode-3 selection being a `csel` of the field offsets on `Type == 0` (`:342-347`) and each float widened by its own `fcvt` (`0x6f0`, `0x6f4`). The object's only fused instructions in `get_halo_concentration` are the three `fmadd` at `0x8e8`, `0x8f0` and `0x8f8`: the inlined Ishiyama et al. (2021) interpolation (`H:277-279`) on the mode-1 path, which no rung reaches and the package does not port. No expression of the two routines has a multiply feeding an add or subtract, so the package writes each statement as one C expression and no site uses an explicit `fma()`. `oracle_c12.o` and `sage26_set_halo_concentration.o` (Mimic's `-O2`, arm64) contain no fused instruction.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

12 fixtures of the two routines, owned by `sage26_set_halo_concentration` (with the 15 joining fixtures below, 27 in all): eight `inv_*` (`concentration_from_vmax_vvir`, 48 cases) and four `ghc_*` (`get_halo_concentration`, 30 cases). Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R5-reachable** (4) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (8). The inputs were written by a scratch generator recorded in the slice's validation record; every output by the oracle. The `*_r5_snap32_rows` and `*_r5_snap63_rows` fixtures take real galaxies from the R5 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R5/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `b350d5d406066b9cecf94603d5aa9597e1dcf1074015bf4edb9131bd5139d7c3`), matched by `GalaxyIndex` to the previous snapshot: Type 0 rows that were Type 0 and whose halo grew (so SAGE26's joining replaced `Vvir` with the new halo's, which is the `Vvir` the writer outputs, `R/src/core_build_model.c:267-270`, `R/src/io/save_gals_hdf5.c:902-903`), written `Concentration` 0, exactly 1.0, the smallest above 1, the median and the largest; and, in `ghc_*`, Type 0 rows that were Type 1, with the infall pair written at the previous snapshot. For every one of these rows the oracle's float store equals the reference's written `Concentration` bit for bit (the validation record shows both bit patterns).

| Routine | Cases |
|---|---|
| `concentration_from_vmax_vvir` | `Vvir` +0, -0 and negative, `Vmax` +0, -0 and negative, each guard on its own, and both negative; NaN `Vmax` and `Vvir` (they pass the guard, every `f_mid < NaN` is false, so the return is 1.0), +inf `Vmax` (saturates) and +inf `Vvir` (`ratio_sq` 0); `ratio_sq` 0.990025, the largest double below 1, exactly 1 (twice) and the smallest above 1 (`c_lo` binds at 1.0, `c_hi` ends at the double above 1, the return rounds to exactly 1.0); either side of the jump (`inv_jump`: with `Vvir` 1, the last `Vmax` whose `ratio_sq`, `f(2.5546875)` minus one ulp, returns 1.0 and the next, which returns exactly 2.5546875); the `<` tie at a visited midpoint (`inv_tie_visited_midpoint`: `ratio_sq` equal to `f` at the midpoints 7.21875, 13.4375, 50.75 and 193.78125, found by a scratch search, `first_tie_step` 4, 3, 1, 4); ratios just below, at and beyond `f(200)` (`c_hi` binds at 200 and the return is exactly 200), ratio 10, `1e300` and an overflow to +inf; typical ratios 1.05 to 3.1; the real rows |
| `get_halo_concentration` | `ConcentrationOn` 3 (`ghc_mode3_selection`): Type 0 with the current pair selected and with the current pair below ratio 1, Type 1 with the infall pair selected and below ratio 1, a Type 1 whose infall was never recorded (-1, the creation value), Type 0 with `Vvir` 0, a Type 2 routine-domain probe (any Type but 0 takes the infall pair) and Type 0 at ratio exactly 1, every row's two pairs disagreeing; `ConcentrationOn` 0 (`ghc_mode0_zero`) on the same rows: 0 and no store; the real rows |

Not constructed, each for its reason: the `<` tie at the jump itself (`ratio_sq` exactly `f(2.5546875)`): no double `q` has `fl(q * q)` equal to it (with `Vvir` 1 the neighbouring `Vmax` give `f` minus and plus one ulp), and every `ratio_sq` is such a square of a double quotient; a Type 3 galaxy at the call (joining makes a Type 3 only for a `mergeType` not 0 and `continue`s before the call, `R/src/core_build_model.c:241-244`). The largest c12 input has 43 fields and the largest output 391, within `oracle_c12`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048). `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c12 outputs with every other group's, which it reproduces byte for byte.

### Mutation Sweep

A mutation sweep of the transcriptions (the slice's validation record) compiled 22 mutants of `oracle_c12.c` with `build.sh`'s flags and ran each on every c12 fixture: `A` 0.2162, 59 and 61 steps, `<=` in the bisection, `c_hi` 100, `c_lo` 2, the ratio test removed and made `<=`, each velocity guard made `<`, `ratio_sq` regrouped as `(Vmax * Vmax) / (Vvir * Vvir)`, `log1p` for `log(1 + c)`, `f_mid` reassociated, the return as `c_lo` or `c_hi`, the branches swapped, the dispatch's pairs swapped, selected for Type 1 only and with `Vvir` crossed, the pair rounded through a float ratio, and mode 0 returning 1. 19 are refused by the oracle. Two are killed by the record alone: 61 steps (no fixture's return changes, but the recorded bracket and step count do) and the `Vmax` guard made `<` (a `Vmax` of 0 then forms `ratio_sq` 0 and returns 0 through the ratio test; `guard_returned` and `below_one` differ). One survives: the midpoint written `c_lo + 0.5 * (c_hi - c_lo)`, which gives the same bracket and return on every fixture and on 2,000,000 random ratios (both forms are exact on the bisection's dyadic brackets there); it is not one of SAGE26's statements.

**Package test (the two routines).** `sage26_set_halo_concentration`'s oracle test runs each `inv_*` case through `mimic_sage_concentration_from_vmax_vvir` and each `ghc_*` case through `mimic_sage_get_halo_concentration` with the fixture's `ConcentrationOn` and the case's Type and four floats, and asserts the return, the float store, every flag, the step count, the selected pair and every recorded local bit for bit, each binding bracket end equal to its bound, and the markers' consistency; `ties` and `first_tie_step` are consumed unasserted (not SAGE26 or package state). It prints the tallies the validation record quotes.

### Joining

SAGE26 makes the store inside progenitor joining's FoF-central branch (`B` = `R/src/core_build_model.c`): after `MergTime` (`B:288`), `if (ConcentrationOn > 0) Concentration = (float)get_halo_concentration(ngal, ZZ[halos[halonr].SnapNum], galaxies, run_params)` (`B:290-295`), then the disk and bulge radii (`B:297-298`) and `Type = 0` (`B:300`). The call therefore sees the galaxy's Type before joining: 0 (the current pair, after `B:261-271` updated `Vmax` and, if the halo grew, `Vvir`) or 1 (the infall-frozen pair). `oracle_c06.c`'s `transcribe_join_progenitors` makes the store at that source position, calling SAGE26's own `get_halo_concentration`; mode 3 does not read the redshift it passes, which `read_halo_catalogue` sets for every catalogue snapshot. `read_join_flags` reads `ConcentrationOn` 0 or 3 and refuses any other value, naming `params.ConcentrationOn`. With 3 every galaxy of a `join_progenitors` or `join_and_pass` fixture reads and records its float `Concentration` (`BLOCK_CONCENTRATION`, after every other block) and each progenitor's record gains `concentration_assigned` (central only, with c06's `_absent` convention); with 0 nothing new is read or recorded, so the 22 c06 joining fixtures (18 owned by `sage26_initialise_merger_clock`, 4 `join_and_pass` owned by `sage26_resolve_mergers_and_disruption`) are unchanged byte for byte. `join_and_pass` inherits the store; no `ConcentrationOn 3` `join_and_pass` fixture exists, since the merger pass neither reads nor writes `Concentration`.

**Intermediates.** The store's value is SAGE26's own return, recorded as the galaxy's `Concentration`; the package carries no value of the statement between modules, so the statement has no carried intermediate, and none is continuation-checked, tail-checked or validated two-sided. The inversion's locals are c12's direct fixtures' (above). `concentration_assigned` is a driver local of the transcription, like c06's other joining record fields.

**Fused instructions.** None. Read with `objdump -d -l` from the reference build's `core_build_model.o` (`workspace/e01-reference/src/src`), the joining block (`B:234-344`) has no fused instruction, as c06 recorded; the statement compiles to `ldr w8, [x8, #0x1878]`, `cmp w8, #0x1` and `b.lt` (the gate, `B:291`, `0x3e8`-`0x3f8`), the load of `ZZ[SnapNum]` into `d0` (`B:294`, `0x408`), `bl` to `_get_halo_concentration` (`0x418`, an `ARM64_RELOC_BRANCH26` relocation), `fcvt s0, d0` (`0x420`) and `str s0` (`B:292`, `0x424`). The store is a plain narrowing of the returned double. `oracle_c06.o` keeps its 18 fused instructions, the same sequence as before the statement was added.

**Fixtures.** 15 `tr_join_*` fixtures, owned by `sage26_set_halo_concentration`, with `routine join_progenitors` (run by `oracle_c06` through `C06_ROUTINES`), at R5's flags (`ConcentrationOn 3`, `BulgeSizeOn 3` with `Hubble_h`, `R5.par:57`, `:109`, `:40`). Their inputs were written by a scratch generator from c06's joining fixtures (recorded in the slice's validation record); every output by the oracle. 13 are R5-reachable and 2 are constructed routine-domain probes.

| Path (SAGE26 lines) | Fixtures |
|---|---|
| Type 0 -> 0, the current pair, halo grown (`B:267` taken) and not | `tr_join_central_halo_grows`, `tr_join_central_halo_not_grown`, and the central of `tr_join_type0_to_2_kept`, `tr_join_type1_to_2_kept`, `tr_join_central_second_slot` and both probes |
| Type 0 -> 0 with `(Vmax/Vvir)^2 < 1`: an earlier non-zero value overwritten by 0 | `tr_join_central_ratio_below_one` |
| Type 1 -> 0, the infall pair, chosen to differ from the current pair's result | `tr_join_type1_to_0_infall_pair` |
| Type 0 -> 1 (`B:301-321`): kept, infall recorded | `tr_join_type0_to_1_kept` |
| Type 1 -> 1: kept | `tr_join_type1_to_1_kept` |
| Type 0 -> 2 and Type 1 -> 2 orphans (`B:322-338`): kept | `tr_join_type0_to_2_kept`, `tr_join_type1_to_2_kept` |
| the call's index and the store's slot are `ngal`, not 0 | `tr_join_central_second_slot` (the central in slot 1, after an orphan) |
| Type 2 before joining (`B:240` false): passed over | `tr_join_probe_type2_passed_over` (no Type 2 row is written at any output snapshot of the R5 reference) |
| already merged (`B:242-245`): dropped | `tr_join_probe_already_merged_dropped` (`B:242` is unreachable, "The c06 Group") |
| real R5 galaxies at snapshots 32 and 63 | `tr_join_r5_snap32_central` and `tr_join_r5_snap63_central` (a central, Type 0 -> 0, and its satellite as an orphan, Type 1 -> 2), `tr_join_r5_snap32_satellite_promotion` and `tr_join_r5_snap63_satellite_promotion` (the satellite as its FoF's new central, Type 1 -> 0) |

The real-galaxy fixtures take the R5 reference's rows (Snap_32 rows 146 and 147, Snap_63 rows 385 and 386) with the halo's `Mvir` equal to the galaxy's, so `Vvir` is the written one. For each, the oracle's store equals the reference's written `Concentration` bit for bit: the centrals' from their current pair, the promoted satellites' from their infall pair (each satellite's written value is its last central value, which the infall pair reproduces). Each assigned galaxy's input `Concentration` is its own written value at the previous output snapshot (Snap_27 for snapshot 32, Snap_37 for 63), which differs from the stored value, so the store is visible; each orphan's input is its written value, which it keeps. Not constructed: a galaxy created this snapshot (`B:347-351`, which the oracle refuses; its zero is the creation value, `R/src/model_misc.c:93`, covered by the package's platform-independent test), and a `ConcentrationOn 3` `join_and_pass` fixture (the pass does not touch `Concentration`). The largest joining input has 269 fields and the largest output 257, within `oracle_c06`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048).

**Mutation sweep.** Ten mutants of the statement in `oracle_c06.c`, each compiled with `build.sh`'s flags and run on all 37 joining fixtures (the slice's validation record): the statement moved after `Type = 0`, before `B:261-271` and before `B:284`; the gate removed, never taken, and taken only for a previous Type 0; the call's index and the store's slot made 0; the store truncated through an `int`; the redshift replaced by 0. Eight are killed. Two survive, each equivalent on every fixture by construction: the gate removed (at `ConcentrationOn 0` SAGE26's `get_halo_concentration` returns 0 and the oracle records no `Concentration`, which keeps every c06 output unchanged; at 3 the gate is taken), and the redshift replaced (mode 3 does not read it).

**Package test.** `sage26_set_halo_concentration`'s oracle test runs each `tr_join_*` fixture through the joining harness (`modules/_tests/sage26_oracle_join.h`): the core's `inherit_descendant_halos`, then the shipped `pre_timestep` order, `sage26_initialise_merger_clock`, `sage26_set_halo_concentration` and `sage26_set_disk_scale_radius`, initialised with the fixture's flags. It asserts every recorded member of each retained galaxy bit for bit, apart from c06's named exclusions on rows off SAGE26's main-branch reset and its bookkeeping (`mergeIntoID`, `dT`), and gives each path (current pair, infall pair, kept) a wiring assertion of its own, with the oracle's `concentration_assigned` marking exactly the assigned rows. On a subhalo join the harness's stand-in FoF central (row 0, not a fixture galaxy) is neither recorded nor compared. The two other harness users initialise the module with their fixtures' `ConcentrationOn` 0.

## The c13 Group: The Feedback-Free-Burst Classifier

c13 covers what SAGE26's `EnhancedStarFormationOn 1` switches on. Its first part is the classifier: `determine_and_store_ffb_regime` (`G` = `R/src/model_regimes.c`, `:188-290`) at mode 1, which SAGE26's driver calls once per FoF group after `determine_and_store_regime` and `infall_recipe` and before the substeps (`R/src/core_build_model.c:407-427`), with its two public helpers `calculate_ffb_threshold_mass` (`G:298-313`) and `calculate_ffb_fraction` (`G:321-341`) and its static SplitMix64 draw `halo_uniform` (`G:151-159`). The rung flags are those of the campaign's `par/R6.par`: `EnhancedStarFormationOn 1` (`R6.par:58`; every other line as `R5.par` but `OutputDir`), `Hubble_h 0.73` (`:40`). The package's helpers are `models/sage26/shared/sage_ffb_regime.h`, which `sage26_determine_ffb_regime` calls; this group's fixtures belong to that module.

### Routines

All three are **direct calls** of public SAGE26 routines (declared at `R/src/model_regimes.h:21-26`; `model_regimes` is one of `build.sh`'s twelve objects), compiled into `oracle_c13.c`'s own main (`build.sh` builds `output/oracle/oracle_c13`; `regenerate.sh` selects it by the `C13_ROUTINES` list). `halo_uniform` is static and is reached only through the classifier.

| Routine (`routine` line) | SAGE26 call | Inputs | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| `calculate_ffb_threshold_mass` | `calculate_ffb_threshold_mass(case[k].z, &params)` per case | `params.Hubble_h`, `call.ncase` (up to 32), `case[k].z` (`f64`) | `return.case[k]`; the locals below |
| `calculate_ffb_fraction` | `calculate_ffb_fraction(case[k].Mvir, case[k].z, &params)` per case | `params.EnhancedStarFormationOn` (1; any other value is refused: 0 is the caller's gate, 2 is not ported), `params.Hubble_h`, `case[k].Mvir` and `case[k].z` (`f64`) | `return.case[k]`; the locals below |
| `determine_and_store_ffb_regime` | `determine_and_store_ffb_regime(call.ngal, call.Zcurr, call.snapnum, galaxies, &params)` | `params.EnhancedStarFormationOn` (1), `params.Hubble_h`, `call.Zcurr`, `call.snapnum`, per galaxy `Type`, `mergeType`, the float `Mvir`, `MostBoundID` (`i64`), the input `FFBRegime` and SAGE26's CGM `Regime`; the `rand()` queue; `call.has_reference` and, with 1, `reference.galaxy[i].FFBRegime` | per galaxy `FFBRegime`, `skipped`, `discarded_draw`; per classified row the draw's 64 bits (`draw_bits`, `i64`), the widened `Mvir` and the locals below; `intermediate.draws_discarded`, `intermediate.rand_queue_independent`, `intermediate.RAND_MAX` |

The routine reads `mergeType`, `MostBoundID` and `Mvir` of each galaxy and writes `FFBRegime`; the fixtures also carry `Type` (the package skips a row by `Type` 3), the input `FFBRegime` (a skipped row keeps it, a classified row's is overwritten) and SAGE26's CGM `Regime`, which the routine does not read (the classification has no regime restriction, `G:215-217`). Every other struct member stays poisoned. The oracle refuses a classifier fixture unless SAGE26's call changes no byte of any galaxy other than its `FFBRegime` (mode 1 never writes `g_max`). With `call.has_reference` 1 the oracle refuses the fixture unless SAGE26's `FFBRegime` equals each row's `reference.galaxy[i].FFBRegime`, the value the R6 reference wrote for that row.

### The Test-Only `rand()` Provider

SAGE26 calls `rand()` once per classified row and discards the value (`G:224`), keeping its other draws in place; its useful draw is the hash. `oracle_c13.c` defines `int rand(void)` as `oracle_c10.c` does ("The c10 Group"): in `output/oracle/oracle_c13`, and only there, SAGE26's `rand()` returns the fixture's queue (`rand.queue_length`, `rand.queue[k]`) in order, refuses a call outside a SAGE26 call or past the queue's end, and the oracle refuses a fixture whose queue SAGE26 does not consume exactly, so `intermediate.draws_discarded` is exact: one per row not skipped. The oracle then reruns SAGE26's call with every queue value `q` replaced by `RAND_MAX - q` and refuses the fixture unless every `FFBRegime` is the same (`rand_queue_independent` 1), and, as for c10, calls the routine on each row alone with the queue rewound to observe which rows discard a draw (`discarded_draw`). `cls_rows_queue_a` and `cls_rows_queue_b` hold the same rows under two different queues; their outputs are identical below the header line.

### Transcriptions, Check Kinds and Two-Sided Validation

The locals cannot be read from SAGE26's calls, so they come from **source-reviewed transcriptions** in `oracle_c13.c`, each statement cited to its line: `tr_threshold` (`G:308-312`, with the tails `tr_threshold_from_z_norm` and `tr_threshold_from_log`), `tr_fraction` (`G:326-339`, with the tails `tr_fraction_from_threshold` and `tr_fraction_from_x`), `tr_halo_uniform_bits` and `tr_uniform_from_bits` (`G:153-158`) and `tr_determine_and_store_ffb_regime` (`G:212-240`, mode 1; the discarded `rand()` is not transcribed, its value reaches nothing). The oracle refuses a fixture unless the transcription reproduces SAGE26's return, or every row's `FFBRegime` and skip, bit for bit, and in the classifier its fraction and threshold equal SAGE26's public `calculate_ffb_fraction` and `calculate_ffb_threshold_mass` of the row. Each recorded local reproduces the recorded output from where it is formed: `z_norm`, `log_Mvir_ffb_code`, the threshold `Mvir_ffb`, `x` and the fraction `f_ffb` through the transcribed remainder (**tail-checked**, marker 2), and in the classifier the draw through SAGE26's own public fraction, `(draw < calculate_ffb_fraction(Mvir, Zcurr))` giving SAGE26's `FFBRegime` (**continuation-checked**, marker 1). The recorded output is the return in the threshold and fraction fixtures and the row's `FFBRegime` in the classifier fixtures.

Each local is written with c06's markers (`_absent`, the value, `_checked`, `_check_kind`, `_validated_up`, `_validated_down`, `_validated`). Two-sided validation (campaign decision D25) replaces the local by `nextafter(v, +inf)` and, separately, `nextafter(v, -inf)` in its composition and records whether each changes the recorded output. In the classifier the output is the integer `FFBRegime`, which a one-ulp change of a local changes only where the draw and the fraction lie within an ulp of each other, which no `MostBoundID` can be chosen to give; the classifier's locals are recorded with their directions and none is validated. The threshold and fraction fixtures validate them: the package's oracle test prints the tallies (the validation record). There is no cap or clamp in these routines, so no local binds, and no float store follows them, so none is narrowing-limited.

`x` is SAGE26's local, but SAGE26's object never forms it (below): the tail from a recorded `x` is `1 / (1 + exp(-x))`, which negates a NaN `x` where the object divides a NaN by `-0.15`, so for a NaN `x` (a NaN `Mvir` or threshold) the tail reproduces the NaN but not its sign; the transcription's own fraction is written in the object's form and reproduces SAGE26's NaN bit for bit.

Two observations per threshold evaluation, which are not SAGE26 state: `pow_equals_exp10`, whether libm's `pow(10.0, v)` (called through a volatile pointer, so clang cannot simplify it) equals `__exp10(v)` bit for bit at the recorded `log_Mvir_ffb_code`, and `unfused_threshold_differs`, whether the log term rounded as a separate product and sum gives another threshold.

### Fused Multiply-Adds and Call Forms

Read with `objdump -d -l -r` from the reference build's `model_regimes.o` (`workspace/e01-reference/src/src`, whose machine code equals `build.sh`'s, "Staged Source Equals the Reference Build's Source"; the disassemblies of both, identical below their headers, are in the slice's validation record).

| Function | Offset | Line | Instruction | Expression | Package form |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `determine_and_store_ffb_regime` (`0xf0`-`0x7f8`) | `0x47c` | `G:310` (inlined threshold) | `fmadd d0, d9, d1, d0`: `log10(z_norm)` (`d9`, hoisted out of the loop at `0x1ec`-`0x1f8`) times -6.2 plus `log10(h) + 0.8` (`fadd` at `0x478`) | `0.8 + log10(h) + FFB_THRESHOLD_SLOPE * log10(z_norm)` | explicit `fma(-6.2, log10(z_norm), 0.8 + log10(h))` |
| `calculate_ffb_fraction` (`0x7fc`-`0x8b0`) | `0x860` | `G:310` (inlined threshold) | `fmadd d0, d0, d1, d11` | the same | the same `fma()` (the fraction calls the threshold helper) |
| `calculate_ffb_threshold_mass` (`0x8b4`-`0x918`) | `0x908` | `G:310` | `fmadd d0, d0, d1, d9` | the same | the same `fma()` |
| `determine_and_store_ffb_regime`, mode-2 branch | `0x548`-`0x6ec` | `G:129`-`:139` | 19 `fmadd`, one `fnmadd` and four `fmla.2d`: `inverse_normal_cdf` (`G:108-141`) inlined into the BK25 branch, entered at `0x458` only for `EnhancedStarFormationOn == 2` | the Boylan-Kolchin (2025) concentration scatter | **unreachable** on every rung (mode 2 is not ported, campaign decision D6) |

Calls: `pow(10.0, log_Mvir_ffb_code)` (`G:312`) is a call of `___exp10` at all three sites (relocations at `0x480`, `0x864` and the tail branch at `0x914`), clang's simplification of `pow` with base 10. The logarithms are `_log10` calls (`0x1f8`, `0x470`, `0x4a0`; `0x830`, `0x84c`, `0x87c`; `0x8d8`, `0x8f4`) and the sigmoid's exponential an `_exp` call (`0x4ac`, `0x890`). The fraction's `exp(-x)` (`G:339`) is compiled as `exp(log10(Mvir / Mvir_ffb) / -0.15)`: `fdiv` by the constant `0xbfc3333333333333` (-0.15; `0x4a8`, from the stack slot set at `0x3e4`-`0x3ec`, and `0x88c`), so `x` is never formed. That equals the source form for every non-NaN `x` (round-to-nearest division is sign-symmetric), but a negation flips a NaN's sign bit and the division carries it: a source-form `exp(-x)` compiled without the fold (Mimic's `-O0` unit-test build) returns a NaN of the other sign (the executed check in the slice's validation record), so the package writes the object's form. In the classifier the float `Mvir` is compared with 0 in single precision (`fcmp s10, #0.0`, `0x488`) and widened (`fcvt d1, s10`, `0x498`) for the division, which equals the source's widened double test; the threshold guard is `fcmp d0, #0.0` with `b.ls` (`0x490`), so a NaN passes both. The hash is `eor` with `x23` (`(uint64_t)(uint32_t)snapnum * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15`, hoisted: the `mov w8, w1` zero-extension and `mul` at `0x1e0`-`0x1e4`), `add` (`0x428`), the two `eor`/`mul` steps (`0x42c`-`0x438`), the final xorshift merged with `>> 11` (`lsr #42`, `eor ... lsr #11`, `0x43c`-`0x440`), `ucvtf`, `fadd` of 0.5 and `fmul` by 2^-53 (`0x444`-`0x44c`): no fused instruction. The comparison is `fcmp d8, d1` with `b.pl` (`0x4b8`-`0x4bc`): 0 for greater, equal or unordered.

**The `pow` / `__exp10` check.** The package writes `pow(10.0, ...)`, which Mimic's `-O2` build on Apple clang compiles to `___exp10` (as SAGE26's object) and its `-O0` unit-test build calls as `pow`. The two give the same double at every threshold argument the run and the fixtures reach: the oracle records `pow_equals_exp10` 1 at all 163 threshold evaluations of the c13 fixtures (every mini-Millennium snapshot redshift at `Hubble_h` 0.73, `thr_snapshots_00_31` and `thr_snapshots_32_63`, and every fixture redshift), and the package's oracle test asserts each; a standalone check of the package helper compiled at `-O0` against `__exp10` over the 64 snapshot redshifts and every fixture redshift (191 arguments) finds no difference (the slice's validation record). **The equality is established only for those arguments** (mini-Millennium's snapshot redshifts at `Hubble_h` 0.73 and the c13 fixtures' redshifts) **and does not hold for every simulation package**: the same check over micro-Uchuu's 50 snapshot redshifts at its `Hubble_h` 0.6774 finds one difference, at snapshot 3 (`z` 10.458691417440129), where the argument `0x1.0e8c8da54b3b8p-2` gives `pow` `0x1.d6610c1d85ba4p+0` and `__exp10` `0x1.d6610c1d85ba3p+0`, one ulp apart.

**Contraction.** `oracle_c13.o` fuses exactly the transcribed log term, in its four inlined copies (`fmadd` at `0xd98`, `0x1160`, `0x1d44` and `0x2b00`), and no other site: `unfused_threshold_differs` forms its product in a statement of its own. The package's `sage26_determine_ffb_regime.o` (Mimic's `-O2`, arm64) has exactly one `fmadd` (the explicit `fma()`) and calls `___exp10`. A contraction experiment rebuilt `model_regimes.c` with `-ffp-contract=off` (no fused instruction) and linked it with `oracle_c13.o`: the fused transcription is then refused by `thr_snapshots_00_31`, `thr_snapshots_32_63`, `thr_constructed_redshifts` and `frac_sigmoid_r6_redshifts` (the unfused log term changes the threshold at 24 of the 163 evaluations, `unfused_threshold_differs`), so the fused form is required and the fixtures detect its absence.

### Fixtures and Regenerating

14 fixtures, owned by `sage26_determine_ffb_regime`: four `thr_*` (`calculate_ffb_threshold_mass`, 80 cases), two `frac_*` (`calculate_ffb_fraction`, 39 cases) and eight `cls_*` (`determine_and_store_ffb_regime`, 48 rows). Each `.in` states what it exercises and, on its `Reachability:` line, whether it is **R6-reachable** (5) or a **constructed routine-domain probe** (9). The inputs were written by a scratch generator recorded in the slice's validation record; every output by the oracle. Redshifts are SAGE26's `ZZ = 1 / a - 1` of `simulations/mini-millennium/mini-millennium.a_list` (`R/src/core_init.c:66`; Mimic's `src/core/init.c:60` forms the same expression), equal bit for bit to the R6 reference's `Header/snapshot_redshifts`. The `cls_r6_snap12_rows` and `cls_r6_snap16_rows` fixtures take real rows of the R6 reference output (`/Volumes/Internal/results/sage26-campaign/workspace/e01-reference/out/R6/model_0.hdf5`, SHA-256 `819ba947eda92211a3ec078bd7133c5e9cfe58359b012d7db454ed231bbd5df3`): the written `Type`, float `Mvir`, `Regime` and `SimulationHaloIndex` (SAGE26 writes `llabs` of the halo's `MostBoundID`, `R/src/io/save_gals_hdf5.c:878`; none is negative) as `MostBoundID`, at `ZZ` of the snapshot, each row entering the call with `mergeType` 0 (rows merge in the substeps, after the classification) and its input `FFBRegime` seeded to the opposite of its written value; for every one of these rows SAGE26's `FFBRegime` equals the written value (the oracle checks it, `call.has_reference` 1).

| Routine | Cases |
|---|---|
| `calculate_ffb_threshold_mass` | every mini-Millennium snapshot redshift (`thr_snapshots_00_31`, `thr_snapshots_32_63`); `z` 9 (`z_norm` exactly 1), -0 and 0; 1e54 and 1e300 (the threshold underflows to 0); 8e51 (a subnormal threshold); -1 (`z_norm` 0: the threshold +inf); -1 + 2^-53 (the redshift above -1 nearest to it: the largest finite threshold at `Hubble_h` 0.73, about 6.05e105, so at that `h` only `z = -1` overflows); -2 (a NaN threshold); NaN; +inf (`z_norm` +inf: the threshold 0); -inf; `Hubble_h` 1e302 at `z` 0 and -0.5 (a finite exponent overflows to +inf) and at 9 and 1 (finite) |
| `calculate_ffb_fraction` | the guards each on its own: `Mvir` +0, -0, -1 and -inf; a threshold of 0 (`z` 1e54); `Mvir` 0 against a NaN threshold (the `Mvir` guard alone); then `Mvir` NaN and a NaN threshold (each passes the guards: NaN), `Mvir` +inf (exactly 1), an infinite threshold (`z` -1: exactly 0 through `x = -inf`, not the guard), `Mvir` equal to the threshold at `ZZ[12]` and `ZZ[16]` (exactly 0.5), the smallest float and double subnormals; widened float masses 0.45 dex below to 0.45 dex above the threshold at the redshifts of snapshots 8, 12, 16, 20 and 27 |
