| `determine_and_store_ffb_regime` | `cls_rows_queue_a` and `_b`: Type 0 rows of both outcomes with their input `FFBRegime` overwritten, a Type 1 satellite, a Type 2 orphan with `Mvir` 0, `mergeType` 1 and 4 rows skipped with their input kept, CGM `Regime` 0 and 1 rows with the same `Mvir` and `MostBoundID` (the same result), a negative `MostBoundID` (-1) and one above 2^53 (2^53 + 1, which a double would round), under two `rand()` queues; `cls_mvir_guards` (+0, -0, negative, NaN, +inf and a subnormal `Mvir`); `cls_threshold_zero` (`Zcurr` 1e54: every row 0, an infinite `Mvir` included) and `cls_threshold_infinite` (`Zcurr` -1); `cls_negative_snapshot_probe` (`snapnum` -1: SAGE26's `(uint32_t)` conversion hashes it as 4294967295); the real rows of snapshots 12 and 16 (`FFBRegime` 1 and 0 among them, the Type 1 FFB row of snapshot 16, `MostBoundID` 9391591, included) |

Every branch of the three routines reachable at `EnhancedStarFormationOn 1` is covered: the skip (`G:213`), the discarded draw (`:224`), the mode-1 dispatch (`:227`), both fraction guards on their own and their pass-through (`:332`), the sigmoid (`:337-339`) for finite, NaN and infinite `x`, and both outcomes of the comparison (`:236-240`). Not constructed, each for its reason: the comparison's tie `draw == f_ffb` (no `MostBoundID` can be chosen whose hash lands exactly on a fraction; the package's behavioural test asserts it through its deviate seam, as c10 does); a draw of exactly 1.0 (it needs the hash's top 53 bits all ones, a preimage of SplitMix64); `EnhancedStarFormationOn 0` (`G:194-200` and `:326-328`: SAGE26's driver never calls the classifier then, `R/src/core_build_model.c:425`, and in the package it is the module's gate, covered by its behavioural test); mode 2 (`G:202-209`, `:241-288`: not ported, D6); a `Type` 3 row with `mergeType` 0 (SAGE26 sets `Type` 3 only together with a nonzero `mergeType`). The largest c13 input has 79 fields and the largest output 800, within `oracle_c13`'s `MAX_FIELDS` (1024) and the reader's limit (2048). `regenerate.sh` rewrites the c13 outputs with every other group's, which it reproduces byte for byte.

### Mutation Sweep

A mutation sweep of the transcriptions (the slice's validation record) compiled 30 mutants of `oracle_c13.c` with `build.sh`'s flags and ran each on every c13 fixture: the threshold's divisor 10, its 0.8 (in the full transcription and in the tail), the slope, the log term unfused, `pow` replaced by `exp(v ln 10)` (full and tail), each fraction guard loosened or removed, the width 0.15, `x` multiplied by the width, the object form's sign flipped and written as the source `exp(-x)`, the tail's sign, every hash constant and shift, `>> 11`, the `+ 0.5`, the `(uint32_t)` conversion, the ID carried through a double, the skip test, the comparison `<=`, a perturbed `Mvir` and a shifted `Zcurr`. 26 are refused by the oracle and 3 are killed by the record alone (the final xorshift `>> 30`, the `+ 0.5` removed and the perturbed `Mvir`: SAGE26's `FFBRegime` is reproduced but a recorded draw or local differs). One survives, equivalent on every fixture: the transcription's comparison written `<=`, which differs from `<` only at the tie no fixture can construct (above).

**Package test.** `sage26_determine_ffb_regime`'s oracle test runs each `thr_*` case through `mimic_sage_ffb_threshold_mass`, each `frac_*` case through `mimic_sage_ffb_fraction`, and each `cls_*` fixture's rows through the module's `process()` with `EnhancedStarFormationOn` 1, `ctx->redshift` the fixture's `Zcurr`, `ctx->snapshot_number` its `snapnum`, and halos whose `UniqueGalaxyID` and stored `SnapNum` stay poisoned (so a read of either would surface); its seam only observes, so every deviate is the production draw. It asserts the return or `FFBRegime`, every recorded local and the draw's bits bit for bit, the guard and comparison flags, `pow_equals_exp10` 1 at every threshold evaluation, the discarded-draw count against the rows the package classified, the queue independence and each real row's written value, and the markers' consistency; `unfused_threshold_differs` and `RAND_MAX` are consumed unasserted (observations, not SAGE26 or package state).
